**17.4.4** the Expert shall be required to give notice to each of the said Parties inviting each of them to submit to him within 10 (ten) Working Days of his appointment written submissions and supporting material and shall afford to each of the said parties an opportunity to make counter submissions within a further 10 (ten) Working Days in respect of any such submission and material and the Expert's decision shall be given in writing within 21 (twenty-one) Working Days from receipt of any counter submissions or in the event that there are no counter submissions within 21 (twenty-one) Working Days of receipt of the written submissions and supporting material;

**17.4.5** the Expert shall be at liberty to call for such written evidence from the Parties and to seek such legal or other expert assistance as may reasonably be required;

**17.4.6** the Expert may take oral representations from a Party but will not do so without allowing both parties the opportunity to be present;

**17.4.7** the Expert shall have regard to all representations and evidence when making the decision which shall be in writing with reasons;

**17.4.8** the Expert's decision, once binding, will be enforceable by the English Courts as a contractual obligation and not as an arbitral award; and

**17.4.9** notwithstanding any referral of a dispute to an Expert, the Parties will perform and continue to perform their obligations under this Agreement before, during and after such referral in accordance with the terms of this Agreement.

**17.5** This clause does not apply to any dispute which may arise in relation to any matter which is expressly to be agreed or approved or determined by any Party in its absolute discretion pursuant to this Agreement.

## 18. DEEMED APPROVAL

**18.1** Where it is provided in this Agreement that a matter is to be agreed by any of the Parties or approved by SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) then unless otherwise agreed in writing in any particular case the following provisions of this Clause 18 shall apply PROVIDED ALWAYS that there shall be no deemed approval for Reserved Matters Applications.

**18.2** References in this Clause 18 to "Document" shall be construed as referring to any documents, details, entity or organisation or other matters which have been submitted to any of the Parties including either SCDC or the County Council for approval and submission shall be organised by recorded delivery post to the relevant Party including SCDC and/or the County Council as appropriate.

**18.3** If following submission of a Document to any of the Parties including SCDC or the County Council or the Owners the Party receiving submission of a Document does not respond in writing within 40 Working Days of deemed receipt of the Document pursuant to clauses 13.1 and 13.4 then it shall be deemed that no revisions are required to such Document and the Document shall be deemed to have been approved by the other Parties.

**18.4** If SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) notifies the Owners of any of the following which it reasonably requires:

**18.4.1** any amendments or comments to a Document; or

**18.4.2** proposes a change to the identity of an entity or organisation which is proposed for approval

and the Owners agree that the amendments are reasonable then the Owners shall incorporate:

**18.4.3** the amendments into the Document; or

**18.4.4** change the identity of the entity or organisation to that proposed by SCDC or the County Council (as applicable)

and the Document as amended shall subject to Clause 18.5 below be deemed to have been approved by SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) without further action or approval.

**18.5** The Owners shall confirm to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) in writing that the amendments have been incorporated and agreed and provide SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) with such number of copies of the Document as SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) shall reasonably require save that no further information or copies of Documents shall be required to be sent in addition to the written confirmation to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) in relation to approval of an entity or organisation.

**18.6** If the Owners do not agree to any or to only some of the amendments notified to it by SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) pursuant to Clause 18.4 then the Owners shall notify SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) of this and provide written reasons why the amendments or comments have not been agreed and if the Owners and SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) cannot within 40 Working Days thereafter agree the terms of the relevant Document then the provisions of Clause 17 (Dispute Resolution) of this Agreement shall apply and

**18.6.1** if the Expert's decision is that the amendments are reasonable and should be incorporated or the identity of entity or organisation should be changed to that proposed by SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) then the Document shall be deemed to be approved with those amendments being incorporated; or

**18.6.2** if the Expert's decision is that the amendments are unreasonable and should not be incorporated then the Document shall be deemed to be approved without those amendments being incorporated and in the case of the entity or organisation it shall be the entity or organisation as proposed by the Owners that shall be deemed to be approved; or

**18.6.3** if the Expert's decision is that the document be rejected or that the entity or organisation should not be deemed to be approved then there shall be no Deemed Approval for the purposes of this Clause.

**18.7** Where SCDC and/or the County Council (as appropriate) within 40 Working Days of deemed receipt of the Document pursuant to Clause 18.4 notify the Owners in writing that they reject the Document and provide written reasons for the rejection and if the Owners and SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) cannot within 40 Working Days thereafter agree the terms of the relevant Document then the provisions of Clause 17 of this Agreement shall apply and

**18.7.1** if the Expert's decision is that the amendments are reasonable and should be incorporated or the identity of entity or organisation should be changed to that proposed by SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) then the Document shall be deemed to be approved with those amendments being incorporated; or

**18.7.2** if the Expert's decision is that the amendments are unreasonable and should not be incorporated then the Document shall be deemed to be approved without those amendments being incorporated and in the case of the entity or organisation it shall be the entity or organisation as proposed by the Owners that shall be deemed to be approved; or

**18.7.3** if the Expert's decision is that the document be rejected or that the entity or organisation can be rejected then there shall be no Deemed Approval for the purposes of this clause.

**18.8** The provisions of Clause 18.4 to 18.7 shall apply equally where SCDC and/or the County Council (as appropriate) submit Documents under this Agreement to any of the other Parties for Agreement or approval and for the avoidance of doubt for the purposes of this Clause 18 only all references in clauses Clause 18.4 to 18.7 to "Owners" shall be replaced by "SCDC or County Council (as applicable)" and references to "SCDC or the County Council (as applicable)" shall be replaced by "Owners".

## 19. ENFORCEMENT PROTOCOL

**19.1** Save in the case where emergency enforcement action is being required before taking action to enforce any of the terms of this Agreement and subject to Clause 2.8 SCDC and/or the County Council shall give written notice to the Owners stating the nature of the breach and the steps required to remedy the breach and shall stipulate a timescale for remedying the breach.

**19.2** Subject to Clause 2.8 and Clause 19.1 above SCDC and/or the County Council shall give the Owners the opportunity to discuss the breach with SCDC and/or the County Council as appropriate and then remedy it.

**19.3** Subject to Clause 2.8 and Clause 19.1 above if the Owners do not remedy the breach within the stipulated timescale or such longer time period as may be reasonably proposed by the Owners in writing and approved by SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable), SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall be able to pursue legal enforcement remedies.

**19.4** Nothing within this Clause 19 shall prevent SCDC and/or the County Council exercising their statutory rights or taking legal proceedings to enforce the Owners' obligations set out in this Agreement.

**19.5** The Councils shall be entitled to rely on the enforcement provisions contained within Section 106(5) to Section 106(8) of the 1990 Act but if any provision is held to be a contractual provision outside of the 1990 Act, the Councils shall be entitled to use usual contractual enforcement methods including claim for damages where there is a financial payment due, injunction where obligations have a negative trigger and specific performance in respect of any positive obligation to deliver on-site infrastructure or facilities or put in place relevant security.

**19.6** The Owners shall permit the Councils and their authorised employees and agents upon reasonable written notice to enter the Site at all reasonable times for the purpose of verifying whether or not any obligation arising hereunder has been performed or observed.

**19.7** Without prejudice to any other right, remedy or power herein contained or otherwise available to the Councils if there is a breach of a requirement in a Planning Obligation to carry out any operations in, on, under or over the Site the Councils may subject to Clause 19.2 and Clause 19.3:

**19.7.1** enter the Site and carry out the operations; and

**19.7.2** recover from the Owners any expenses reasonably and properly incurred by the Councils in doing so as a debt due and owing.

## 20. MATERIAL DEFAULT

**20.1** In the case of any ongoing material default(s) of compliance by the Owners of the obligation to properly complete and/or manage and maintain any Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof in accordance with the obligations under this Agreement which results in a material adverse impact on the Development SCDC and/or the County Council may:

**20.1.1** Subject to clauses 2.8 and 19.1 above provide written notification to the Owners setting out:

   - **(a)** the nature and extent of the material default; and
   - **(b)** the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to which the material default relates including a full note with details of the same; and
   - **(c)** the steps which are reasonably necessary and which the Owners are required to take in order to rectify the material default; and
   - **(d)** a reasonable timeframe for the Owners to rectify the material default

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) has:

   - **(e)** used Reasonable Endeavours to notify the Owners of such material default within a reasonable period having regard to the nature/seriousness of the default and following SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) becoming aware of the material default; and
   - **(f)** invited the Owners to enter into discussions with SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) with a view to rectifying that default.

**20.1.2** In the event that the Owners dispute any of the matters within Clause 20.1.1 the matter may be referred to the Expert pursuant to Clause 17 (Dispute Resolution) and for the avoidance of doubt this may include a dispute in respect of Clause 20.1.1(d) in which case the timeframe for rectification of the material default set by SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall not apply.

**20.2** In the event that the Owners do not rectify the material default to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) within the timeframe set out in the notice or within the timeframe set by the Expert SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) may then:

**20.2.1** provide written notification to the Owners that SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) intends to take steps to rectify the material default; and

**20.2.2** take any of the steps set out in the notice which may include the following: and which are in addition to any statutory powers it may have in this regard:

   - **(a)** to enter upon the area of the Facility and/or Amenity with or without vehicles and equipment;
   - **(b)** to carry out the steps identified at Clause 20.1.1(c) within the timeframe identified at Clause 20.1.1(d); and
   - **(c)** to recover SCDC's and/or the County Council's (as applicable) reasonable costs for carrying out such steps from the Owners.

**20.3** In the event that there is an on-going material default in respect of any one given part of any Facility and/or Amenity which results in a material adverse impact on the Development for a period of 12 months or more and PROVIDED THAT notice has already been served pursuant to Clause 20.1.1 then SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) may:

**20.3.1** provide written notification to the Owners setting out:

   - **(a)** the nature and extent of the ongoing material default; and
   - **(b)** the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to which the material default relates including a full note detailing the same; and
   - **(c)** the steps which are reasonably necessary and which the Owners are required to take in order to rectify the material default; and
   - **(d)** a reasonable timeframe for the Owners to rectify the material default; and
   - **(e)** that SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) may call for the transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to it or the Town Council in the event that the material default is not rectified within the timeframe.

**20.3.2** in the event that the Owners dispute any of the matters within Clause 20.1.1 the matter may be referred to the Expert pursuant to Clause 17 (Dispute Resolution) and for the avoidance of doubt this may include a dispute in respect of Clause 20.3.1(d) in which case the timeframe for rectification of the material default set by SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall not apply.

**20.4** In the event that Clause 20.3 applies and the Owners do not rectify the ongoing material default to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) within the timeframe set out in the notice or within the timeframe set by the Expert SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) may then call for a transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof.

**20.5** These provisions shall apply mutatis mutandis in favour of the Owners where SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) or if relevant the Town Council has taken ownership of an area of any Facility and/or Amenity pursuant to this Agreement.

**20.6** Where it is intended to transfer a Facility and/or Amenity to a particular Alternative Body and there has been an Insolvency Event in relation to that Alternative Body who was to be the transferee of such Facility and/or Amenity then the Transfer shall not proceed to that Alternative Body and instead the Transfer shall only proceed to an Alternative Body which has not been the subject of any Insolvency Event.

**20.7** Where:

**20.7.1** any Facility and/or Amenity has been leased to a particular Alternative Body; and

**20.7.2** an Insolvency Event has subsequently occurred in relation to that Alternative Body then:

**20.7.3** the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure a lease or a Transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity to an Alternative Body which has not been the subject of any Insolvency Event; and

**20.7.4** in the event that there is an outstanding material default which has not been rectified the provisions of Clause 20.1 to Clause 20.4 shall apply and as applicable SCDC may call for a transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to SCDC or the Town Council and the County Council may call for a transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to the County Council.

## 21. LOCAL LAND CHARGE REGISTRATION

**21.1** SCDC and/or the County Council (where applicable) shall register this Agreement in its Register of Local Land Charges as soon as practicable after the date of this Agreement.

**21.2** Where the Agreement is released in part by a future Agreement, SCDC and/or the County Council (where applicable) will place a note to that effect against the entry made in the Local Land Charges Register.

**21.3** Where the Agreement comes to an end under Clause 9 (Termination of this Agreement) SCDC shall vacate or cancel the entry made in the Local Land Charges register in relation to this Agreement or otherwise record the fact that it has come to an end and no longer affects the Site.

**21.4** If the Owners make a request in writing for SCDC to place a note against the entry made in the Local Land Charges Register stating which obligations under this Agreement have been wholly or partially discharged released and/or complied with, SCDC will place such a note against the entry PROVIDED THAT such obligations have been discharged released and/or complied with.

## 22. CONTRACTS (RIGHTS OF THIRD PARTIES) ACT 1999

The Parties intend that no terms of this Agreement may be enforceable pursuant to the Contract (Rights of Third Parties) Act 1999 by any person who is not a Party to this Agreement save for:

**22.1** any successor(s) in title to the Owners; or

**22.2** any successor(s) in function to SCDC and the County Council; or

**22.3** where relevant the Alternative Body or any successor(s) to the Alternative Body.

## 23. INDEXATION

**23.1** All financial amounts set out in this Agreement or the Schedules hereto relating to Contributions or Works in Kind are stated as at the date of this Agreement but where any such amounts fall due for payment or are paid after the date of this Agreement and are stated to be subject to Indexation as set out in the Indexation Table the relevant figure shall be adjusted to reflect changes in the Index (either as specified in the Indexation Table as attached at Appendix 2 or where not specified in the Indexation Table the Index agreed between the Parties acting reasonably) between the date of 29 July 2015 and the relevant payment date.

**23.2** The Parties to this Agreement acknowledge that when indexation is calculated on any payment then the relevant Index figures to calculate the indexation amount applying for the period between 29th July 2015 and the relevant payment date shall be the figures set out in the latest Index published at the relevant payment date as shown in the Indexation Table whether the figures in that latest published Index show forecast or other figures applicable for 29th July 2015 and the relevant payment date.

## 24. INTEREST ON LATE PAYMENT AND OCCUPATION RESTRICTION(S)

If any payment(s) due from the Owners under any of the provisions of this Agreement are not made on or before the date on which the payment is due (for which purpose a sum shall be taken as due not later than the day before the relevant event occurs in relation to Occupation and/or Commencement of Development or otherwise prior to which the payment is to be made) the sum due shall bear Interest from the due date until the date of payment and the Owners covenant with SCDC and the County Council to pay any Interest accrued at the same time and to the same recipient as the principal sum on which it has accrued is paid.

## 25. SECURITY

**25.1** The parties agree that HCA is of sufficient financial standing such that HCA shall not be obliged to provide any security to the Councils in respect of compliance with the Planning Obligations as landowner except in the events that:

**25.1.1** 1/3rd of the total of HCA's Assets are sold or cease to be partly or wholly owned by HCA; or

**25.1.2** if HCA so elects.

**25.2** In respect of Clause 25.1 HCA shall prior to the occurrence of such events:

**25.2.1** notify the Councils not less than 15 (fifteen) days in advance of such sale or other disposal which will result in the reduction of HCA's assets by 1/3rd; and

**25.2.2** provide or procure the provision of such security in accordance with Clause 25.3 below as the Councils acting reasonably may require and it shall not proceed with such sale or disposal of HCA's Assets (in whole or in part) such that it results in a reduction of those Assets by 1/3rd of its total Assets until such security has been provided.

**25.3** Where required by Clause 25.1 above and/or after the occurrence of such event or events as referred to in Clause 25.1 then prior to the Commencement Date (provided the Planning Permission has not been Commenced) or 30 days prior to the next Contributions Trigger following such event (whichever is the sooner) HCA shall secure the Planning Obligations by any of the following in accordance with Appendix 5 or such other means reasonably required by the Councils:

**25.3.1** a guarantee or guarantees by up to three Relevant Limited Companies; or

**25.3.2** a Contribution Bond in the form of the draft set out in Appendix 3 in respect of such obligations Provided That should such a Contribution Bond not be reasonably available on the market at the time or should the total cost (including fees) of obtaining such a Contribution Bond exceed the sum of 2% per annum of the outstanding Contribution Bond Sum this obligation shall cease and determine and HCA shall instead procure a guarantee or guarantees pursuant to Clause 25.3.1 in respect of any outstanding obligations which would otherwise have been required to be covered by a Contribution Bond under this Clause;

**25.3.3** a land charge in the form of the draft set out in Appendix 6; or

**25.3.4** any other form of security which may be approved in writing by the Councils acting reasonably.

**25.4** If security is given by means of one or more guarantees under Clause 25.3.1 (by a Relevant Limited Company or Companies) then the following provisions shall apply:

**25.4.1** HCA shall produce to the Councils:

   - **(a)** copy of the last two years audited accounts of each Relevant Limited Company or Companies (and for each year thereafter copies of the relevant audited accounts until the relevant obligation or covenant shall have been fully performed or observed); and

   - **(b)** if the Relevant Limited Company is registered abroad a certificate from a lawyer practising in the jurisdiction in which the Relevant Limited Company or Companies is registered that the Relevant Limited Company or Companies has the capacity and authority to enter into the said security and that the document has been properly executed by the Relevant Limited Company or Companies in accordance with the requirements of that jurisdiction;

**25.4.2** if the Councils reasonably request in writing to HCA it shall within a reasonable time and in any event within 21 days cause the auditors of the Relevant Limited Company or Companies at the cost of HCA to supply to the Councils a certificate of the net asset value of the Relevant Limited Company or Companies as at the date of the certificate; and

**25.4.3** if any Relevant Limited Company or Companies shall at any time cease to be a Relevant Limited Company (for any reason) then:

   - **(a)** HCA shall notify the Councils having the benefit of such security of that fact; and

   - **(b)** HCA shall forthwith secure the performance or observance of the relevant planning obligation(s) or covenant(s) by any of the means set out in Clause 25.3.

**25.5** If security is given by means of a Contribution Bond(s) under Clause 25.3 then:

**25.5.1** The bond amounts shall secure the Contribution that remains to be paid together with Indexation calculated at the date the Contribution Bond is put in place.

**25.5.2** The bond amounts shall (subject to Clause 25.6) be subject to reduction upon the issue of a certificate of the gross value of works or contribution outstanding being below the initial bond amounts respectively subject to a sufficient reasonable margin.

**25.5.3** Following the payment of any Contribution secured by way of or the performance of any obligation secured by way of a Bond or Bonds to the County Council the Owners shall serve the Payment Notification on the County Council.

**25.5.4** The County Council covenants with the Owners that it shall within 5 Working Days of receipt of the Payment Notification issue a Release Letter to the Surety in respect of the relevant Bond acknowledging the payment of the said sum or Contribution or the performance of the said obligation and authorising the reduction of the Bond Sum and if the Bond Sum shall thereby be reduced to nil the said Release Letter shall authorise the release of the relevant Bond by the Surety thereunder.

**25.6** A certificate shall for the purposes of Clause 25.5.2 be approved by the SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) or determined by an Expert in accordance with Clause 17 in the event of a dispute.

**25.7** HCA hereby covenants with the Councils that where security in whatever form is to be provided by HCA in accordance with the above provisions:

**25.7.1** In respect of the Campus Site it shall be provided to the County Council forthwith and in respect of other work or contributions it shall be provided to the Councils prior to or not later than the Commencement Date or in the event that has already passed the trigger at which a Planning Obligation is due to be performed (or in the case of a Planning Obligation to be performed in Phases prior to the first of such Phases); and

**25.7.2** shall continue in effect until the Planning Obligations and covenants for which that security shall have been provided shall have been fully performed and observed.

**25.8** For the purposes of Clause 25.3.2 in relation to any Contributions Bond this means firstly that the bond (being in the form set out in Appendix 3 or in such other form as may reasonably be required by the Councils) is from a bank or insurance company of good repute (which bank or insurance company must first have been approved in writing by the Councils) and secondly that the bond has been validly and effectively executed, released and delivered to each of the Councils as required by this Deed.

**25.9** Where ever there is a restriction on Occupation provided for in this Agreement to secure the delivery of a Planning Obligation SCDC has the discretion to waive that restriction in favour of an alternative security or alternative means of enforcement acceptable to it to the intent that (without limitation) a purchaser of part of the Site which does not include a Facility, School Building or Amenity situated on that part and in respect of which a Reserved Matters Approval has been granted shall not be subject to a restriction tied to the delivery of a Facility, School Building or Amenity on other land which is not within its ownership or control.

## 26. HCA PRIMARILY LIABLE

Unless otherwise agreed in writing by the Councils the HCA is to remain primarily liable for proper performance and discharge of all covenants and obligations of the Owners save where a relevant Facility or Amenity or the Campus Site or a School Building is transferred to any of the Councils or an Alternative Body nominated in such regard by the Councils or where an Affordable Rented Housing Site is transferred to SCDC or a Registered Provider for bringing forward that part of the Development.

## 27. TITLE

The HCA warrants that the Site comprises and is made up exclusively by the HCA Land, the Option Land and the Balance Land and otherwise land which comprises adopted public highway and the HCA further warrants that save for the Option Land and the Balance Land there is no other land which needs to be bound by the Obligations to secure the proper planning and enhanced quality of the Development and to make it acceptable in planning terms that would otherwise be unacceptable other than the works that the Parties are agreed may be outside of the Site.

## 28. LAND TRANSFERS

Not used.

## 29. OVERALL CAP ON CONTRIBUTIONS

The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another as follows:

**29.1** Save as provided for in this Clause 29, the total liability of the Owners in respect of all Contributions and Work in Kind is limited to no more than £73 million (the "Overall Costs Cap") and which shall together cover:

**29.1.1** any monies paid by the Owners to SCDC or the County Council by way of a Contribution; and

**29.1.2** the Actual Cost of any Works in Kind carried out;

PROVIDED THAT the Parties to this Agreement acknowledge and agree that:

   - **(a)** the Overall Costs Cap shall be taken as being the aggregate of all Contributions and Actual Costs of any Works in Kind as described in this Agreement before any inflationary effects are taken into account by the imposition of the obligation to Index Link any such Contributions or Actual Costs of any Works in Kind as may be specified in this Agreement; and

   - **(b)** the costs of compliance:

      - **(i)** with the Affordable Housing obligations in Schedule 7 (Affordable Housing) and

      - **(ii)** with the ordnance obligations in Schedule 12 (Environment, Ordnance and Heritage); and

      - **(iii)** in delivering all of the Amenities

      shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

   - **(c)** where any remedial works or works to address Defects are to be undertaken by the Owners then the costs of undertaking such works shall fall outside of the Overall Costs Cap and shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded and such costs shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

   - **(d)** Subject to Clause 29.11 in the event the Actual Costs exceed the Allocated 106 Costs then the Owners shall be responsible for bearing such excess Actual Costs and shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded and such costs shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

   - **(e)** the cost of compliance by the Owners to the County Council pursuant to Part 2 of Schedule 2 to this Agreement shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded.

**29.2** The Owners shall continue to make full payments of the Contributions and complete the Works in Kind as required by this Agreement until such time as the Overall Costs Cap has been reached after which subject to the provisions of clause 29.1 above the liability of the Owners for those obligations that remain to be discharged in full or in part under this Agreement in respect of Contributions and Works in Kind shall cease (and these Contributions and Works in Kind shall be known as the "Remaining Obligations") and thereafter SCDC and the County Council shall pursuant to the respective obligations in paragraph 1.3 in Schedule 17 and paragraph 1.3 in Schedule 18 use all Reasonable Endeavours to identify and secure all such necessary funding to ensure that each of the Remaining Obligations or part thereof can be discharged as soon as possible having regard to the relevant trigger for such Remaining Obligations in accordance with the provisions of this Agreement whether this requires SCDC and/or the County Council to procure Completion of (i) any works to which Contributions would otherwise have been used or (ii) any Works in Kind that remain to be Completed.

**29.3** In the event that a situation where a Clawback Contribution arises in accordance with paragraph 1.2 of Schedule 17 and/or paragraph 1.2 of Schedule 18 then the provisions of Clause 29.4 shall apply as regards how any monies are to be held retained used expended or applied by SCDC and the County Council.

**29.4** SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another and with the Owners that should either be in possession of any monies that would by operation of this Agreement be considered a Clawback Contribution (which Council shall for the purposes of this Clause 29 be defined as the "Retaining Authority") then:

**29.4.1** the Retaining Authority shall immediately place the Clawback Contribution into a designated account from which monies shall only be permitted to be withdrawn either:

   - **(a)** to discharge any Remaining Obligations which by operation of Clause 29.2 the Owners shall have been released from performing and discharging; or

   - **(b)** to refund (where all Remaining Obligations under this Agreement have been discharged in full), to the relevant Party (i.e. that originally paid the relevant monies which now form the Clawback Contribution) any remaining monies in the account that relate to the Clawback Contribution together with interest accrued thereon (such interest to be calculated from the date the Clawback Contribution arose through the operation of the paragraphs recited in the definition of Clawback Contribution until the date of repayment of the relevant monies)

and for the avoidance of doubt SCDC and the County Council shall either respectively create their own designated accounts or jointly create a joint designated account where they become a Retaining Authority PROVIDED THAT where the Retaining Authority cannot for whatever reason create a designated account it shall propose an alternative means of ring fencing the Clawback Contribution(s) to the Owners that will suitably protect the Clawback Contribution(s) such that it shall only be capable of being drawn against for the purposes as set out above in this Clause 29.4.1 and the Retaining Authority shall before implementing the alternative means obtain the Owners' prior written approval or deemed approval pursuant to Clause 18 (Deemed Approval) to the alternative means PROVIDED ALWAYS that such alternative means of ring fencing shall not be acceptable where interest would not accrue on the Clawback Contribution(s) and PROVIDED FURTHER THAT until such time as an alternative means has been approved or deemed approved by the Owners the Retaining Authority shall ensure that the Clawback Contribution(s) is ring fenced and is not used or otherwise committed or expended and is attracting interest; and

**29.4.2** SCDC and the County Council shall together identify the Remaining Obligations and any Clawback Contribution(s) held by either SCDC or the County Council shall be used and expended to discharge those Remaining Obligations in the order of priority that such Remaining Obligations fall due to be performed or discharged as provided for in this Agreement; and

**29.4.3** SCDC and the County Council shall confirm to one another and the Owners in writing every six calendar months commencing two years after Commencement of Development:

   - **(a)** the amount of Clawback Contributions (if any) they hold pursuant to Clause 29.4.1 and which notification shall also identify the Contribution that the Clawback Contribution(s) originated from and the amount of the relevant original Contribution which remains unexpended, and

   - **(b)** where any Clawback Contribution has been expended to discharge any Remaining Obligations the notification shall identify which Remaining Obligation(s) has been so discharged

AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT SCDC and the County Council shall produce and serve:

   - **(c)** a written notification pursuant to this Clause 29.4.3 within 40 Working Days of the date when the last of the Remaining Obligations has been performed or discharged PROVIDED ALWAYS that for the purposes of this Clause 29.4.3(c) the Remaining Obligations shall not include any On Site SUDs Contribution payable to the Councils (or any one of them) pursuant to paragraph 3.5 of Part 1 of Schedule 4 of this Agreement; and

   - **(d)** a final written notification pursuant to this Clause 29.4.3 within 40 Working Days in the event that the On Site SUDs Contribution is payable to the Councils pursuant to paragraph 3.5 of Part 1 of Schedule 4.

**29.4.4** Where applicable SCDC and the County Council shall identify those Clawback Contributions that they each hold and shall agree as between themselves whether any monies need to be transferred as between SCDC and the County Council or vice versa in order to ensure (subject to there being sufficient monies held by SCDC or the County Council in the relevant designated or other approved account) that the next Remaining Obligation is then having regard to the relevant trigger performed or discharged in full PROVIDED THAT it is acknowledged that this may involve transfer of all or part of any Clawback Contribution held by a Retaining Authority to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) to be used to procure the carrying out of the Work in Kind or payment of Contributions and that SCDC and the County Council shall be permitted to utilise any and all Clawback Contributions that either hold at any time a Remaining Obligation falls due to be performed or discharged and to do so in priority to the use of any other funds that SCDC or the County Council shall secure pursuant to the obligations imposed by Clause 29.2.

**29.5** A Clawback Contribution shall be repayable to the Owners:

**29.5.1** where the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind pursuant to this Agreement total at least £6 million before Indexation; or

**29.5.2** following service of the written notifications pursuant to Clause 29.4.3 and the Retaining Authority shall when serving the written notification pursuant to Clause 29.4.3(c) above return any unexpended monies together with interest thereon to the Party that paid the Contribution that the Clawback Contribution originated from; and

**29.5.3** in the event that the On Site SUDS Contribution is payable to the Councils pursuant to paragraph 3.5 of Part 1 of Schedule 4 of this Agreement then when serving the final written notification pursuant to Clause 29.4.3(d) above the Council receiving the On Site SUDS Contribution shall return any unexpended monies together with interest thereon to the Party that paid the Contribution from which the Clawback Contribution originated.

**29.6** Where a Clawback Contribution is repayable to the Owners in accordance with Clause 29.5 SCDC and/or the County Council shall within 20 Working Days of the Clawback Contribution being due to be repaid repay any unexpended balance to the person that paid the relevant Contribution together with any interest accrued (less any tax which may be required to be deducted by law) SAVE THAT where the construction of any buildings or works in relation to which such contribution(s) have been paid have been completed prior to the expiration of relevant time period then this paragraph shall operate subject to the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind pursuant to this Agreement totalling at least £6 million before Indexation so as to obligate SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) to repay any unexpended balance to the Party that paid the relevant Contribution within 20 Working Days of the issue of a certificate or equivalent that confirms the completion of the construction of the relevant building and completion of any maintenance or defects period.

**29.7** In the event that there has been a Procurement Cost Saving in respect of any particular Facility then provided that:

**29.7.1** All obligations in respect of both the Works in Kind and the Contributions have been discharged under this Agreement; and

**29.7.2** the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind payable pursuant to this Agreement total at least £6 million before Indexation

then once the Works in Kind have been Completed or a Council Final Certificate has been issued (as applicable) in respect of the particular Facility pursuant to this Agreement, then the Owners shall be entitled to retain all of the Procurement Cost Saving relevant to the particular Facility in question.

**29.8** Once the Works in Kind have been Completed or a Council Final Certificate has been issued (as applicable) in respect of any particular Facility pursuant to this Agreement within 15 Working Days of such occurrence, the Owners shall notify SCDC in respect of SCDC Matters or the County Council in respect of County Matters of the following:

**29.8.1** the Actual Cost of the Works in Kind; and

**29.8.2** confirmation of whether there has been any Procurement Cost Savings made in respect of the Works in Kind and demonstrating such Procurement Cost Savings and identifying the quantum to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC in respect of SCDC Matters and the County Council in respect of County Matters and the means by which it will be demonstrated which shall be applied in all instances shall be agreed between the Owners the County Council and SCDC.

**29.9** In the event the Owners are entitled by the operation of any part of the provisions in this Clause 29 (Overall Cap on Contributions) to retain any part of a Procurement Cost Saving or be paid a Clawback Contribution then it is agreed that the Owners shall not be required to apply or use such monies in relation to Works in Kind and/or the Contributions.

**29.10** The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another and agree that once the Overall Costs Cap has been reached (taking account of the provisions of Clause 29.1 and the provisions of Affordable Housing in accordance with Schedule 7 (Affordable Housing)) the Owners shall have no further liability in respect of or responsibility for and shall be released from discharging those covenants and obligations in this Agreement as to payment of Contributions by the Owners and completion of Work in Kind by the Owners and SCDC and the County Council covenant with the Owners that they shall both comply with the provisions of Clause 29.2 in relation to the completion of relevant works and obligations which then remain outstanding.

**29.11** Where there are Allocated 106 Costs but there is also reference to work being undertaken to a particular standard the cost of meeting that standard over and above the Allocated 106 Costs the Owners shall be responsible for bearing such excess Actual Costs but such costs shall be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded.

## 30. JURISDICTION

This Agreement is governed by and interpreted in accordance with the Laws of England.

## 31. COUNTERPARTS

This Deed may be executed in any number of counterparts each of which shall constitute an original and all the counterparts shall together constitute one and the same agreement.

Delivered as a deed on the date of this document.

## Schedule 2 — Education

### Relevant Definitions

**"Building Bulletin 99"** means the Design Bulletin 99 (Briefing Framework for Primary School Projects)(second edition) (internal and external fittings) incorporating revisions to BB82: Area Guidelines for Schools 2006 published by The Stationery Office ISBN-13 978 0 11 277153 7 and ISBN-10 0 11 271153 7 or any replacement or revision thereof in force at the time the Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification is agreed;

**"Building Bulletin 98"** means the Design Bulletin 98 (Briefing Framework for Secondary School Projects incorporating revisions to BB82: Area Guidelines for Schools (Secondary edition) published by The Stationery Office or any replacement or revision thereof in force at the time the specification for the Secondary School Building is agreed;

**"Campus Site"** means the area of land within the Site with a minimum site area of 16.74 hectares to be provided by the Owners to the County Council for the provision of the Secondary School Building the Post 16 Provision Contribution Purposes, the Special School the First Primary School Building the Indoor Sports Contribution Purposes and the Community Facilities whose location is shown edged dark blue on Plan 5 in Appendix 23 meeting the School Site Specification or such other area of land as may be agreed between the County Council and the Owners for this purpose;

**"Campus Site Land Transfer"** means a transfer by the Owners to the County Council of the unencumbered freehold interest in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 10 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the Campus Site and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer on terms to be agreed but which shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict use of the land for education purposes and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto which for the avoidance of doubt may include community uses; and

   - **(b)** not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

   - **(c)** include for the benefit of the Campus Site the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the management and maintenance of the Primary School Land for its purpose for primary education; and

   - **(d)** be at nil consideration; and

   - **(e)** include a covenant that the Campus Site shall not be used for any purpose other than for use as an education facility and associated ancillary facilities which for the avoidance of doubt may include community uses.

**"Campus Site Preparatory Works"** means work for the preparation of the Campus Site in readiness for construction of the Secondary School Building, the First Primary School Building, Post 16 Provision Contribution Purposes and the Special School together with the associated playing fields;

**"Community Facilities"** means the campus facilities namely the indoor and outdoor sports facilities (including playing fields, artificial grass pitch, multi-use games areas, sports hall, swimming pool) and secondary school hall as sport, community and arts facilities;

**"Community Facilities Principles Agreement"** means an agreement setting out the principles of community use and access to the Community Facilities in the form set out at Appendix 22 or in such other form as shall be agreed in writing by SCDC.

**"Detailed Primary School or Secondary School Building Works Specification"** means the detailed specification and plans of the School Building addressing construction materials programme and methods of working which shall be approved by the Project Core Team;

**"Early Years Facility/Opportunity"** means the opportunity to provide a facility for childcare teaching and learning facilities for children up to the age of 5;

**"Early Years Lease"** means a lease on reasonable commercial terms and appropriate for an Early Years Facility either (as may be agreed between the HCA and the Early Years Provider) of land on terms which require the Early Years Provider to construct the Early Years Facility or of an Early Years Facility constructed or to be constructed by the HCA;

**"Early Years Offer"** means delivery unconditionally but open for 90 days to the relevant Early Years Provider of a single contract to grant and take an Early Years Lease of the relevant Early Years Facility executed as a deed by the freeholders of the whole of the relevant Early Years Facility which shall be delivered with the intention by the freeholder that the Early Years Provider may sign and date the said contract so that it becomes legally enforceable between the parties;

**"Early Years Provider"** means a person or organisation providing childcare teaching and learning facilities for children up to the age of 5;

**"First Primary School Building"** means an establishment provided under the Education Act 1996 that provides education to children from birth to 11 years on the Campus Site and which in this case shall be a three form entry primary school facility with three classes of early years provisions constructed in accordance with guidance on standards as set out in Building Bulletin 99 and constructed in accordance with the Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification and in accordance with the approved drawings, plans, specification and other documents;

**"First Primary School Contribution"** means the sum of £11,820,000 (Eleven Million Eight Hundred and Twenty Thousand British Pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely for the First Primary School Contribution Purpose;

**"First Primary School Contribution Purpose"** means the use of the First Primary School Contribution towards the design and construction of the First Primary School Building on the Campus Site;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification"** means the specification for the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Works to be submitted by the Owners to the Project Core Team to inform the detailed design of the Campus Site and then to the County Council for written approval in accordance with paragraphs 1.1.1 and 1.1.2 of Part 2 of Schedule 2;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Works"** means the works to be done on the Campus Site prior to the transfer of the Campus Site to the County Council and will be taken to mean the following:

   - **(a)** satisfactory temporary construction access (roads and footpaths) of a specification and in a location acceptable to the County Council having regard to other construction activity that is to take place on the Campus Site and which can be utilised by both the County Council and the Owners and in all cases such temporary construction access or a suitable alternative shall remain available without restriction or interruption (save in cases of emergency or as part of everyday construction site health and safety) for the duration of time required by the County Council and it shall afford proper access to the main highway network; and

   - **(b)** the provision of a temporary potable water supply to the Campus Site; and

   - **(c)** the provision of a temporary electricity supply to the Campus Site;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 1) Second Primary School Specification"** means the specification for the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Second Primary School Works to be submitted by the Owners to the Project Core Team to inform the detailed design of the Second Primary School and then to the County Council for written approval in accordance with paragraphs 3.1.1 of Part 2 of Schedule 2;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 1) Second Primary School Works"** means the works to be done on the Second Primary School Land prior to the transfer of the Second Primary School Land to the County Council and will be taken to mean the following:

   - **(a)** satisfactory temporary construction access (roads and footpaths) of a specification and in a location acceptable to the County Council having regard to other construction activity that is to take place on the Second Primary School Land and which can be utilised by both the County Council and the Owners and in all cases such temporary construction access or a suitable alternative shall remain available without restriction or interruption (save in cases of emergency or as part of everyday construction site health and safety) for the duration of time required by the County Council and it shall afford proper access to the main highway network; and

   - **(b)** the provision of a temporary potable water supply to the Second Primary School Land; and

   - **(c)** the provision of a temporary electricity supply to the Second Primary School Land;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Specification"** means the specification for the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Works to be submitted by the Owners to the Project Core Team to inform the detailed design of the Campus Site and then County Council for written approval in accordance with paragraphs 2.1.1 of Part 2 of Schedule 2;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Works"** means the work to be done on the Site pursuant to paragraph 2.1.2 of Part 2 of Schedule 2 and will be taken to mean the following:

   - **(a)** the connection of all main services to the Campus Site commensurate with the energy/service demands indicated by the County Council (acting reasonably) for the Phase 1 Education Uses (or where the Phase 1 Education Uses are to be phased by the County Council for the relevant phase for which the energy/service demands have been provided) of gas, electricity, foul and surface water drainage and telecommunications to a convenient point or points adjacent to the boundary of the Campus Site at a capacity suitable to serve the relevant part and intended purpose of the Campus Site and be capable of interconnection to serve the infrastructure to be constructed thereon;

   - **(b)** serviced with roads to the boundary of the Campus Site commensurate to their intended purpose and constructed to adoptable standard;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Specification"** means the specification for the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Works to be submitted by the Owners to the Project Core Team to inform the detailed design of the Second Primary School and then County Council for written approval in accordance with paragraphs 3.1.2 of Part 2 of Schedule 2;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Works"** means the works to be done on the Site pursuant to paragraph 3.1.7 of Part 2 of Schedule 2 and will be taken to mean the following:

   - **(a)** the connection of all main services to the Second Primary School Land commensurate for their intended purpose of gas, electricity, foul and surface water drainage and telecommunications to a convenient point or points adjacent to the boundary of the Second Primary School Land at a capacity suitable to serve the relevant part and intended purpose of the Second Primary School Land and be capable of interconnection to serve the infrastructure to be constructed thereon;

   - **(b)** serviced with roads to the boundary of the Second Primary School Land commensurate to their intended purpose and constructed to adoptable standard;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Specification"** means the specification for the Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Works to be submitted by the Owners to the Project Core Team to inform the detailed design of the Campus Site and then County Council for written approval in accordance with paragraphs 2.1.5 of Part 2 of Schedule 2;

**"Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Works"** means the works to be done on the Site pursuant to paragraph 2.1.6 of Part 2 of Schedule 2 and will be taken to mean the connection of all main services to the Campus Site commensurate for their intended purpose of gas, electricity, foul and surface water drainage and telecommunications to a convenient point or points adjacent to the boundary of the Campus Site at a capacity suitable to serve the Secondary School Building (Phase 2) as well as the Phase 1 Education Uses and be capable of interconnection to serve the infrastructure to be constructed thereon;

**"Free Serviced Specifications"** means the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification, the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Second Primary School Specification, the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Specification, the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Specification, Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Specification;

**"Highways Agreement"** means an agreement made under section 38 and/or section 278 of the Highways Act 1980 as otherwise agreed between the Owners and the County Council;

**"Indoor Sports Building"** means a building with an internal floor space of at least 1,424 square metres (unless otherwise agreed between SCDC, the County Council and the Owners) to be constructed by the County Council on the Campus Site as part of the Secondary School Building (Phase 2) and which building shall provide a 4 court sports-hall, gym and exercise studios and associated facilities for sports and recreational use by the community as well as the Secondary School together with ancillary cafe and associated car parking facilities;

**"Indoor Sports Building Contribution"** means the sum of £3,208,649 (three million two hundred and eight thousand six hundred and forty-nine pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely for the Indoor Sports Building Contribution Purpose;

**"Indoor Sports Building Contribution Purpose"** means for the construction completion and fitting out and equipping of the Indoor Sports Building in accordance with the Indoor Sports Specification;

**"Indoor Sports Building Specification"** means the specification for the Indoor Sports Building appended to this Agreement at Appendix 11;

**"Market"** means for a period of 12 months ("the Marketing Period") the bona fide seeking of offers from relevant persons by undertaking some or all of the following activities as appropriate to the circumstances (and the expression "Marketing" shall be construed accordingly): advertising in at least 2 relevant periodicals or newspapers, writing to relevant persons in the business of providing the facilities relevant to the obligation to which the Marketing relates, pursuing leads provided by the SCDC and/or the County Council as the case may be;

**"MUGA"** means a multi games area comprising of the following:

   - **(a)** 4 court for tennis, netball and basketball;

   - **(b)** fully floodlit which must meet the relevant Governing Body of Sport (Lawn Tennis Association and the All England Netball Association) standard and also BS EN 1293:2007. The floodlights will have demountable columns and all electric components will be contained within the columns to help ensure easy maintenance — they will be required to meet BS EN 12193:2007 quality standard;

   - **(c)** a gross area of 0.3ha;

   - **(d)** to meet BS EN 15312:2007 and the relevant and most recent Governing Body of Sport (Lawn Tennis Association and the All England Netball Association) standards in terms of design, dimensions, drainage and foundations and construction for the 'primary' sport. It should also be equipped to meet the relevant Governing Body of Sport (Lawn Tennis Association and the All England Netball Association) recommendations including provision of goals, fencing, posts and nets;

   - **(e)** constructed tennis and netball courts will meet the "club" standards of the relevant Governing Bodies of Sport (Lawn Tennis Association and the All England Netball Association) in all aspects of build including foundations, drainage, construction, dimensions, floodlights, fencing and macadam-type playing surface. The courts will be equipped with playing lines, posts and nets as recommended by the relevant Governing Body;

   - **(f)** detailed design / specifications and maintenance and management plans must be submitted to and approved by the LPA;

   - **(g)** minimum buffer zone of 80m;

to be constructed by companies approved by the Sport and Play Construction Association (SAPCA).

**"Northstowe Phase 1 Site"** has the same meaning as in Clause 1 of this Agreement;

**"Northstowe Phase 2 Site"** means the phase of development for Northstowe submitted by the Homes and Communities Agency under application number S/2011/7401;

**"Opening Date"** means the first date of the use of the relevant School Building;

**"Phase 1 Education Uses"** means the Secondary School Building (Phase 1) the Special School and the Indoor Sports Building;

**"Post 16 Provision Contribution"** means the sum of £3,640,000 (Three Million Six Hundred and Forty Thousand British Pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely on the Post 16 Provision Contribution Purposes;

**"Post 16 Provision Contribution Purposes"** means the use of the Post 16 Provision Contribution towards the delivery and provision of post 16 education for up to 300 students on the Campus Site;

**"Practical Completion"** means unless the context otherwise admits the issue of a certificate of practical completion by a suitably qualified architect, surveyor or engineer as the case may be and the expressions "Complete", "Completion" and "Completed" shall be construed accordingly;

**"Primary School Revenue Contribution"** means the sum of £100,000 (One Hundred Thousand British Pounds) to be used towards the initial funding required to start up the First Primary School Building and the Second Primary School Building including the appointment of a head teacher or head teachers and administrative support prior to the Opening Date;

**"Project Core Team"** means a steering group comprised of representatives of the County Council (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team), the School Promoter that will operate and manage the primary school and/or secondary school that will be located within the relevant School Building (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team), HCA or their nominee (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team) and SCDC (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team as relates to the Community Facilities) and which group shall be solely responsible for taking any decision in relation to any and all of the following:

   - **(a)** detailed Primary School or Secondary School Building Works Specification including for the avoidance of doubt the construction standards, design, layout, fixtures and fitting applicable to the construction of the School Building;

   - **(b)** any decisions relating to matters and works arising out of RIBA Work Stages 0 to 5 as set out in the RIBA Outline Plan of Work;

   - **(c)** approving the timetable for the design procurement and construction of the School Building;

   - **(d)** approving the employer's requirements for the School Building;

   - **(e)** approving any significant changes to the design proposed by the contractor by the County Council to construct the School Building;

PROVIDED THAT each party acting reasonably may be permitted to invite other interested parties to the meetings as agreed by the other parties in advance;

**"Project Core Team Terms of Reference"** means those matters referred to in Part 6 to this Schedule which are the responsibilities and powers and extent of the Project Core Team's decision making;

**"Relevant Permission"** means the last planning permission or Reserved Matters Approval necessary to be obtained to enable the lawful construction of the entity in question;

**"RIBA Plan of Work"** means the RIBA Plan of Work 2013 or any replacement or revision thereof and includes the following Work Stages and any reference in this Agreement to RIBA Works Stages shall be Work Stages 0 to 5 inclusive set out in the RIBA Plan of Work 2013;

**"School Building"** means the First Primary School Building, the Second Primary School Building, the Secondary School Building and the Special School and any one of them;

**"School Building Contribution"** means the First Primary School Contribution, the Post 16 Provision Contribution, the Second Primary School Building Contribution, the Secondary School Contribution and the Special School Contribution;

**"Second Primary School Building"** means an establishment provided under the Education Act 1996 that provides education to children from birth to 11 years on the Second Primary School Land and which in this case shall be a two form entry primary school facility with two classes of early years provisions constructed in accordance with guidance on standards as set out in Building Bulletin 99 and constructed in accordance with the Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification and in accordance with the approved drawings, plans, specification and other documents;

**"Second Primary School Building Contribution"** means the sum of £8,570,000 (Eight million five hundred and seventy thousand British pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely for the Secondary Primary School Building Contribution Purposes;

**"Second Primary School Building Contribution Purposes"** means the use of the Second Primary School Contribution towards the design and construction of the Second Primary School Building on the Second Primary School Land;

**"Second Primary School Land"** means the area of land within the Site shall be 2.3 hectares in size to be provided by the Owners whose location is shown edged light blue on Plan 5 at Appendix 23 or such other location as shall be agreed between the County Council and the Owners and in each case meeting the School Site Specification PROVIDED THAT in any event the Second Primary School Land shall be accessed by a road which shall be built to an adoptable standard AND PROVIDED FURTHER that the Owners shall use reasonable endeavours to enter into a Highway Agreement as to future adoption of such road;

**"Second Primary School Land Transfer"** means a transfer by the Owners to the County Council of the unencumbered freehold interest in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 10 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the Second Primary School Land and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer on terms to be agreed but which shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict use of the land as a Primary School and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto which for the avoidance of doubt may include community uses; and

   - **(b)** not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

   - **(c)** include for the benefit of the Second Primary School Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the management and maintenance of the Second Primary School Land for its purpose for primary education; and

   - **(d)** be at nil consideration; and

   - **(e)** include a covenant that the Second Primary School Land shall not be used for any purpose other than for use as a primary education and associated ancillary facilities which for the avoidance of doubt may include community uses;

**"School Promoter"** means the group, company, charitable body, or other such organisation which has been selected and approved by the Secretary of State for Education to establish a school. The promoter is then responsible for all aspects of running and managing the school including admissions, teaching and learning, governance and employment of staff;

**"School Site Specification"** the specification for the Campus Site and the Second Primary School Land set out in Appendix 7;

**"Second Primary School Preparatory Works"** means works for the preparation of the Second Primary School Land in readiness for the construction of the Second Primary School Building and playing fields;

**"Secondary School Building"** means a twelve form entry secondary school facility to be built on the Campus Site which may be built on a phased basis which shall also include car parking landscaping and outdoor play provision constructed in accordance with guidance on standards as set out in Building Bulletin 98 and references to "Secondary School Building (Phase 1)" shall be the Secondary School Building constructed to a four form entry secondary school facility and reference to "Secondary School Building (Phase 2)" shall be the extension of the Secondary School Building (Phase 1) to provide a twelve form entry secondary school facility;

**"Secondary School Contribution"** means the sum of £10,570,000 (Ten Million Five Hundred and Seventy Thousand British pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely on the Secondary School Contribution Purposes;

**"Secondary School Contribution Purposes"** means the use of the Secondary School Contribution towards the design and construction of the expansion of the Secondary School Building on the Campus Site or alternatively towards providing additional secondary school provision or improvement within the vicinity of the Site which is capable of meeting the additional demand that has arisen from the Development as a result of the increase in the projected child yield;

**"Special School"** means a school providing education to children aged 0-19 years with special education needs to be co-located on the Campus Site;

**"Special School Contribution"** means the sum of £2,760,120 (Two Million Seven Hundred and Sixty Thousand One Hundred and Twenty British Pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely on the Special School Contribution Purposes;

**"Special School Contribution Purposes"** means the use of the Special School Contribution towards the design and construction of the Special School or alternatively towards providing additional special needs school provision or improvement within the vicinity of the Site which is capable of meeting the additional demand that has arisen from the Development as a result of the increase in the projected child yield;

**"Special School Revenue Contribution"** means the sum of £29,172 (Twenty Nine Thousand One Hundred and Seventy Two British Pounds) to be used by the County Council solely towards funding the delivery, maintenance or running cost of the Special School.

### Part 1 — Early Years Opportunity

The Owners covenant with the County Council:

**1.1** To Market the Early Years Opportunity from Commencement of Development on the first Development Parcel which includes Dwellings for the Marketing Period.

**1.2** If during the Marketing Period serious written interest is obtained from an Early Years Provider confirming an intent to take an Early Years Lease to use Reasonable Endeavours to make an Early Years Offer.

### Part 2 — Delivery of the Campus Site and Primary School

Steps to facilitate the transfer of the Campus Site to the County Council

**1.1** The Owners covenant with the County Council to undertake the following unless otherwise agreed with the County Council:

**1.1.1** To prepare and submit to the County Council (having consulted the Project Core Team) the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification approval prior to the grant of the Relevant Permission for the Secondary School Building or by 31st July 2017 (whichever is the later) and in any event prior to Commencement.

**1.1.2** Not to allow or permit Commencement until the County Council has confirmed approval (or approval has been deemed pursuant to Clause 18 of this Agreement) to the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification.

**1.1.3** Within 6 months of the approval of the Relevant Permission for the Secondary School Building or by 31st October 2017 (whichever is the later) to undertake:

   - **(a)** (subject to Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification being approved by the County Council (or deemed to be approved)) the Practical Completion of the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Works not otherwise than in accordance with the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification as approved by the County Council (or deemed to be approved); and

   - **(b)** the delivery to the County Council by hand or recorded delivery of the Campus Site Land Transfer properly executed by the Owners and/or anyone else with an interest in the Campus Site; and

**1.1.4** Unless otherwise agreed with the County Council the Owners will not further Commence that part of the Development that has not yet Commenced unless:

   - **(a)** the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Works have been Practically Completed on the Campus Site in accordance with the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Campus Site Specification as approved in writing by the County Council (or deemed to be approved); and

   - **(b)** the Campus Site Land Transfer which has been duly and properly executed by the Owners or anyone else with an interest in the Campus Site has been delivered to the County Council by hand or recorded delivery.

**1.2** Prior to the transfer to the County Council of the Campus Site the Owners covenant to allow the County Council and any person authorised by the County Council upon prior reasonable written notice and subject at all times to relevant health and safety requirements to enter the Campus Site with or without plant or machinery for the purpose of carrying out surveys, inspections and the Campus Site Preparatory Works (provided that such works do not constitute Commencement).

Steps following the transfer of the Campus Site to the County Council

**2.1** The Owners covenant with the County Council to undertake the following:

**2.1.1** within 3 months of the County Council notifying the Owners of the energy and service demands for the Phase 1 Education Uses (or where the Phase 1 Education Uses are to be phased by the County Council for the phase for which the County Council has provided details) to prepare and submit to the County Council (having consulted the Project Core Team) the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Specification;

**2.1.2** (unless otherwise agreed in writing with the County Council and subject to Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Specification being approved by the County Council (or deemed to be approved)) 6 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Phase 1 Education Uses (or where the Phase 1 Education Uses are to be phased by the County Council the anticipated opening date for the relevant part) to undertake and complete the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Works upon the Campus Site such works to be completed in accordance with the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Specification as approved in writing by the County Council (or deemed to be approved);

**2.1.3** not to further Commence or permit or allow further Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date 6 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Phase 1 Education Uses (or where the Phase 1 Education Uses are to be phased by the County Council the anticipated opening date for the relevant part) (or such other date as may otherwise have been agreed in writing by the County Council) until the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Works have been completed upon the Campus Site in accordance with paragraph 2.1.2 of this Part 2 of Schedule 2; and

**2.1.4** for the avoidance of doubt where the Phase 1 Education Uses are to be phased by the County Council and the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Campus Site Works are also phased paragraph 2.1.1, 2.1.2 and 2.1.3 will apply to each phase of the Phase 1 Education Uses.

**2.1.5** within 3 months of the County Council notifying the Owners of the energy and service demands for the Secondary School Building (Phase 2) to prepare and submit to the County Council (having consulted the Project Core Team) the Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Specification;

**2.1.6** (unless otherwise agreed in writing with the County Council and subject to Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Specification being approved by the County Council (or deemed to be approved)) 6 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Secondary School Building (Phase 2) to undertake and complete the Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Works upon the Campus Site such works to be completed in accordance with the Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Specification as approved in writing by the County Council (or deemed to be approved);

**2.1.7** not to further Commence or permit or allow further Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date 6 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Secondary School Building (Phase 2) from the date of the Campus Site Land Transfer (or such other date as may otherwise have been agreed in writing by the County Council) until the Free Serviced (Phase 3) Campus Site Works have been completed upon the Campus Site in accordance with paragraph 2.1.6 of this Part 2 of Schedule 2.

**2.2** In order to enable the Owners to meet their obligations under this Paragraph 2 the County Council covenant to notify the Owners of:

**2.2.1** the anticipated opening date for the Phase 1 Education Uses and the Secondary School Building (Phase 2) 18 months prior to the anticipated opening date for each; and

**2.2.2** the energy and service demands for:

   - **(a)** the Phase 1 Education Uses (or where the Phase 1 Education Uses are to be phased by the County Council for the relevant phase);

   - **(b)** the Second School Building (Phase 2)

12 months prior to the relevant anticipated opening date.

Steps to facilitate the Second Primary School

**3.1** The Owners covenant with the County Council to undertake the following unless otherwise agreed with the County Council:

**3.1.1** Prior to the date being 21 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Second Primary School to prepare and submit to the County Council (having consulted the Project Core Team) the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Second Primary School Specification;

**3.1.2** within 3 months of the County Council notifying the Owners of the energy and service demands for the Second Primary School to prepare and submit to the County Council (having consulted the Project Core Team) the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Specification;

**3.1.3** Within 6 months of the approval of the Relevant Permission for the Second Primary School Building and 14 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Second Primary School (whichever is the later) to deliver to the County Council by hand or recorded delivery of the Second Primary School Land Transfer properly executed by the Owners and/or anyone else with an interest in the Second Primary School Land Transfer;

**3.1.4** (unless otherwise agreed in writing with the County Council and subject to the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Secondary School Primary Specification being approved by the County Council (or deemed to be approved)) 6 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Second Primary School to undertake and complete Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Works such work to be completed in accordance with the Free Serviced (Phase 1) Second Primary School Specification as approved in writing by the County Council (or deemed to be approved); and

**3.1.5** (unless otherwise agreed with the County Council) not to further Commence or permit or allow further Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date being 3 months of the County Council notifying the Owners of the energy and service demands for the Second Primary School unless the Owners have prepared and submitted to the County Council the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Specification;

**3.1.6** (unless otherwise agreed with the County Council) not to further Commence or permit or allow further Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date being 6 months of the approval of the Relevant Permission for the Second Primary School Building and 14 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Second Primary School (whichever is the later) unless the Second Primary School Land Transfer which has been duly and properly executed by the Owners or anyone else with an interest in the Second Primary School Land has been delivered to the County Council by hand or recorded delivery;
