**3.1.7** (unless otherwise agreed with the County Council) not to further Commence or permit or allow further Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date being 6 months prior to the anticipated opening date for the Second Primary School unless the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Works have been Practically Completed on the Second Primary School Land in accordance with the Free Serviced (Phase 2) Second Primary School Specification as approved in writing by the County Council (or deemed to be approved);

**3.1.8** Prior to the transfer to the County Council of the Second Primary School Land to allow the County Council and any person authorised by the County Council upon prior reasonable written notice to enter the Second Primary School Land with or without plant or machinery for the purpose of carrying out surveys, inspections and the Second Primary School Preparatory Works (provided that such works do not constitute Commencement).

**3.2** In order to enable the Owners to meet their obligations under this Paragraph 3 of this Part 2 of Schedule 2 the County Council covenant to notify the Owners of:

**3.2.1** the anticipated opening date for the Second Primary School 24 months prior to the anticipated opening date; and

**3.2.2** the energy and service demands for the Second Primary School 18 months prior to the relevant anticipated opening date.

**3.3** The Owners and the County Council hereby agree:

**3.3.1** to use reasonable endeavours to reuse the façade "central section" of the main officers mess building currently situated on the Site to form part of the Second Primary School;

**3.3.2** prior to the 2000th Occupation across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site to jointly procure an update to the feasibility study that was previously undertaken to assess the feasibility of using the officers' mess for a school such update to also be accompanied by a full structural survey, asbestos survey and any other survey required by statute or as agreed between the Owners and the County Council in order to assess the retention and reuse of the said façade which shall include but not limited to the suitability of the façade's retention and reuse, the cost effectiveness of the façade's retention and reuse in connection with the Second Primary School Building Contribution and any increase in maintenance of the façade part of the Second Primary School;

**3.3.3** where the conclusions of the surveys and further studies lead to the County Council and the Owners agreeing (acting reasonably) that the officers' mess façade "central section" can be reused in the construction of the Second Primary School the Second Primary School Land shall be transferred with the officers' mess in situ; and

**3.3.4** where the officers' mess façade "central section" is not to be reused the Second Primary School Land shall be transferred by the Owners to the County Council in accordance with the County Council's usual specification appended as Appendix 7.

The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that the benefit of any and all guarantees or warranties relating to the surveys, investigations and works undertaken by the Owners or its contractors before the Secondary Primary School Land is transferred to the County Council are assigned to the County Council.

### Part 3 — Education Contribution

The Owners shall pay the First Primary School Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**1.1** £11,820,000 (Eleven Million Eight Hundred and Twenty Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on or before Occupation of more than One Thousand Six Hundred (1,600) Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site;

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to the County Council.

The Owners shall pay the Second Primary School Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**2.1** £8,570,000 (Eight Million Five Hundred and Seventy Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on or before Occupation of more than Three Thousand (3,000) Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to the County Council.

The Owners shall pay the Primary School Revenue Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**3.1** £50,000 (Fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date 6 (six) months prior to the Opening Date of the First Primary School Building (as notified by the County Council).

**3.2** £50,000 (Fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date 6 (six) months prior to the Opening Date of the Second Primary School Building (as notified by the County Council).

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date specified herein until such payment is made.

The Owners shall pay the Secondary School Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**4.1** £10,570,000 (Ten Million Five Hundred and Seventy Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on or before Occupation of more than two thousand five hundred (2,500) Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to the County Council.

The Owners shall pay the Post 16 Provision Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**5.1** £3,640,000 (Three Million Six Hundred and Forty Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on or before Occupation of more than four thousand two hundred (4,200) Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to the County Council.

The Owners shall pay the Special School Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**6.1** £2,760,120 (Two Million Seven Hundred and Sixty Thousand One Hundred and Twenty British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on or before Occupation of more than two thousand five hundred (2,500) Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to the County Council.

The Owners shall pay the Special School Revenue Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**7.1** £29,172 (Twenty Nine Thousand One Hundred and Seventy Two British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date 6 (six) months prior to the Opening Date of the Special School Building (as notified by the County Council).

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation of any part of the Site not yet Occupied by the date specified herein until such payment is made.

### Part 4 — Campus Site Facilities Works

Subject to the County Council having received the relevant part of monies due under this Schedule or under Schedule 18 the County Council will use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus Site to ensure that at the date of the opening to pupils of each relevant part of the Campus Site those parts of the Community Facilities which are appropriate to the relevant part of the Campus Site are completed such they are fit for purpose and ready and available and where the County Council having used reasonable endeavours as aforesaid has been unable to complete or procure completion by such time then the County Council shall remain under an on-going obligation to use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus Site as soon as reasonably practical thereafter Provided That if the Campus Site is operational and open prior to the trigger for the relevant part of the monies due under this Schedule or under Schedule 18 the County Council will use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus as soon as reasonably practical.

Where any School Promoter takes a legal interest in part of the Campus Site they shall as a successor in title to the County Council (and even though the County Council may retain a legal interest in all or part of the Campus Site) share an obligation pursuant to the interest of the School Promoter to likewise use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus Site to ensure that at the date of the opening to pupils of each relevant part of the Campus Site those parts of the Community Facilities which are appropriate to the relevant part of the Campus Site are completed such they are fit for purpose and ready and available and where the School Promoter having used reasonable endeavours as aforesaid has been unable to complete or procure completion by such time then the School Promoter shall remain under an on-going obligation to use reasonable endeavours to procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus Site as soon as reasonably practical thereafter Provided That if the Campus Site is operational and open prior to the trigger for the relevant part of the monies due under this Schedule or under Schedule 18 the County Council will use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus as soon as reasonably practical.

The County Council will as soon as reasonably practical following completion of the Campus Site Transfer enter into a Community Facilities Principles Agreement in the form set out at Appendix 22 or in such other form as shall be agreed in writing by SCDC.

The County Council will procure that each relevant School Promoter which is relevant to the Campus Site will prior to taking possession of that part of the Campus Site which is appropriate to them first of all enter into a Community Facilities Principles Agreement.

The County Council and any relevant School Promoter shall use reasonable endeavours to use any part of the Indoor Sports Building Revenue Contribution received by any one of them to ensure that such Contribution is used in the best and most efficient way to give priority to the community use of the Community Facilities.

### Part 5 — Establishment of the Project Core Team

The County Council and HCA shall as soon as reasonably practicable following the date of this Deed establish the Project Core Team PROVIDED THAT The County Council shall not be permitted to undertake any work actions decisions or otherwise as are described as being within the Project Core Team Terms of Reference.

The County Council shall invite HCA or its nominee to all Project Core Team meetings that are convened whether such meetings are held in person or by telephone or videoconferencing and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT this provision shall also apply to the County Council and the School Promoter.

The County Council covenants to convene Project Core Team meetings at least once every 4 weeks.

The Project Core Team Terms of Reference shall be as set out in Part 6 of this Schedule.

The Project Core Team meetings will continue to operate from the date of establishment until Occupation of the Second Primary School.

The County Council shall consider, take into account, incorporate and action any proposals suggestions recommendations or comments made by the HCA whether the HCA make them in the Project Core Team meetings or writing served on the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt the Project Core Team shall approve any actions variations amendments documentation and information before the County Council and/or the contractor appointed to construct the Primary School Building take any such action, make such variation or amendment or finalise documentation or information or otherwise expend any monies related to tasks and stages referred to in the definition of Project Core Team in this Agreement.

### Part 6 — Project Core Team Terms of Reference

**1** Purpose

The County Council and HCA have agreed to establish, as soon as reasonably practicable and in advance of any of the work, actions, decisions or otherwise as being within the Terms of Reference referred to below being undertaken or made, a Project Core Team of relevant qualified persons to deliver the actions necessary to deliver in the design and construction of the School Building. The purpose of the Project Core Team is to oversee the design and construction of the School Building and to ensure that the County Council, HCA, and the School Promoter act collaboratively on the decisions that have to be made in delivery of the School Building.

**2** Representation

**2.1** Initially, the Project Core Team shall consist of the following:

**2.1.1** Cambridgeshire County Council

**2.1.2** HCA

**2.1.3** School Promoters

**2.1.4** SCDC (as relates to the Community Facilities)

**2.2** New Project Team members may be added as the needs of the Project requires by mutual agreement between the parties. The Project Core Team will meet every month unless decided otherwise by the parties on the Project Core Team.

**3** Terms of Reference

**3.4** Approvals by the Project Core Team shall unless stated otherwise be decisions approved/agreed by a majority of the parties on the Project Core Team.

The terms of reference of the Project Core Team will be as follows:

**3.1** to approve the design brief for the School Buildings to the County Council;

**3.2** to approve any changes to the design brief;

**3.3** to approve the Detailed Primary School or Secondary School Building Work Specification;

**3.4** to approve the timetable for the design, procurement and construction of the School Buildings and anticipated opening dates;

**3.5** to approve the scheme design (up to Stage 5 of the RIBA Plan of Work) for the School Buildings;

**3.6** to approve the employer's requirements for the School Building. To approve any significant changes to the Stages 0 to 5 of the RIBA Plan of Work design proposed by the Contractor;

**3.7** to receive/review monthly update reports on progress provided by the School project manager/contractor;

**3.8** in order to secure the Reserved Matters Approval or other form of planning approval for the School Building;

**3.9** to approve detailed specifications for any parts of the Community Facilities.

### Part 7 — Indoor Sports Provision and MUGA

**1** The Owners shall pay the Indoor Sports Building Contribution to the County Council on or before Occupation of more than two thousand five hundred (2,500) Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the Indoor Sports Building Contribution has been paid to the County Council.

**2** SCDC shall pay £32,000 (thirty-two thousand pounds) to the County Council within 14 days of receipt of a notice that the County Council will within the next 14 days contract with a third party for the construction of the MUGA such sum to be applied by the County Council towards the construction of the MUGA on the Campus Site.

## Schedule 3 — Not used

## Schedule 4 — Sustainable Urban Drainage System

Relevant Definitions:

**"Expert"** means an independent and suitable person holding appropriate professional qualifications and of at least 5 years post qualification drainage experience appointed by the Owners at its own cost to issue the Expert Completion Certificate and the Expert Final Certificate.

**"Expert Completion Certificate"** means the report or certificate issued by the Expert certifying that the On Site SUDS have been completed in accordance with the On Site SUDS Specification and details submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 19 to the Planning Permission and the On Site Maintenance Period may commence.

**"Expert Final Certificate"** means a report or certificate issued by the Expert confirming that all works to which the certificate relates have been maintained to its reasonable satisfaction during the On Site SUDS Maintenance Period and that no defects have arisen during the said Maintenance Period which have not been remedied to the reasonable satisfaction of the Expert.

**"On-Site SUDS"** means surface water drainage measures/features which will be located within the Development and which shall include the following to serve the Development:

   - **(a)** the ponds which are to be contained within the maximum designed retained water level and which are shown indicatively on the On Site SUDs Plan and a surface water pumping station;
   - **(b)** swales watercourses and ditches;
   - **(c)** ponds and infiltration basins;
   - **(d)** soakaways; and
   - **(e)** all associated inlet/outlet structures, control devices, headwalls silt traps catchpits culverts and manholes

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT for the purposes of the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer(s) the On-Site SUDS shall (i) exclude any surface water drainage measures/features located wholly within and only serving the adopted highway and (ii) wholly within and only serving particular open space.

**"On-Site SUDS Contribution"** means:

   - **(a)** in the event that the On-Site SUDS are to be transferred to the Councils or a nominee of the Councils the maintenance sum agreed between the Councils and the HCA prior to the date of this Agreement to be paid to the relevant transferee; and
   - **(b)** in the event that the On-Site SUDS are to be transferred to the Alternative Body such sum as is agreed between the parties by reference to any statutory or local guidance in place at the time of the transfer to be paid to the Alternative Body

to be paid by the Owners towards the cost of the management and maintenance of the On-Site SUDS and replacement (as necessary) of the pump for the on-site balancing pond.

**"On-Site SUDS Plan"** means the plan appended to this Agreement and marked Plan 6 at Appendix 23.

**"On-Site SUDS Land Transfer"** means the transfer of the:

   - **(a)** unencumbered freehold interest in the land in the event that the transfer is to the Councils or a nominee of the Councils or;
   - **(b)** unencumbered freehold or leasehold interest in the land in the event that the transfer is to the Alternative Body

in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 12 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the On-Site SUDS and any Incidental Open Space by the Owners to SCDC or the Alternative Body and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer which shall nevertheless:

   - **(i)** not include any terms which would restrict public access unless for health and safety grounds; and
   - **(ii)** not include any terms which would adversely and whether directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
   - **(iii)** include a reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the On-Site SUDS for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraphs 3.6 and 3.7 of Part 1 of this Schedule; and
   - **(iv)** include the grant of any rights reasonably necessary for the proposed end use of any adjoining land;
   - **(v)** include for the benefit of the On-Site SUDS the grant of any rights of access over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the management and maintenance of the On-Site SUDS for its purpose for public open space, ecological habitat creation, water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes;
   - **(vi)** be at nil consideration; and
   - **(vii)** the transfer shall include a covenant that the On-Site SUDS shall not be used for any purpose other than for public open space, ecological habitat creation, water attenuation and/or surface water and ancillary drainage or ancillary or similar purposes associated with the Development.

**"On Site SUDS Maintenance Period"** means:

   - **(a)** in the event the On Site SUDS are to be transferred to:
      - **(i)** the Councils or a nominee of the Councils; or
      - **(ii)** an Alternative Body not being a statutory water undertaker that is legally permitted to adopt own or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features and measures; or
      - **(iii)** a Management Company; or
      - **(iv)** if the Owners retain the On Site SUDS which are then maintained by a Management Company

the period shall be up to 24 months from the date of the relevant Expert Completion Certificate or such shorter period as a statutory undertaker that is legally permitted to adopt or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features may reasonably require whether or not the On-Site SUDS are transferred to that statutory undertaker;

**"On Site SUDS Specification"** means a detailed specification to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners and submitted to SCDC pursuant to Planning Condition 19.

### Part 1 — On-Site SUDS

**1** The Owners shall lay out construct and complete the On-Site SUDS within the Site in accordance with details submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 19 appended to the Planning Permission.

**2** No later than Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall offer the On-Site SUDS pursuant to the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT it shall notify SCDC of the same and such notification shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body as a suitable entity or organisation for the purposes of the management and maintenance of the On-Site SUDS SAVE THAT SCDC shall not be entitled to refuse to approve the Alternative Body if the Alternative Body proposed by the Owners is a statutory water authority that is legally permitted to adopt own or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features and measures PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the On-Site SUDS to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the On-Site SUDS the actual transfer will be completed when the Expert Final Certificate is issued AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT if the Alternative Body is a statutory water undertaker the actual transfer will be completed as soon as practicable following the completion of the On-Site SUDSs and any On-Site SUDS Maintenance Period as agreed between the Owners and the statutory water undertaker.

**3** Subject to paragraph 2 in the event that the Alternative Body declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the On Site SUDS within 40 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer the Owners shall serve written notice on the Councils for the purposes of paragraph 3.1 below.

**3.1** The written notice shall include an offer to transfer the On-Site SUDs to the Councils or a nominee of the Councils pursuant to the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if either of the Councils or a nominee of the Councils accepts the offer of the On-Site SUDS the actual transfer will be completed by the parties following the issue of the Expert Final Certificate.

**3.2**

**3.2.1** notify SCDC in writing that it intends either to

   - **(a)** transfer the On-Site SUDS to a Management Company; or
   - **(b)** retain the On-Site SUDS and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the On-Site SUDS on the Owners' behalf

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options in paragraph 3.2 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify the Councils that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company.

**3.3** In the event that the Councils or a nominee of the Councils declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it of the On-Site SUDS within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall:

In the event that the Alternative Body accepts the transfer of the On-Site SUDS the Owners shall offer to pay the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the Alternative Body and for the avoidance of doubt there shall thereafter be no contribution payable to SCDC pursuant to this Schedule.

**3.4** In the event the On-Site SUDS are to be managed and maintained by a Management Company then the Owners may elect to transfer the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the Management Company.

**3.5** In the event that the Councils or a nominee of the Councils accepts the transfer of the On-Site SUDS the Owners shall pay the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the Councils or a nominee of the Councils to be used for the maintenance and management of the On-Site SUDS as follows:

**3.5.1** 10% (ten per cent) on transfer of the On-Site SUDS; and

**3.5.2** 30% (thirty per cent) on the fifth anniversary of the transfer of the On-Site SUDS; and

**3.5.3** 30% (thirty per cent) on the tenth anniversary of the transfer of the On-Site SUDS; and

**3.5.4** 30% (thirty per cent) on the fifteenth anniversary of the transfer of the On-Site SUDS.

**3.6** The Owners shall not locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over any part of the On Site SUDS comprising a pond.

**3.7** Subject to paragraph 3.6 above and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners may locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over any other part of the On Site SUDS Land.

### Part 2 — Covenants relating to Laying Out and Certification

**1** In the event the On-Site SUDS are to be transferred to an Alternative Body that is a statutory water authority then the provisions of paragraphs 2 3 and 4 of this Part 2 shall not apply.

**2** The Owners shall:

**2.1** Prior to Commencement of the construction of the On-Site SUDS notify the Councils of the following:

**2.1.1** the date upon which construction of the On-Site SUDS are to commence; and

**2.1.2** the name and details of the Expert who shall be supplying any of the Expert certificates for the purposes of this Schedule;

**2.2** Provide the Councils or a nominee of the Councils with written notification that it intends to supply the Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the On-Site SUDS no less than 20 Working Days before supplying it in accordance with paragraph 2.3 of Part 2 of this Schedule;

**2.3** Supply the Councils or a nominee of the Councils with a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the On-Site SUDS forthwith following Completion of the On-Site SUDS;

**2.4** Serve on the Councils or a nominee of the Councils at the same time as the Expert Completion Certificate is issued a copy of such Certificate certifying that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction;

**2.5** In the event that the Expert is unable to issue the Expert Completion Certificate because there are necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of the Expert as soon as reasonably practicable;

**2.6** Upon completion of any remedial work, to notify in writing the Councils or a nominee of the Councils that they have invited the Expert to inspect the remedial works identified pursuant to paragraph 2.5 of this Part of this Schedule and to issue an Expert Completion Certificate confirming that the On-Site SUDS have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction;

**2.7** Allow the Councils and any person authorised by the Councils upon prior reasonable written notice to enter unto the Site without machinery for the purpose of inspecting the construction of the On-Site SUDS.

**3** Following issue or deemed issue of an Expert Completion Certificate in respect of the On-Site SUDS the Owners shall maintain the On-Site SUDS for the On Site SUDS Maintenance Period to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC.

**4** On expiration of the On Site SUDS Maintenance Period the Owners shall:

**4.1** notify the Councils or a nominee of the Councils that they have invited the Expert to inspect the On-Site SUDS and issue an Expert Final Certificate confirming that such works have been maintained to their reasonable satisfaction;

**4.2** if the Expert inspects the On-Site SUDS and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of the Expert as soon as reasonably practicable;

**4.3** upon completion of any remedial works, to serve notice on the Expert inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 4.2 and issue an Expert Final Certificate confirming that the On-Site SUDS have been maintained to the reasonable satisfaction of the Expert.

**4.4** No later than 20 Working Days following the issue of the Expert Final Certificate by the Expert provide a copy of the Expert Final Certificate to the Councils.

**4.5** procure that the benefits of any and all guarantees or warranties relating to

   - **(a)** the preparation of the On Site SUDs Specification (if not already in place), and
   - **(b)** the issue of the certificates by the Expert under this Schedule

are each assigned to SCDC (and/or if appropriate the County Council) or if relevant the nominee of the Councils or the Alternative Body.

**5** The Owners shall properly maintain the On-Site SUDS or any part thereof in accordance with:

**5.1** the On Site SUDS Specification; and

**5.2** the details submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 19 appended to the Planning Permission; and

**5.3** in any event to ensure that the existing Awards Drains operated by SCDC are not materially adversely affected;

unless and until such time that the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer is completed to the Councils or a nominee of the Councils an Alternative Body or Management Company who shall thereafter become responsible for the maintenance of the On-Site SUDS PROVIDED THAT transfer of the On-Site SUDS may be completed on a phased basis following the issue of Expert Final Certificate in relation to such part of the On-Site SUDS.

## Schedule 5 — Off Site Flood Mitigation Works

**"Drainage Upgrade and Maintenance Contribution"** means the sum of £185,850 payable as a commuted sum which shall be used for the Drainage Works Purposes.

**"Drainage Works Purposes"** means the works for the upgrade and/or maintenance and/or technical assistance of surface water drains in connection with the management and maintenance of (i) the Awards Drains within and adjacent to the Site or other Awards Drains and/or (ii) Off Site Flood Mitigation Works that are in either case impacted by the Development to the extent that management and/or maintenance is reasonably required to them.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st, 2nd and 3rd Works Land"** means the areas shown on Plan 15 edged red and to the south west of Longstanton in which the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Work and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works will be located.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation Works"** means (i) two balancing ponds off Hatton's Road which provide Off Site flood mitigation in accordance with NAAP Policy NS/21 and comprising the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works, the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Work and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works and (ii) works at Dry Drayton and comprising the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st, 2nd and 3rd Works Contribution"** means the sum of £873,500 to be paid by the Owners to SCDC towards the cost of the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works other than the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Contribution"** means the difference (if any) between the cost of undertaking the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works (including the cost of designing the works and the cost of acquiring the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land) and the sum of £400,000 and where there is a positive difference to be paid by the Owners to SCDC towards the cost of the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land Transfer"** means the transfer:

   - **(a)** to SCDC (or the Parish Council of Oakington if so nominated by SCDC and the Parish Council are willing to accept a transfer) of the unencumbered freehold interest in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 13 ('the Council form of Transfer') or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Work Land (or relevant parts thereof as per the arrangements set out in this Schedule where the whole of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land is not to be the subject of a single transfer); or

   - **(b)** to the Alternative Body of the unencumbered freehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works (or relevant parts thereof) and including any associated Incidental Open Space agreed with the Alternative Body as per the arrangements set out in this Schedule where the whole of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land is not to be the subject of a single transfer) and the form of transfer will be agreed between the Owners and the Alternative Body provided that the final form of Transfer shall be approved or deemed approved by SCDC;

and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer which shall nevertheless:

   - **(c)** include reservation of rights of access and services over the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land for the benefit of other land adjacent to the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of Schedule 9 Part 1 paragraphs 6.1.1 and 6.1.2 which shall apply mutatis mutandis; and

   - **(d)** include the grant of any and all rights reasonably necessary for the proposed end use of any adjoining land;

   - **(e)** include for the benefit of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access over any adjoining land that is retained by the Owners which rights are reasonably required for the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land for its purpose for water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes;

   - **(f)** be at nil consideration; and

   - **(g)** the transfer shall include a covenant that the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land shall not be used for any purpose other than for water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes associated with the Development and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works"** means that part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works being the area which forms the "Awards Drains" and which is to be delivered as part of Phase 1 of Northstowe.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works"** means the pond and by-pass channel(s) to be provided as part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works being the area which is contained within the maximum designed retained water level and which is to be delivered as part of Phase 1 of Northstowe.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works"** means the balance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works which are not part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Work and which is to be delivered as part of Phase 1 of Northstowe.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works"** means off site flood mitigation work within Dry Drayton to mitigate current fluvial flood risks, including the effects of climate change, affecting Oakington village by providing a large balancing pond, or balancing ponds, for Oakington Brook with the ponds serving to hold water when the brook is nearing capacity, during times of high rainfall, in order to reduce the risk of overtopping of the channel through Oakington and the precise detail and location of the works will be identified precisely as part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Specification and shall be subject to the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Cost Cap.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Cost Cap"** means the sum of £400,000 such sum to cover the cost of undertaking the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works (including the cost of designing the works and the cost of acquiring the Off Site Flood Mitigation Work Land.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land"** means the area of land on which the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works are situated the location of which is to be identified pursuant to paragraph 1.4 of Part 1 of this Schedule.

**"Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Specification"** means a detailed specification for the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works and which detailed specification shall include:

   - **(a)** Hydraulic calculations;

   - **(b)** Details of control devices and flow control measures;

   - **(c)** Geotechnical design criteria;

   - **(d)** Permeability assessment;

   - **(e)** Proposed landscape treatment;

   - **(f)** Proposals designed to minimise long term maintenance;

   - **(g)** Proposals designed to maximise ecological benefit; and

   - **(h)** Any other design feature(s) as reasonably required to ensure the effective operation of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.

### Part 1 — Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works

The Owners shall:

**1.1** Prior to Occupation of 2,000 Dwellings on Phase 2 to serve notice on SCDC identifying a specific location showing the extent and boundaries of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land and the Owners shall use all reasonable endeavours to agree the location and boundaries of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land with SCDC as soon as reasonably practical thereafter and then having agreed such location and boundaries to enter into a supplementary form of section 106 Agreement in the form attached as part 3 of Appendix B or in such other form as shall be agreed with SCDC whereby such land then becomes bound by the obligations in this Schedule mutatis mutandis;

**1.2** Lay out construct and complete the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works prior to the Occupation of 2,500 Dwellings on Phase 2 or if earlier within 24 months of the substantive commencement of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works and in either case complete in accordance with the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Specification and the details submitted and approved pursuant to any planning permission for the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works;

**1.3** Provide SCDC written notification that it intends to serve a written notice offering SCDC a right of first refusal for SCDC (or the Parish Council of Oakington if so nominated by SCDC and the Parish Council are willing to accept a transfer) to take a transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land and such notification shall be served no less than 60 Working Days before supplying a notice served in accordance with paragraph 1.4 of this Part 1 of this Schedule;

**1.4** Prior to Occupation of 2,500 Dwellings on Phase 2 to serve a written notice offering SCDC a right of first refusal for SCDC (or the Parish Council of Oakington if so nominated by SCDC and the Parish Council are willing to accept a transfer) to take a transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land;

**2** SCDC covenants with the Owners that it will within 20 Working Days of receipt of a notice served pursuant to paragraph 1.4 above either (i) accept the offer referred to in paragraph 1.4 above or (ii) reject/decline such offer PROVIDED THAT SCDC shall not be required to complete the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land Transfer until such time as the Council Final Certificate relevant to all of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works has been issued or deemed to have been issued;

**3** In the event that SCDC or the Parish Council of Oakington (if so nominated by SCDC) declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land within 20 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer then the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC offering the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT in such notification to set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body as a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land SAVE THAT SCDC shall not be entitled to refuse to approve the Alternative Body if the Alternative Body proposed by the Owners is the Environment Agency or a statutory water authority that is legally permitted to adopt own or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features and measures PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land the actual transfer will be completed when the Council Final Certificate is issued;

**4** Where the Alternative Body declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land within 20 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer the Owners shall serve a further written notice on SCDC for SCDC (or the Parish Council of Oakington if so nominated by SCDC and the Parish Council are willing to accept a transfer) again offering to complete a transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land and the provisions of paragraph 2 of this Part 1 of this Schedule shall apply;

**5** Where SCDC and the Parish Council decline the offer referred to in paragraph 4 of this Part 1 of this Schedule ownership and responsibility of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land shall remain with the Owners Provided That SCDC and the Owners acknowledge that arrangements as to potential transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works (including an allowance for the cost and/or a contribution towards the future maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works) may be a matter to be covered in any subsequent agreement entered into pursuant to Section 106 of the 1990 Act for the remaining phase or phases of Northstowe.

**6** In the event that SCDC (or the Parish Council of Oakington if so nominated by SCDC) accepts the transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Land the Owners shall pay the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Contribution to SCDC on completion of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works to be used solely towards maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works provided always that it is accepted by all parties that any such contribution shall not be deemed to satisfy any maintenance contribution which may properly and reasonably be due in relation to the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works under any subsequent agreement entered into pursuant to Section 106 of the 1990 Act for the remaining phase or phases of Northstowe.

**7** Prior to Occupation of 2,000 Dwellings on Phase 2 the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Specification shall be submitted to and approved by or deemed to have been approved by SCDC.

### Part 2 — Covenants relating to Laying Out and Certification

The Owners shall:

**1.1** provide SCDC with written notification that it intends to supply a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works or specified parts thereof no less than 20 Working Days before supplying it in accordance with paragraph 1.2 of this Part 2 of this Schedule; and

**1.2** prior to the Occupation of 2,500 Dwellings on Phase 2 or if earlier within 24 months of the substantive commencement of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works complete or procure completion of all of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works and supply SCDC with a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works provided that this will not prevent the Owners supplying at an earlier time certificates of Practical Completion as to specified parts thereof as soon as reasonably practical following Completion of the relevant part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works; and

**1.3** serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works or specified parts thereof and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction; and

**1.4** if SCDC chooses to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works and identifies necessary remedial work, complete such remedial work at its own cost to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and

**1.5** upon completion of any remedial work, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 1.4 of this Part 2 of this Schedule and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works or specified parts thereof have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction.

PROVIDED THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works within 10 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Completion Certificate within 15 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Completion Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 1.3 to 1.5 of this Part 2 of this Schedule shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Council Completion Certificate or a Council Completion Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to all of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.

**2** Following issue or deemed issue of a Council Completion Certificate in respect of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works the Owners shall in any event and irrespective of the provisions of Part 1 of this Schedule including in particular paragraphs 1.3, 1.4, 3, 4, 5 and 6 of the said Part 1 maintain the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works for a maintenance period of 2 years to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC.

**3** On expiration of the Maintenance Period for the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works the Owners shall:

**3.1** serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the relevant parts of Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works specified in the notice and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that such works have been maintained during such period to their reasonable satisfaction;

**3.2** if SCDC chooses to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works specified in the notice and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable;

**3.3** upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 3.2 of this Part 2 of this Schedule and issue a Council Certificate confirming that the relevant part of Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works have been maintained to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC up to that date PROVIDED THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works within 40 Working Days of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in Schedule 5, Part 2 paragraph 3.1 to paragraph 3.3 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Council Certificate or a Council Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued in relation to the relevant parts of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works.

**4** The Owners' obligation to maintain the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works in accordance with this Part 2 of this Schedule shall be to maintain in accordance with:

**4.1** the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th Works Specification; and

**4.2** the details submitted and approved pursuant to the planning permission for the Off Site Flood Mitigation 4th; and

**4.3** in any event to ensure that the existing Awards Drains operated by SCDC are not materially adversely affected.

### Part 3 — Drainage Upgrade and Maintenance Contribution and Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st, 2nd and 3rd Works Contribution

**1** At any time after Commencement SCDC may notify the Owners in writing of the works to be undertaken for the Drainage Works Purposes and the Owners shall pay the Drainage Upgrade and Maintenance Contribution on the later of the date being 90 days from receipt of service of the said notice on the Owners or Occupation of any Dwelling and such contribution is to be used by SCDC for the Drainage Works Purposes only.

**2** At any time after Commencement SCDC may notify the Owners in writing calling for payment of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st, 2nd and 3rd Works Contribution and the Owners shall pay the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st, 2nd and 3rd Works Contribution on the later of the date being 90 days from receipt of service of the said notice on the Owners or Occupation of any Dwelling.

## Schedule 6 — Transport including the Cambridgeshire Guided Busway

Relevant definitions:

**"Annual Transport Monitoring Contribution"** means the initial payment of a sum of £20,000 (twenty thousand British pounds) to be paid by the Owners on Commencement and thereafter an annual payment of a sum of £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) on ten occasions as provided for in this Schedule to the County Council on an annual basis for monitoring the transport effects of the Development through:

   - **(a)** Installing new and/or replacing existing traffic counters
   - **(b)** downloading traffic count data from the proposed permanent traffic counters, and
   - **(c)** undertaking infill traffic counts at other sites; and
   - **(d)** other forms of traffic surveys if agreed at the Northstowe Transport Working Group;

**"Capital Transport Monitoring Measures Contribution"** means the sum of £1,750,000 (one million seven hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid in instalments as provided for in this Schedule by the Owners to the County Council for investment into capital works and equipment to facilitate additional traffic monitoring, traffic calming, traffic management and highway improvement measures for the Transport Monitoring Measures Area;

**"CGB Capital Funding Contribution"** means the sum of £6,257,000 (six million two hundred and fifty seven thousand British pounds) to be paid in instalments by the Owners to the County Council towards the cost of implementing the Cambridgeshire Guided Busway scheme and for no other purpose;

**"Cambridgeshire Guided Busway"** means the public transport scheme which forms part of the transport corridor consisting of road improvements bus lanes and bus priority lanes of which 16 miles (25 km) is dedicated for the use of the Guided Busway. The Guided Busway connects the population centres of Cambridge Huntingdon and St. Ives in Cambridgeshire;

**"Commencement of Town Centre"** means the Commencement of any subphase within the Town Centre

**"Community Transport Contribution"** means a total sum of £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid in instalments by the Owners to the County Council as revenue and capital support for the Community Transport Service;

**"Community Transport Service"** means a service or network of transport services capable of providing essential journeys for those who have difficulty accessing conventional public transport. These shall include (but are not limited to) Dial-a-Ride services, voluntary car schemes and community bus schemes;

**"Cycleway Network Construction Contribution"** means the sum of £412,500 (four hundred and twelve thousand and five hundred British pounds) towards the cost of constructing the Cycleway Works;

**"Cycleway Network Design Contribution"** means the sum of £37,500 (thirty seven thousand five hundred British pounds) towards the cost of designing the Cycleway Works;

**"Cycleway Works"** means work to improve the cycleway from Oakington to Girton to be provided by the County Council;

**"Parking Management/Traffic Regulation Orders Contribution"** means the sum of £50,000 (fifty thousand British Pounds) to be paid by the Owners to the County Council towards the cost of consultation, design and implementing the necessary parking management and/or traffic regulation orders within the Development;

**"Parking Management Monitoring Survey"** means a survey to monitor on-street parking within the Town Centre and nearby neighbourhoods to the Town Centre using a methodology to be agreed with the County Council;

**"Parking Management Scheme"** means measures to control parking in the Town Centre and nearby neighbourhoods to the Town Centre using the Parking Management/Traffic Regulation Orders Contribution;

**"Town Centre"** means the area shown coloured red and green and red hatching on the land use parameter plan;

**"Transport Monitoring Measures Area"** means the area shown edged red on the plan attached to this Agreement marked Plan 10 in Appendix 23;

### 1. CGB CAPITAL FUNDING CONTRIBUTION

**1.1** The Owners shall pay the CGB Capital Funding Contribution to the County Council by way of the following instalments:

**1.1.1** £1,042,833 (one million and forty two thousand eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to first Occupation of the 200th Dwelling;

**1.1.2** £1,042,833 (one million and forty two thousand eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to Occupation of the 700th Dwelling; and

**1.1.3** £1,042,833 (one million and forty two thousand eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to Occupation of the 1400th Dwelling.

**1.1.4** £1,042,833 (one million and forty two thousand eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to Occupation of the 2,100th Dwelling

**1.1.5** £1,042,833 (one million and forty two thousand eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to Occupation of the 2,800th Dwelling

**1.1.6** The balance of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to Occupation of the 3,300th Dwelling

### 2. COMMUNITY TRANSPORT CONTRIBUTION

**2.1** The Owners shall pay the Community Transport Contribution to the County Council for the purposes of providing the Community Transport Service as follows:

**2.1.1** £40,000 (forty thousand British pounds) prior to Occupation of the 300th Dwelling;

**2.1.2** £21,000 (twenty one thousand British pounds) on the first anniversary and subsequent anniversaries of the date of Occupation of the 300th Dwelling with the final instalment of £21,000 (twenty one thousand British pounds) falling due on the tenth anniversary of the date of Occupation of the 300th Dwelling.

### 3. PARKING MANAGEMENT/TRAFFIC REGULATION ORDERS CONTRIBUTION

**3.1** If at any time during the period of 15 years from Commencement of the Town Centre the County Council reasonably determines that a Parking Management Scheme for the Town Centre is required then the County Council will notify the Owners in writing of such requirement and

**3.1.1** The Owners shall pay the County Council the Parking Management/Traffic Regulation Order Contribution within 90 days of the date of notification set out in paragraph 3.1 above; or

**3.1.2** If the need for a Parking Management Scheme is disputed the Owners shall submit to the County Council for written approval the methodology for undertaking the Parking Management Monitoring Survey and thereafter undertake a Parking Management Monitoring Survey at its own cost using the approved methodology and provide a copy of the survey report to the Councils within three months of its completion. If the said report determines that a Parking Management Scheme for the Town Centre is required then the County Council will notify the Owners in writing of such requirement and Owners shall pay the Parking Management/Traffic Regulation Order Contribution within 90 working days of the date of such notification

**3.2** If following the results of the Parking Management Monitoring Survey the Owners and the County Council remain in dispute as to the need or absence of need for a Parking Management Scheme for the Town Centre that dispute shall be referable to an expert in accordance with Clause 17.

### 4. CAPITAL TRANSPORT MONITORING MEASURES CONTRIBUTION

**4.1** Subject to paragraphs 4.2 and 4.3 the Owners shall pay the Capital Transport Monitoring Measures Contribution to the County Council as follows:

**4.1.1** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 500th Dwelling;

**4.1.2** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 1,000th Dwelling;

**4.1.3** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 1,500th Dwelling;

**4.1.4** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 2,000th Dwelling

**4.1.5** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 2,500th Dwelling

**4.1.6** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 3,000th Dwelling

**4.1.7** £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 3,400th Dwelling

**4.2** The payments referred to in paragraphs 4.1.1, 4.1.2, 4.1.3, 4.1.4, 4.1.5, 4.1.6 and 4.1.7 shall only fall due if the County Council shall have first provided written evidence to the Owners that:

**4.2.1** the proposed mitigation works are directly related to the relevant phase of the Northstowe development within the Transport Monitoring Measures Area; and

**4.2.2** it has obtained all appropriate consents permissions and authorisations required for the implementation of the proposed mitigation work.

**4.3** In the event the County Council shall not have provided the evidence referred to in paragraph 4.2 above by the respective due dates the Owners shall not be obliged to make the payments referred to in paragraph 4.1 until such time as the said evidence has been provided and on receipt of such evidence the Owners shall pay the relevant contribution referred to in paragraph 4.1 no later than 90 Days after receipt of the said evidence.

**4.4** In the event the County Council provides the evidence referred to in paragraph 4.2.1 above, the Owners shall pay to the County Council no later than 90 Days after receipt of the said evidence such sum as is required to enable advanced work to determine potential scheme such sum not exceeding £25,000 per scheme development AND FURTHER PROVIDED THAT any sum paid to the County Council by the Owners under this paragraph shall be deducted from the next relevant payment referred to in paragraph 4.1.

### 5. ANNUAL TRANSPORT MONITORING CONTRIBUTION

**5.1** The Owners shall pay to the County Council:

**5.1.1** £20,000 (twenty thousand British pounds) prior to Occupation of the first Dwelling; and

**5.1.2** £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) on the first anniversary and subsequent anniversaries of the date of Occupation of the first Dwelling with the final instalment of £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) falling due on the tenth anniversary of the date of Occupation of the first Dwelling.

### 6. CYCLEWAY NETWORK DESIGN/CONSTRUCTION CONTRIBUTION

**6.1** The Owners shall pay the Cycleway Network Design Contribution to the County Council on Commencement;

**6.2** The Owners shall pay the Cycleway Network Construction Contribution to the County Council no later than 90 Days after the County Council have provided written evidence to the Owners:

**6.2.1** of the details of the proposed scheme; and

**6.2.2** it has obtained all appropriate consents permissions and authorisations required for the implementation of the proposed scheme

Provided That the County Council shall not be entitled to submit such evidence to the Owners prior to Commencement.

## Schedule 7 — Affordable Housing

### Relevant Definitions

In this Schedule the following words and phrases shall have the meanings ascribed to them:

**"Affordable Housing"** means (i) the Starter Homes and (ii) Affordable Rented Housing as defined in Annex 2 of the National Planning Policy Framework, or in either case as otherwise defined by other legislation or as agreed by SCDC;

**"Affordable Housing Scheme"** means a scheme to be submitted for each Development Parcel in which Affordable Rented Housing Units and/or Starter Homes are to be located to provide the Affordable Rented Housing Units and the Starter Home Units in the locations as set out in the scheme and which will (where a Development Parcel includes Affordable Rented Housing Units) secure the provision by an Approved Registered Provider and/or SCDC of the Affordable Rented Housing Units such scheme to identify the following for the Development Parcel concerned:

   - **(a)** details of the types, size, number and broad locations of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and the Starter Home Units to be provided and confirming Clustering arrangements and a programme for their phased delivery; and
   - **(b)** setting out the design of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and the Starter Home Units which shall include details which show the Affordable Rented Housing Units and the Starter Home Units designed in a way which is materially indistinguishable in external design and appearance from the Market Housing Units of similar size; and
   - **(c)** whether the mix of Dwellings is consistent in delivering a mix of the Affordable Rented Housing Units across the Development that would reflect the indicative mix as set out in Part 6 of this Schedule 7; and
   - **(d)** such other information as SCDC may reasonably require.

**"Affordable Rented Housing"** means the tenure which is referred to in the National Planning Policy Framework published in March 2012 as offering flexible tenancies to households who are eligible for social rented housing which is not subject to national rent regimes but is subject to other controls that require a rent of no more than 80% of the gross local Market Rent or on such terms as Government policy determines and which shall be offered for rent by a Registered Provider to persons who are otherwise unable to secure private sector housing for purchase or rent in the prevailing economic circumstances and who have in the opinion of SCDC need for such housing as demonstrated by their position on SCDC's Lettings Policy register or equivalent list provided always that in any event the rental levels of any individual Affordable Housing Rented Unit shall not exceed the Local Housing Allowance Level (or any equivalent benefit level) for that Dwelling unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC or unless Government Policy dictates;

**"Affordable Rented Housing Building Lease"** means a lease of an Affordable Rented Housing Site on the HCA's then current standard terms (or such other terms as may be agreed between the HCA and the Nominated Registered Provider or where applicable SCDC) which:

   - **(a)** provides for the construction of Affordable Rented Housing Units on the Affordable Rented Housing Site in accordance with the relevant Reserved Matters Approval;
   - **(b)** provides that following the physical completion of the individual Affordable Rented Housing Units in accordance with the relevant Reserved Matters Approval to the HCA's satisfaction and the discharge of any other relevant pre-conditions including compliance with the HCA's quality standards the transfer of the freehold to Nominated Registered Provider;
   - **(c)** be otherwise on such terms as may be negotiated with the Registered Provider or SCDC; and
   - **(d)** shall be at such consideration as reflects the then market value for affordable housing (having regards to the terms of this Agreement in relation to the delivery, letting, nominations and management) Provided always that the Owners have provided evidence to SCDC of the market value which might reasonably be expected to be achieved having regard to the above.

**"Affordable Rented Housing Cap"** means Affordable Rented Housing of 10% of the total number of Dwellings comprised in the Development with a tenure split of 100% Affordable Rented Units unless otherwise agreed by SCDC (in its absolute discretion);

**"Affordable Rented Housing Indicative Mix"** means the indicative mix of dwelling types specified in Part 6 of this Schedule 7;

**"Affordable Rented Housing Site"** means the part or parts of the Site on which the Affordable Rented Housing is to be provided and each relevant Affordable Rented Housing Site shall be sufficient in extent for the erection of the Affordable Rented Housing Units including (without prejudice to the generality of the foregoing) adequate car parking spaces walkways gardens and other normal domestic facilities and with services to include the provision of vehicular access, drains, sewers, electricity, gas and telecommunication infrastructure provided that such services may be put in place after the grant of the Affordable Rented Housing Building Licence or the date of transfer of the Affordable Rented Housing Site to a Nominated Registered Provider or SCDC as appropriate pursuant to the provisions of Part 2 (Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer) of this Schedule and "Affordable Rented Housing Sites" shall be construed accordingly;

**"Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer"** means a transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered freehold interest in any part of an Affordable Rented Housing Site and each part shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer on terms to be agreed but which shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict use of the land for Affordable Rented Housing and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and
   - **(b)** not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
   - **(c)** subject to the provisions of paragraph 10 in Part 1 of this Schedule 7 include the reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Affordable Rented Housing Site for the benefit of any other part of the Site where such rights are reserved for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity; and
   - **(d)** include for the benefit of the Affordable Rented Housing Site the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the enjoyment, management and maintenance of the Affordable Rented Housing Site;
   - **(e)** include a covenant that the Affordable Rented Housing Site shall not subject to the provisions set out in clause 11 of this Agreement mutatis mutandis be used for any purpose other than for use for Affordable Rented Housing;
   - **(f)** include a requirement the Affordable Rented Housing Site shall be remediated so that the land is fit for residential use;
   - **(g)** be otherwise on such terms as may be negotiated with the Registered Provider or SCDC; and
   - **(h)** be at such consideration as reflects the then market value for affordable housing (having regards to the terms of this Agreement in relation to the delivery, letting, nominations and management) Provided always that the Owners have provided evidence to SCDC of the market value which might reasonably be expected to be achieved having regard to the above.

**"Affordable Rented Housing Units"** means those Dwellings which are identified for Affordable Rented Housing purposes and which shall comprise 10% (ten percent) of the total number of Dwellings across the whole of the Development and which shall meet the requirement for Clustering and for the avoidance of doubt nothing in this Schedule shall require the Owners to provide Affordable Housing in each Development Parcel but subject always to the provision that the Affordable Housing Units shall not be back loaded to late phases of the Development;

**"Agreement for an Affordable Rented Housing Building Lease"** means an agreement to grant an Affordable Rented Housing building lease on the HCA's standard terms or such other terms as may be agreed between the HCA and the Nominated Registered Provider or where applicable SCDC;

**"Approved Affordable Housing Scheme"** means the Affordable Housing Scheme approved or deemed approved pursuant to the provisions of paragraph 3.1 of Part 1 of this Schedule 7;

**"Chargee"** means any mortgagee or chargee of the Registered Provider or the successors in title to such mortgagee or charge or any receiver or manager (including an administrative receiver) appointed pursuant to the Law of Property Act 1925;

**"Clustering"** means that the Affordable Rented Housing Units shall (unless otherwise agreed with SCDC) be constructed in clusters throughout the Development of a minimum of 6 and a maximum of 25 Affordable Rented Housing Units within each Affordable Rented Housing Site and Provided always that there shall be no adjoining of Affordable Rented Housing Sites;

**"Discount"** means the percentage discount to be applied to the Open Market Value of a Starter Home Unit which must:

   - **(a)** be a minimum of 20% (twenty percent); and
   - **(b)** on first Disposal be such other percentage discount to ensure that a Starter Home Unit is not sold at a price which exceeds the Price Cap

and which for each Starter Home Unit the percentage will be fixed on the first Disposal such percentage discount to then be applied to any subsequent Disposal during the Restricted Period Provided That should the Starter Home Regulations provide for any tapering or reduction of the discount amount during the Restricted Period the percentage discount shall be tapered or reduced in line with the Starter Home Regulations.

**"Discounted Price"** means the price calculated by applying the Discount to the Open Market Value of the relevant Starter Home Unit.

**"Disposal"**, **"Dispose"** and **"Disposed"** means the sale of the freehold or the grant of a lease or underlease of 21 (twenty one) years or more or the assignment of a lease or underlease with more than 21 (twenty one) years left to run of the Starter Home Units (or Unit as the case may be) and for the sake of clarity the term "Disposal", "Dispose" and "Disposed" shall not include the creation of mortgages or charges.

**"First Time Buyer"** has the meaning given by section 57AA(2) of the Finance Act 2003 and which means a person who:

   - **(a)** has not previously been a purchaser in relation to Relevant Acquisition which consisted of or included residential property;
   - **(b)** has not previously acquired an equivalent interest in such land under the law of Scotland or a territory outside the United Kingdom;
   - **(c)** has not previously been, or been one of the persons who was, "the person" for the purposes of section 71A, or 73 of the Finance Act 2003 in a case where the first transaction within the meaning of the section concerned was a Relevant Acquisition which consisted of or included residential property, and
   - **(d)** would not have been such a person for those purposes in such a case if the provisions mentioned in paragraph (c) had been in force, and had had effect in the territory concerned, at all material times (subject, where required, to appropriate modifications)

or such other definition as may be prescribed by the Starter Home Regulations.

**"Lettings Policy"** means SCDC's housing lettings policy incorporating a choice based lettings policy being a statutory allocations scheme which all housing authorities must adopt by 2010 in order to offer social housing tenants a choice of the opportunity to express preferences about the accommodation or accommodation to be allocated to them and which may also incorporate a local lettings policy being a policy addressing allocations at a more local level a copy of which is attached at Appendix 14;

**"Market Housing Units"** means those units forming part of the Development which are general market housing for sale on the open market and are not Starter Home Units or Affordable Rented Units;

**"Market Rent"** means market rent as defined in the latest edition of the RICS Red Book reflecting the properties' size and location as verified by a valuation in accordance with the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors Valuation and Appraisal Standards (Fifth Edition as updated and amended from time to time) certified by a member of the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors;

**"Nominated Registered Provider"** means a Registered Provider of Social Housing as nominated by the Owners and (where the Owners are not the HCA or the HCA ceases to be the regulator of Registered Providers of Social Housing) approved or deemed approved by SCDC in accordance with the provisions of clause 18, such approval not to be unreasonably withheld or delayed) which provider has been registered under section 80 of the Housing and Regeneration Act 2008 with the HCA or other entity providing Affordable Housing under equivalent arrangements;

**"Price Cap"** means the sum of £250,000 (two hundred and fifty thousand pounds) unless varied by the Starter Home Regulations.

**"Qualifying First Time Buyer"** means a purchaser who at the date of the relevant Disposal of a Starter Home Unit will be:

   - **(a)** a First Time Buyer;
   - **(b)** is at least 23 years old but has not yet reached the age of 40 (forty); and
   - **(c)** occupying the Starter Home Unit as their only or main residence
   - **(d)** meets any other criteria specified in regulations made by the Secretary of State (for example, relating to nationality)

PROVIDED THAT where there is more than one purchaser, each of them must comply with (a) (c) and (d) above but only one need comply with (b) above and Provided Further that what is meant by Qualifying First Time Buyer from time to time shall be consistent with the Starter Home Regulations.

**"Registered Provider"** means a Registered Provider of Social Housing registered under Section 80 of the Housing and Regeneration Act 2008 or other entity providing Affordable Housing under equivalent arrangements who is approved or accredited by the HCA;

**"Relevant Acquisition"** means an acquisition of a major interest in land other than:

   - **(a)** the grant of a lease for a term of less than 21 (twenty one) years, or
   - **(b)** the assignment of a lease which has less than 21 (twenty one) years to run.

**"Restricted Period"** means the period of 5 (five) years from the date of the first Disposal of the Starter Home Unit as a dwelling or such other period as the Secretary of State may prescribe in the Starter Home Regulations.

**"Starter Home Open Market Value"** means the best price in line with published RICS Guidance at which a Disposal of a Starter Home Unit might reasonably be expected to have been completed unconditionally for cash consideration on the date of valuation assuming:

   - **(a)** a willing seller and buyer;
   - **(b)** that prior to the date of the valuation there has been a reasonable period (having regard to the nature of the Starter Home Unit and the state of the market) for the proper marketing of the interest for the agreement of price and terms and for the completion of the Disposal;
   - **(c)** that no account is taken of an additional bid by a purchaser with a special interest;
   - **(d)** that any restrictions imposed upon the Starter Home Unit in or by reason of this Deed are disregarded; and
   - **(e)** the transaction is an arm's length transaction, after prior marketing and where the parties had each acted knowledgeable, prudently and without compulsion as defined in the RICS Valuation - Professional Standards January 2014.

**"Starter Home Regulations"** means regulations (if any) made by the Secretary of State pursuant to the Housing and Planning Act 2016.

**"Starter Home Units"** means 40% (forty per cent) of the total number of dwellings to be provided as part of the Development as Starter Homes the details and location of which shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC pursuant to Affordable Housing Scheme(s) Reserved Matters Application(s) and approval and "Starter Home Unit" shall be construed accordingly.
