**"Starter Homes"** means subject to Part 9 of this Schedule a new dwelling to be made available for purchase by a Qualifying First Time Buyer either as a freehold or leasehold interest which subject to paragraph 1.2 of Part 3 of this Schedule 7 is not to be Disposed other than in accordance with the terms of Part 3 of Schedule 7.

**"Sub-let"** means the grant of a lease or other arrangement (including an assured shorthold tenancy) for exclusive possession of the whole of a Starter Home Unit for any period of time to a person who is not a Qualifying First Time Buyer.

**"SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer"** means the offer to be made by the Owners to SCDC in accordance with paragraph 1.1 of Part 2 of this Schedule 7.

**"Transferee"** means the person or persons to whom a Starter Home Unit is Disposed in accordance with the provisions of this Schedule.

### Part 1 — Affordable Housing Scheme

The Owners covenant with SCDC:

**1.1** that each of the Affordable Rented Housing Units shall only be used for the purposes of Affordable Rented Housing in accordance with this Schedule save where provided otherwise in this Schedule or in Clause 11; and

**1.2** to provide the Starter Home Units within the Development in accordance with the Planning Permission.

**1.3** to provide accelerated physical delivery of the Affordable Rented Housing Units so that 20% of the total Dwellings on each Development Parcel (unless another level is agreed with SCDC in its absolute discretion in respect of one or more Development Parcels) are Affordable Rented Housing Units until a total of 200 Affordable Rented Housing Units are achieved on the Development.

**1.4** Following the provision of the first 200 Affordable Rented Housing Units (or earlier by agreement by SCDC) pursuant to paragraph 1.3 above, the Owners and SCDC shall meet in good faith and acting reasonably to consider the pace of delivery of the remaining Affordable Rented Housing Units as part of the Development in accordance with this Schedule 7.

Prior to or concurrent with the submission of any Reserved Matters Application for each Development Parcel the Owners shall either:

**2.1** in the event that the Development Parcel is to include Affordable Rented Housing Units or Starter Home Units submit in writing to SCDC the Affordable Housing Scheme for its approval pursuant to Clause 18 (but excluding deemed approval); or

**2.2** without prejudice to paragraphs 1.3 above and 4 below, the Owners shall subject as hereinafter provided notify SCDC in writing that the Development Parcel shall not include any Affordable Rented Housing Units or Starter Home Units PROVIDED ALWAYS that the number of Affordable Rented Housing Units to be delivered as a proportion of the number of Dwellings provided accords with the table below:

| No. of Dwellings | No. Affordable Rented Housing Units |
|-----------------|-------------------------------------|
| 50 | 10 |
| 125 | 25 |
| 250 | 50 |
| 375 | 75 |
| 500 | 100 |
| 750 | 150 |
| 1000 | 200 |

**2.3** In producing an Affordable Housing Scheme, the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to limit the extent of common facilities (including private unadopted roads and accesses) the maintenance of which is funded by a service charge levied on the owners or occupiers of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that:

   - **(a)** any adoptable sewers serving the Affordable Rented Housing Site are subject to agreements under Section 104 of the Water Industry Act 1991 as soon as reasonably practicable; and
   - **(b)** any adoptable roads serving the Affordable Rented Housing Site are subject to agreements under section 38 of the Highways Act 1980 as soon as reasonably practicable.

**2.4** Any service charge payable in respect of the Affordable Rented Housing Units shall be kept to the minimum consistent with the limitations imposed by the Housing Act 1985 and (if required by SCDC) the Affordable Housing Scheme shall include proposals by the Owners to ensure that the service charge does not exceed levels which may prevent the Affordable Rented Housing Units qualifying as affordable under any guidelines or criteria of the Homes and Communities Agency or the Tenant Services Agency (or their respective successors) current at the date when the Affordable Rented Housing Scheme is being submitted for approval.

The Owners covenant with SCDC not to Commence Development of any Development Parcel until either:

**3.1** the Affordable Housing Scheme relevant to that Development Parcel has been approved or deemed approved by the SCDC pursuant to clause 18 of this Agreement; or

**3.2** in the case of para 2.2 above the Owners have notified SCDC in writing that the Development Parcel shall not include any Affordable Rented Housing Units or Starter Home Units.

**4** The Owners covenant with SCDC that 10% (ten percent) of the Dwellings constructed as part of the Development shall be provided as Affordable Rented Housing Units in accordance with the Affordable Housing Scheme (unless otherwise agreed by SCDC) such that:

**4.1** by the provision of 2500 Dwellings, not less than 250 Affordable Rented Housing Units shall have been provided; and

**4.2** by the provision of 3000 Dwellings, not less than 300 Affordable Rented Housing Units shall have been provided.

**5** The Owners covenant with SCDC that 40% (forty percent) of the Dwellings constructed as part of the Development shall be provided as Starter Homes and shall be provided on each Development Parcel where there are to be Starter Homes in accordance with the Affordable Housing Scheme (unless otherwise agreed by SCDC).

**6** The Owners shall be permitted subject to paragraph 7 below to seek to amend the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme at any time following the initial approval or deemed approval of the Affordable Housing Scheme pursuant to paragraph 2 above SAVE THAT in seeking to do so the Owners shall submit such amendments for approval to SCDC in writing and thereafter the provisions of Clause 18 shall apply.

**7** The Owners covenant with SCDC that they shall not seek to vary an Approved Affordable Housing Scheme other than in accordance with the following provisions:

**7.1** before the Owners seek to vary the approved Affordable Housing Scheme they shall have first satisfied SCDC that they have used Reasonable Endeavours to provide the Affordable Rented Housing and the Starter Homes in accordance with the Affordable Housing Scheme as originally approved; and

**7.2** the Owners shall submit such information reasonably requested by SCDC in order for SCDC to consider and determine any such application for a variation to the Affordable Housing Scheme.

**8** Where the Owners seek to vary an Approved Affordable Housing Scheme either prior to Commencement of Development on the relevant Development Parcel or during development on the Development Parcel the Owners shall not Occupy more than 50% of the Dwellings until the variation to the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme has been approved or where the variation has not been approved the development proceeds in accordance with the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme.

**9** Where an Affordable Housing Scheme for a Development Parcel is submitted for approval prior to the submission of any Reserved Matters Application for the Development Parcel the Owners covenant with SCDC that a Reserved Matters Application for a Development Parcel shall only be made if it reflects the provisions of the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme for that Development Parcel.

**10** Where applicable within a relevant Affordable Rented Housing Site the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to provide any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within adoptable public highway or in common facilities but for the avoidance of doubt such Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage shall not be laid underneath and/or within 3 metres of the actual or anticipated building footprint of any part of the Affordable Rented Housing Site.

### Part 2 — Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer

The Owners covenant with SCDC as follows:

**1.1** Prior to the submission of the first Reserved Matters Application seeking approval of reserved matters for any Dwelling to have made an offer to SCDC to enter into an Agreement for an Affordable Rented Housing Building Lease with SCDC in respect of 10 per cent or more of Affordable Rented Housing Units across the entire Site but in clusters of not greater than 25 Affordable Rented Housing Units on terms consistent with the Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer.

**1.2** If within 40 Working Days of the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer being made the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer has been accepted in writing by SCDC either in whole or in part (PROVIDED that SCDC shall not elect to take any of the Affordable Rented Housing Units offered in part unless the number of Affordable Rented Housing Units to be built and transferred to SCDC exceeds 10% of the Affordable Rented Housing Units to be provided across the Site) the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to exchange an Agreement for a Building Lease with SCDC within 3 months of the date when the written acceptance (subject to contract) of the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer is received by the Owners.

**1.3** In the event that SCDC: (i) does not respond to the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer within the said period of 40 Working Days; or rejects the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer the Owners will be free to market the Affordable Rented Housing Units that were subject to the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer to a Registered Provider or through direct delivery and management by the HCA.

**1.4** If by Commencement contracts have been exchanged with a Nominated Registered Provider for the build and transfer of the Affordable Rented Housing Units (less any Affordable Rented Housing Units that are subject to the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer) the provisions of paragraph 2 of this Part 2 shall apply.

**1.5** If by Commencement contracts have not been exchanged between the Owners and Nominated Registered Provider for the build and transfer of the Affordable Rented Housing Units (less any Affordable Rented Housing Units that are subject to the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer) the provisions of paragraph 3 of this Part 2 shall apply.

**1.6** If prior to the Commencement of any Development Parcel which includes Dwellings contracts have been exchanged with a Nominated Registered Provider for the build and transfer of the Affordable Rented Housing Units (less any Affordable Rented Housing Units that are subject to the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer) within that Development Parcel the provisions of paragraph 2 of this Part 2 shall also apply.

**2** In the event the provisions of paragraph 1.4 or 1.6 of this Part 2 apply then:

**2.1** where the HCA is procuring the delivery of the Affordable Rented Housing Units, the HCA will grant an Affordable Rented Housing Building Lease to the Nominated Register Provider after the grant of each Reserved Matters Approval for the Affordable Rented Housing Units within the relevant Development Parcel and shall transfer the freehold interest in each relevant Affordable Rented Housing Site to the Nominated Registered Provider in accordance with the terms of the Affordable Rented Housing Building Lease;

**2.2** In the event the provisions of paragraph 1.5 of this Part 2 apply then the Owners shall (in respect of the relevant Development Parcel on which Affordable Rented Housing Units are to be provided): where the HCA is not procuring the delivery of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and the delivery of Affordable Rented Housing Units are being procured by a developer or Owners that are not the HCA, the freehold interest in the relevant Affordable Rented Housing Site will be transferred to the Nominated Registered Provider after the grant of the Reserved Matters Approval for the Affordable Rented Housing Units within the relevant Development Parcel by way of an Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer.

**3.1** on or before Commencement of Development on the relevant Development Parcel offer to enter into an Agreement for a Building Lease with SCDC for the Affordable Rented Housing Site in such Development Parcel on terms consistent with the Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer where the Owners are the HCA procuring the delivery of the Affordable Rented Housing Units; or

**3.2** where the HCA is not procuring the delivery of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and the delivery of Affordable Rented Housing Units are being procured by a developer or Owners that are not the HCA on or before Commencement of each relevant Development Parcel to offer an Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to SCDC for the Affordable Rented Housing Site in such Development Parcel;

**3.3** If within 40 Working Days of the offer being made in accordance with paragraph 3.1 or 3.2 of this Part 2 (as applicable) SCDC has accepted in writing the relevant offer the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to exchange an Agreement for a Building Lease with SCDC or to submit an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to SCDC for completion within 3 months of the date when the written acceptance (subject to contract) of the offer pursuant to paragraph 3.1 or paragraph 3.2 of this Part 2 is received by the Owners.

**3.4** In the event that:

**3.4.1** SCDC does not respond to the offer being made in accordance with paragraph 3.1 or 3.2 of this Part 2 within the said period of 40 Working Days or rejects the relevant offer; or

**3.4.2** SCDC has not exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease or completed an Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer within 3 months of the date when the written acceptance (subject to contract) of the offer pursuant to paragraph 3.1 or paragraph 3.2 of this Part 2 is received and subject to the requirements of paragraph 2.2 then paragraph 3.5 and 3.6 of this Part 2 shall apply.

**3.5** The Owners shall apply to SCDC to amend the Affordable Housing Scheme for the relevant Development Parcel and any other Affordable Housing Scheme to move the Affordable Rented Housing Units onto another Development Parcel Provided That SCDC shall not be required to accept such amendment where the effect of the amendment will be to back load the provision of Affordable Rented Housing Units to late phases of the Development. If the application to amend the Affordable Housing Scheme is not approved then the provisions of paragraph 3.6 of this Part 2 shall apply;

**3.6** If the circumstances in paragraph 3.4 apply or immediately following any non-approval of the amendment of the Affordable Housing Scheme pursuant to paragraph 3.5 of this Part 2 the Owners shall continue to seek to exchange with a Nominated Registered Provider for the build and transfer of the Affordable Rented Housing Units (less any Affordable Rented Housing Units that are subject to the SCDC Affordable Rented Housing Offer) in such Development Parcel.

### Part 3 — Starter Homes

**1** Starter Home Units

**1.1** The Owners covenant with the Council as follows:

**1.1.1** to provide the Starter Home Units within the Development in accordance with the Planning Permission;

**1.1.2** prior to the first Disposal of each Starter Home Unit to submit to the Council for their approval details of the Starter Home Open Market Value and Discounted Price proposed for that Unit and not to Dispose of any Starter Home Unit unless the Starter Home Open Market Value and the Discounted Price has been approved by the Council (such approval not to be unreasonably withheld or delayed);

**1.1.3** not to Dispose of any Starter Home Unit other than in accordance with the terms of this Part 3 of Schedule 7;

**1.1.4** not to Dispose of any Starter Home Unit on first Disposal other than to a Qualifying First Time Buyer;

**1.1.5** not to Dispose of any Starter Home Unit on first Disposal for a price exceeding the Discounted Price applicable at the date of the Disposal;

**1.1.6** not to Dispose of any Starter Home Unit on first Disposal for a price exceeding the Price Cap;

**1.1.7** not to allow a Starter Home Unit to be Sub-let during the Restricted Period;

**1.1.8** that the first Disposal of a Starter Home Unit shall contain the following covenants by the Transferee for themselves and their successors in title to the Starter Home Unit for the benefit of the Council:

   - **(a)** not to Dispose of the Starter Home Unit during the Restricted Period for a price exceeding the Discounted Price;

   - **(b)** not to Dispose of the Starter Home Unit during the Restricted Period other than to a Qualifying First Time Buyer;

   - **(c)** not to Sub-let or permit the Starter Home Unit to be Sub-let during the Restricted Period;

   - **(d)** not to make any external or structural alteration or addition or any opening in the boundary structure of the Starter Home Unit during the Restricted Period unless otherwise permitted by the Starter Home Regulations;

   - **(e)** not to Dispose of the Starter Home Unit without having first procured the entry of a restriction on the Proprietorship Register of the title for the relevant Starter Home Unit in accordance with paragraph 1.1.9 of this Part 3 of Schedule 7; and

   - **(f)** not to Dispose of the Starter Home Unit during the Restricted Period without on each occasion procuring that the Transferee covenants directly with the Council to observe and perform the covenants contained in this paragraph 1 of Part 3 of this Schedule 7 and delivers a completed deed of covenant in such terms to the Council.

**1.1.9** (so as to preserve the Starter Home Units as starter homes for the Restricted Period) upon the first Disposal of a Starter Home Unit to a Qualifying First Time Buyer to procure that a restriction be entered on the Proprietorship Register of the title for the relevant Starter Home Unit in the following form (or with minimal changes to achieve the same effect but acceptable to the Land Registry):

"No disposition of the registered estate by the proprietor or by the proprietor of any registered charge is to be registered without a certificate signed by a solicitor or conveyancer for SCDC confirming that the provisions of clause [o] of the [transfer] [registered lease] have been complied with".

**1.1.10** The Starter Home Units shall be released from the provisions of this Schedule at the end of the Restricted Period after which they may be sold on the open market and treated as Market Housing Units PROVIDED THAT the owner of the Starter Home Unit has, following the end of the Restricted Period, submitted to the Council a written request for confirmation that the terms of this Schedule have been complied with during the Restricted Period including sufficient evidence to demonstrate to the satisfaction of the Council compliance with this Schedule during the Restricted Period, such evidence including (but not limited to) Council tax bills, affidavit/statutory declaration(s), certified copies of transfer documents (if any) and up to date official copies of the registered title and the Council have so confirmed.

**1.2** The provisions of this Schedule shall not be binding on a mortgagee in possession exercising a power of sale of a Starter Home Unit.

### Part 4 — Trigger Controls

In the event of any conflict between the triggers at paragraphs 1–3, 2.2, 4.1 and 4.2 of Part 1 and the provisions set out in this Part 4 then the provisions set out elsewhere in this Schedule 7 shall prevail.

In respect of each Development Parcel containing Affordable Rented Housing Units and/or Starter Homes the Owners covenant that unless otherwise agreed with SCDC:

**1.1** no more than 50% of the Market Housing Units within that Development Parcel shall be Occupied until:

**1.1.1** where the Development Parcel includes Starter Home Units but not Affordable Rented Housing Units 50% of the Starter Home Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; or

**1.1.2** where the Development Parcel includes Starter Home Units and Affordable Rented Housing Units 50% of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and 30% of the Starter Home Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation;

**1.1.3** where the Development Parcel includes Affordable Rented Housing Units but no Starter Home Units:

   - **(a)** 50% of the Affordable Rented Housing Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; or

   - **(b)** the Owners have unconditionally exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease with SCDC or submitted an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to SCDC for completion; or

   - **(c)** the Owners have unconditionally exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease with a Nominated Registered Provider or submitted an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to a Nominated Registered Provider.

**1.2** no more than 80% of the Market Housing Units within that Development Parcel shall be Occupied until:

**1.2.1** where the Development Parcel includes Starter Home Units but not Affordable Rented Housing Units 100% of the Starter Home Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; or

**1.2.2** where the Development Parcel includes Starter Home Units and Affordable Rented Housing Units:

   - **(a)** 100% of the Affordable Rented Housing Units and 100% of the Starter Home Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; or

   - **(b)** 100% of the Starter Home Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation and the Owners have:

      - **(i)** Unconditionally exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease with SCDC or submitted an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to SCDC for completion in respect of the Affordable Rented Housing Units; or

      - **(ii)** the Owners have unconditionally exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease with a Nominated Registered Provider or submitted an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to a Nominated Registered Provider.

**1.2.3** where the Development Parcel includes Affordable Rented Housing Units but no Starter Home Units:

   - **(a)** 100% of the Affordable Rented Housing Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; or

   - **(b)** the Owners have unconditionally exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease with SCDC or submitted an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to SCDC for completion; or

   - **(c)** the Owners have unconditionally exchanged an Agreement for a Building Lease with a Nominated Registered Provider or submitted an executed Affordable Rented Housing Site Transfer to a Nominated Registered Provider.

**1.2.4** In respect of the Development the Owners covenant that unless otherwise agreed with SCDC no more than 90% of the ultimate total number of Market Housing Units within the Development shall be Occupied until 100% of the Affordable Rented Housing Units in respect of the Development have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation.

### Part 5 — Affordable Homes Build Standards

Where an Affordable Rented Housing Site is transferred to a Nominated Registered Provider or where an Affordable Rented Housing Unit is transferred to SCDC (or SCDC's nominee) the Owners covenant with SCDC to construct and complete or procure the construction and completion of the Affordable Rented Housing Units (together with all necessary services connections and infrastructure) in accordance with the relevant Affordable Housing Scheme.

The Affordable Rented Housing Units shall be deemed to be completed either when the NHBC Buildmark Certificate (or equivalent) shall have been issued in respect of each of the Affordable Rented Housing Units or when the local authority building control consultancy or approved inspector certifies them complete, if earlier.

### Part 6 — Affordable Rented Housing Indicative Mix (as to Numbers of Bedrooms)

The Affordable Rented Housing Indicative Mix is as follows:

   - **(a)** Not less than 65% of the Affordable Rented Dwellings in the Development shall have 1 or 2 bedrooms; and

   - **(b)** Not less than 20% of the Affordable Rented Dwellings in the Development shall have 3 or 4 bedrooms;

and the exact numbers of property sizes and types shall be agreed as part of the Affordable Housing Scheme approval Provided That SCDC hereby agree to the Affordable Rented Housing Indicative Mix and shall not fail to approve any Affordable Housing Scheme that is consistent in delivering a mix of the Affordable Rented Housing Units across the Development that would reflect the indicative mix as set out in the Affordable Rented Housing Indicative Mix But for the Avoidance of Doubt the number of bedrooms and sizes shall come forward through the Affordable Housing Scheme and shall reflect the housing needs of SCDC from time to time.

### Part 7 — Lettings Policy

Subject to there being in force a Lettings Policy as produced by SCDC from time to time the Owners covenant with SCDC to ensure the retention at all times of the provision of:

**1.1** any and all of the Affordable Rented Units on first Occupation; and

**1.2** 75% of the Affordable Rented Units on any subsequent Occupation

in accordance with that Lettings Policy PROVIDED THAT if SCDC propose to amend the Lettings Policy from that appended to this Agreement then SCDC shall first consult with the Owners and the relevant Registered Provider or Registered Providers (as successors in title to the Owners of the relevant part of the Development affected by the change) in relation to any proposed amendments and the Owners and the relevant Registered Provider or Registered Providers shall not be required to comply with the amendments unless and until such amendments have first been approved by the Owners and the relevant Registered Provider or Registered Providers for the Development Parcel or Development Parcels affected by the changes.

In circumstances where no Lettings Policy exists and where a Nominated Registered Provider has agreed to purchase the Affordable Rented Housing Units then no Dwelling designated for Affordable Rented Housing shall be occupied unless the Nominated Registered Provider has first entered into a Nomination Agreement which reflects the arrangements set out in Part 8 (Nominations Arrangements) of this Schedule or is in SCDC's then current form of Nomination Agreement which (inter alia) sets out the criteria for selecting those who qualify for occupation of the Dwelling, and the procedure for nominating prospective occupiers, the form of such agreement to be subject to any amendments reasonably required by the Nominated Registered Provider or its funders and agreed by SCDC acting reasonably.

### Part 8 — Nominations Arrangements

Pursuant to paragraph 2 of Part 7 (Lettings Policy) of this Schedule, as between the Nominated Registered Provider and SCDC the following provisions shall govern the nominations/allocations of occupation of the Affordable Rented Housing Units.

The Nominated Approved Registered Provider shall notify SCDC in writing from time to time of any Affordable Rented Housing Unit which shall become vacant and available for occupation.

The Nominated Registered Provider and SCDC shall liaise as necessary with regard to local housing need generally and the list of Qualifying Persons (as defined in paragraph 7 of this Part of this Schedule).

In cases where SCDC shall have the right of nomination:

**4.1** SCDC will normally submit 3 names of nominees to the Nominated Registered Provider within seven (7) working days of receipt of written notice of a vacancy from the Nominated Registered Provider.

**4.2** SCDC may however nominate the Nominated Registered Provider's preferred choice unless there shall in the opinion of SCDC be good reasons for not doing so; in such cases SCDC may within seven (7) days as aforesaid nominate another person to whom the dwelling shall be allocated by the Nominated Registered Provider unless there shall in the opinion of the Nominated Registered Provider be good reasons for not so allocating; and if the Affordable Rented Housing Unit is not so allocated, the Nominated Registered Provider and SCDC shall discuss further nominations by SCDC for that dwelling.

The Occupation of any Affordable Rented Housing Unit shall be limited to a Qualifying Person in the Cambridgeshire Sub-Region. "Cambridgeshire Sub-Region" (meaning the geographical area covered by the following Councils: Cambridge City Council, East Cambridgeshire District Council, Fenland District Council, Forest Heath District Council, Huntingdonshire District Council, South Cambridgeshire District Council, St Edmundsbury Borough Council) PROVIDED THAT the Nominated Registered Provider may allow persons who are not Qualifying Persons in the Cambridgeshire Sub-Region to occupy any dwelling if there are no Qualifying Persons in the Cambridgeshire Sub-Region available and willing to take up occupation within a period of two (2) months of when a dwelling becomes vacant and PROVIDED THAT such disposition is in accordance with the objects of the Nominated Registered Provider.

SCDC and the Nominated Registered Provider shall have the following nomination rights in respect of the Affordable Rented Housing Units.

| Nomination | SCDC | The Nominated Registered Provider |
|---|---|---|
| The initial allocation of each Affordable Dwelling | 100% | — |
| Thereafter | 75% | 25% |

**7** "Qualifying Person" means either:

**7.1** an individual who shall satisfy SCDC that he or she is in housing need as defined by and/or described in SCDC's latest adopted policies (so far as may be relevant) and who shall prior to the grant of any tenancy or lease or any agreement for a tenancy or a lease or any assignment produced to the Nominated Registered Provider or other grantor written confirmation by SCDC that such an individual is a Qualifying Person for this purpose;

**7.2** an individual on the current list (if any) of Qualifying Persons (deemed pre-certificated by SCDC) provided by SCDC ("District Council's List") PROVIDED THAT the circumstances of the Qualifying Person shall not have materially changed from the date of the certificate or the District Council's List (as the case may be) to the date of grant of a tenancy, lease or assignment such that he or she would cease to be in housing need (as defined) or on the District Council's List;

**7.3** is registered on the Council's Housing Register.

### Part 9 — Starter Home Type Products

The Owners and SCDC hereby agree that to the extent that planning policy or planning guidance, the Starter Home Regulations or amendments to the Housing and Planning Act 2016 broadens the definition of starter homes as currently defined in section 2 of the Housing and Planning Act 2016 to permit different tenures:

**1.1** Then following the grant of Reserved Matters Approvals that will facilitate the delivery of 20% of the total number of Dwellings on the Development as Starter Homes the Owners and SCDC agree to review the provisions of Part 3 of this Schedule 7 and the associated definitions to decide whether, having regard to the housing needs of SCDC at that time, the provisions should be adapted to encompass the broader definition of tenures permitted Provided That agreement to any variations to the provisions of Part 3 of this Schedule 7 and the associated definitions shall be at the joint absolute discretion of SCDC and the Owners.

**1.2** Any modifications agreed pursuant to paragraph 1.1 of this Part 9 of Schedule 7 shall be secured by way of a deed of agreement between the parties to this Agreement.

## Schedule 8 — Community Buildings

### Relevant Definitions

**"Community Buildings"** means the Community Hub, Library and the Health Facility and "Community Building" means any one of these facilities or combined facilities as appropriate.

**"Community Buildings Contribution Cap"** means £14,548,805 (fourteen million, five hundred and forty eight thousand, eight hundred and five British pounds) being the maximum amount of money that the Owners shall be obliged by the terms of this Agreement to use or pay towards the design, construction and fitting out of the Community Buildings Purpose and in calculating whether the said cap has been exceeded the Costs shall be taken into account.

**"Community Hub"** means a building (or part of a building) whose internal floor space shall be at least 1,265m² (unless otherwise agreed between the Owners and SCDC) to be provided as part of the Development for the use as a multi-use flexible community facility accommodating the uses set out in the Initial Community Building Specification and could include the Library Facility once the Town Centre Strategy is defined, PROVIDED THAT for the avoidance of doubt SCDC and the Owners agree that nothing in this definition or Schedule shall prevent SCDC from extending the Community Hub by way of future extensions outside the scope of this Agreement which for the avoidance of doubt shall not be at the Owners' cost and shall not be covered by any Contribution payable pursuant to this Agreement.

**"Community Buildings Actual Contribution"** means the sum to be spent on the Community Buildings subject to the Community Buildings Contribution Cap.

**"Community Buildings Purpose"** means for the designing construction completion and fitting out and equipping of the Community Buildings in accordance with the Community Hub Detail Specification and the Health and Library Facility Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed approved by the Owners.

**"Community Building Land"** means an area (or areas if agreed by the Project Core Team) of Serviced land within the Town Centre excluding the Town Park the location of which shall be identified through the Town Centre Strategy capable of accommodating the Community Buildings and any necessary ancillary external servicing, circulation, storage or landscaping directly associated with the Community Buildings and provision of or access to Blue Badge car parking spaces PROVIDED THAT in any event the Community Building Land shall be accessed by a road which will be built to an adoptable standard AND PROVIDED FURTHER that the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to enter into a section 38 highway Agreement as to future adoption of such road within a reasonable timeframe having regard to the anticipated date of Completion of the Community Building and PROVIDED FURTHER THAT to the extent that any external servicing, circulation, storage or landscaping is shared space such space shall not form part if the Community Building Land for the purpose of the Community Building Transfer.

**"Community Building Land Transfer"** means a transfer by the Owners to SCDC (or the Town Council if applicable) of the unencumbered freehold in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 15 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the Community Building Land (or relevant part) and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer which shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict use of the land as a Community Building and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and

   - **(b)** not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

   - **(c)** include reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Community Building Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site where such rights are reserved for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity and telecommunications subject to the provisions of paragraph 5 of Part 1 (Community Buildings Location and Specifications) of this Schedule; and

   - **(d)** include for the benefit of the Community Building Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Community Building Land for its purpose for the benefit of the community;

   - **(e)** be at nil consideration; and

   - **(f)** include a covenant that the Community Building Land shall not be used for any purpose other than primarily for community use or for supporting community activity and development and use for a community building and associated ancillary facilities and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto and (health and library related uses); and

   - **(g)** include a covenant that the Community Building that has or will be constructed on the Community Building Land shall after construction subject to suitable funding be maintained for the benefit of the community arising from the Development which shall include permitting voluntary sector use; and

   - **(h)** provisions dealing with the arrangements set out in paragraph 6 of Part 2 (Community Building Land Transfer Generic Arrangements) of this Schedule.

**"Community Buildings Detailed Specifications"** means the Community Hub Detailed Specification, the Library Specification and the Health Facility Detailed Specification.

**"Community Hub Detailed Specification"** means a detailed specification for the Community Hub to be prepared by the Community Project Core Team by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in preparing and reviewing specifications for facilities of this sort consistent with the intended use such specification to include room shape sizes and heights as well as details of external and internal work specification and finishes and also include details of services and heating PROVIDED THAT the specification shall be in accordance with the Initial Community Hub Specification and shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Community Hub such that would result in a combined cost to construct and fit out and equip the Community Hub which when combined with the Health facility and the Library Facility would exceed the Community Buildings Contribution Cap.

**"Community Project Core Team"** means a steering group comprised of representatives of SCDC, the County Council and the HCA or their nominee and which group shall be solely responsible for taking any decision in relation to any and all of the following:

   - **(a)** Community Hub Detailed Specification, Library Detailed Specification and the Health Facility Detailed Specification; and

   - **(b)** approving the timetable for the design, procurement and construction of the Community Buildings;

PROVIDED THAT each party acting reasonably may be permitted to invite other interested parties to the meetings as agreed by the other parties in advance;

**"Community Project Core Team Terms of Reference"** means the terms of reference set out in part B of this Schedule 8.

**"Costs"** means those costs applicable to the Community Buildings which shall be permitted to be included within any calculation to establish whether the Community Buildings Contribution Cap has been exceeded shall include:

   - **(a)** any costs arising in connection with any construction contract let in connection with the construction and completion of the Community Buildings which shall also include the provision of landscaping and access arrangements internal to the Community Building Land, boundary treatments and car parking and for the avoidance of doubt unless such costs are already covered in the main construction contract the costs shall include costs of any inspections by the Surveyor as provided for in this Schedule 8 and the costs of inspections by such other person or company undertaking inspections of the Community Buildings for the purpose of issuing the Certificate of Practical Completion and/or the Certificate of Final Completion; and

   - **(b)** abnormal costs agreed by SCDC acting reasonably which shall include responding within a reasonable timescale having regard to any programme including but not limited to acoustic mitigation, external works and retaining structures, disabled ramps and lifts, foundations to cater for slopes on the Community Building Land to accommodate for gradients on and adjacent to this land, measures to address potential for flooding;

   - **(c)** any reasonable commitment fees, procurement fees, finance charges and any interest payable in respect of the Community Buildings;

   - **(d)** design costs including all professional fees associated with the Community Buildings:

      - **(i)** where the contract is a design and build contract 12% of the total contract value; or

      - **(ii)** 12% of the total cost cap;

   - **(e)** statutory fees;

   - **(f)** costs associated with requirements from the statutory undertakings;

   - **(g)** cost of fixtures and fitting and equipment;

   - **(h)** such reasonable legal agents and consultancy fees (whether such fees relate to the Owners' (or SCDC's if the Community Building is constructed by SCDC) internal staff or employees or external advisors) as may be associated with the entering into of the construction contract or any other contracts for the design, construction, completion, fitting out and equipping of the Community Buildings,

PROVIDED THAT the parties acknowledge and agree that in identifying the Costs as defined by this definition no element of double counting of elements of costs shall be permitted.

**"Defects"** means any defects shrinkage or other faults in relation to the construction and/or design of the Community Building (and including for the avoidance of doubt any latent construction and/or design defects as are defined or described within the design contract and/or construction contract and such contracts shall contain proper and reasonable provisions relating to and definitions of defects) relating to the Community Building as procured by the party to this Agreement with whom the responsibility for the design and construction of the Community Building rests but which for the avoidance of doubt defects shall not include any defects or faults in relation to construction equipment or internal fitting out where the relevant damage defect or fault is created as a result of works or accidents undertaken by the subsequent owners occupiers or users of the Community Building or where such construction equipment or internal fitting out has itself been undertaken by the subsequent owners occupiers or users of the Community Building.

**"Health and Library Facility"** means a building or part of a building whose total internal floor space shall be 2,877m² (unless otherwise agreed between the Owners and SCDC) together with associated car parking facilities and associated landscape or amenity space to be provided as part of the Development for the use as a health facility and Library and Lifelong Learning Facility accommodating the areas set out in the Initial Health and Library Facilities Specifications PROVIDED THAT for the avoidance of doubt SCDC and the Owners agree that nothing in this definition or Schedule shall prevent SCDC from extending the Health and Library Facility by way of future extensions outside the scope of this Agreement which for the avoidance of doubt shall not be at the Owners' cost and shall not be covered by any Contribution payable pursuant to this Agreement.

**"Health and Library Facilities Detailed Specification"** means a detailed specification for the Health Facility and Library to be prepared by the Community Project Core Team by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in preparing and reviewing specifications for buildings of this sort consistent with the intended use such specification to include room shape sizes and heights as well as details of external and internal work specification and finishes and also include details of services and heating PROVIDED THAT the specification shall be in accordance with the Initial Health facility and/or Library Specification and shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Health and Library Facilities such that would result in a combined cost to construct and fit out and equip the Health and Library Facilities which when combined with the Community Hub would exceed the Community Buildings Contribution Cap.

**"Health and Library Facilities Purpose"** means for the designing, construction completion and fitting out and equipping of the Health and Library Facilities in accordance with the Health and Library Facility Detailed Specifications or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed approved by the Owners.

**"Initial Community Hub Specification"** means the specification for the Community Hub appended to this Agreement at Appendix 16.

**"Initial Health and Library Facility Specification"** means the specification to the Health and Library Facilities appended to this Agreement at Appendix 16 and as supplemented by the Library and Life Long Learning Facility Specification.

**"Library and Lifelong Learning Capital Contribution"** means the sum of £368,550 (Three Hundred and Sixty Eight Thousand Five Hundred and Fifty British Pounds) to be spent by the County Council solely on the Library and Life Long Learning Capital Contribution Purposes;

**"Library and Lifelong Learning Capital Contribution Purposes"** means the use of the Library and Life Long Learning Contribution towards the fit out cost of the Library and Lifelong Learning Facility;

**"Library and Lifelong Learning Facility"** means floorspace of 1,000 sqm (plus 50 sqm workplace) which meets the Library and Life Long Learning Facility Specification and suitable for a range of multi-functional community services and facilities including a library under the Public Libraries and Museums Act 1964 or other relevant enabling legislation which help to serve the learning cultural reading and information needs of a community.

**"Library and Life Long Learning Facility Specification"** means floorspace which meets the following criteria:

   - **(a)** is predominantly on a single level at ground floor unless otherwise agreed between the County Council and the Owners;

   - **(b)** in the same building (unless otherwise agreed between the County Council and the Owners) will have access to shared meeting facilities, staff facilities (including kitchen), buggy parking, delivery access/a delivery parking bay and customer toilets;

   - **(c)** good clear views in from the street and from within the building in which the floorspace is contained;

   - **(d)** offering the potential for part of the floorspace to be made secure (but the floorspace may otherwise open/flow into shared space within the building in which the floorspace is contained);

   - **(e)** offering the potential for self-service opening whenever the building in which the floorspace is contained is open;

   - **(f)** of a layout, preferably square or rectangular that will give good sight lines with opportunities if possible for discrete zones for different functions and for flexibility in use of space;

   - **(g)** having excellent power and data connections to enable installation of appropriate information and communications technology and future changes in layout;

   - **(h)** which is accessible on foot by cycle and by public transport;

   - **(i)** which is accessible to everyone in the community to use for a range of activities and services;

   - **(j)** a flexible space that is able to accommodate new services and changing service delivery patterns in the future;

   - **(k)** has CIBSE standard lighting levels suitable for a library, built to BREEAM rating "very good", with a ceiling of 1.5 times normal height for offices and load-bearing floors suitable for library shelving,

   - **(l)** use of as much natural ventilation as possible with comfort cooling where necessary in areas of high heat gain with an internal temperature capped at 25 Degrees Celsius.

   - **(m)** use of security measures throughout the building for the safety of customers, staff, and resources, appropriate to the operation of the building.

**"Library and Lifelong Learning Revenue Contribution"** means the sum of £461,160 (Four Hundred and Sixty One Thousand One Hundred and Sixty) to be spent by the County Council solely on the Library and Life Long Learning Revenue Contribution Purposes;

**"Library and Lifelong Learning Revenue Contribution Purposes"** means the use of the Library and Life Long Learning Revenue Contribution towards funding the delivery, maintenance or running cost of the Library and Life Long Learning Facility;

**"Option"** and **"Options"** means the option and options set out in Part 2 (Community Building Land Transfer Generic Arrangements), Part 3 (Option 1 Transfer and Construction Arrangements) and Part 4 (Option 2 - Transfer and Construction Arrangements), of this Schedule.

**"Surveyor"** means such person or persons as may be notified in writing by SCDC to the Owners and suitably qualified in accordance with the general requirements of the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors.

### Part 1 — Community Buildings Location and Specifications

**1** The location of the Community Building Land shall be identified by the Owners and approved by SCDC through the Town Centre Strategy.

**2** In the event that the Owners submit the Reserved Matters Application for the Community Buildings (or any part) then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application the Owners shall undertake pre-application discussion with SCDC (through the Community Project Core Team) in relation to the exact boundaries for the relevant building and the Community Building Land (or relevant part) and the exact boundaries for the Community Building and the Community Building Land (or relevant part) shall subject to paragraph 4 of this Part 1 (Community Buildings Location and Specifications) of this Schedule below be submitted to and approved by SCDC either:

**2.1** as part of Reserved Matters Applications for any of the Development Parcel(s) adjacent to which the Community Building Land is located; or

**2.2** as a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Community Buildings (or relevant part).

**3** In the event that SCDC submits the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application for the Community Buildings (or relevant part) then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application it shall have obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the exact size and boundaries of the building (through the Community Project Core Team) and in the event that such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is submitted before the relevant Community Building Land Transfer is completed then SCDC shall have also obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the extent of the site in relation to which such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is made.

**4** The Community Building Detailed Specifications shall be prepared and agreed by the Community Project Core Team prior to the Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel adjacent to which the Community Building Land is located; or prior to the Reserved Matters Application solely for the Community Buildings (or relevant part) save for matters that will not affect approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Community Buildings (or relevant part).

**5** The Owners shall:

**5.1** use Reasonable Endeavours to locate any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within a Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Community Buildings or the relevant part) which Service Corridor shall not be located underneath the actual or anticipated building footprint of the Community Buildings PROVIDED THAT if SCDC has not advised the Owners in writing of the actual or anticipated building footprint before the said Service Corridor has been approved or deemed to have been approved then the Owners shall be permitted to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the approved or deemed approved Service Corridor notwithstanding that the footprint of the Community Buildings (or relevant part) may ultimately be constructed within the Service Corridor; and

**5.2** save as otherwise specifically agreed by an Expert, not to locate Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Community Building Land which are outside of the Service Corridor and/or which are under an existing or known building footprint.

### Part 2 — Community Building Land Transfer Generic Arrangements

**1** Prior to Occupation of 750 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which sets out which of the options set out in paragraphs 1.1 and 1.2 of this Part 2 of this Schedule the Owners intend to elect to progress.

**1.1** Option 1: SCDC to construct the Health and Library Facilities and for the Owners to pay that part of the Community Building Actual Contribution attributable to the Health and Library Facilities to SCDC to be used for the Health and Library Facilities Purpose PROVIDED THAT if the Owners elect to pursue this option the written notice shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Community Building Land (or relevant part) pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer associated with the Health and Library Facilities PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the Owners shall not be obliged to transfer the Community Building Land or relevant part any earlier than the date that is 2 years prior to the anticipated date for the Occupation of 1,501 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site; and

**1.2** Option 2: The Owners to construct the Health and Library Facilities to the Health and Library Facilities Building Detailed Specification SUBJECT TO that part of the Community Buildings Contribution Cap attributable to the Health and Library Facilities not being exceeded.

**2** Prior to Occupation of 2,100 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which sets out which of the options set out in paragraphs 2.1 and 2.2 of this Part 2 of this Schedule the Owners intend to elect to progress.

**2.1** Option 1: SCDC to construct the Community Hub and for the Owners to pay that part of the Community Building Actual Contribution attributable to the Community Hub to SCDC to be used for the Community Hub Purpose PROVIDED THAT if the Owners elect to pursue this option the written notice shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Community Building Land pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer associated with the Community Hub PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the Owners shall not be obliged to transfer the Community Building Land or relevant part any earlier than the date that is 2 years prior to the anticipated date for the Occupation of 4,200 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site; and

**2.2** Option 2: The Owners to construct the Community Hub to the Community Hub Detailed Specification SUBJECT TO that part of the Community Buildings Contribution Cap attributable to the Community Hub not being exceeded.

**3** In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they:

**3.1** elect to pursue Option 1 for any of the Community Buildings then the provisions of Part 3 (Option 1 Transfer and Construction Arrangements) of this Schedule shall apply;

**3.2** elect to pursue Option 2 for any of the Community Buildings then the provisions of Part 4 (Option 2 Transfer and Construction Arrangements) of this Schedule shall apply.

**4** In the event that SCDC declines or does not accept any of the offers made to it pursuant to Options 1 and 2 to have the relevant Community Building Land transferred to it within 10 Working Days for SCDC to construct (as applicable) the relevant Community Building the Owners shall not be obliged to transfer the Community Building Land nor will the Owners be obliged to pay that part of the Community Buildings Contribution attributable to the relevant Community Building to SCDC and the provisions of and restrictions in the relevant Options referred to above as in respect of each of the Options 1 and 2 (as applicable) shall cease to apply and for the avoidance of doubt in any of these circumstances the Owners shall not be obliged to construct the relevant Community Building nor otherwise transfer or manage the relevant Community Building Land save where the relevant Option indicates that the Owners have elected to construct the relevant Community Building rather than elect for SCDC to construct the Community Building.

**5** Whether the Owners or SCDC construct the Community Building the following provisions shall apply and the party with whom the responsibility to construct the Community Building rests (the "Constructing Party") shall seek and consult with SCDC or the Owners whichever is not the Constructing Party (the "Other Party") through the Community Project Core Team or otherwise to obtain the prior input and representations in writing from the Other Party in relation to the following and where received the Contracting Party shall act in good faith in taking into account any such input and representations before making any decisions or preparing details or taking steps referred to below PROVIDED THAT any such representations are received within 40 Working Days of any such request:

**5.1** Tendering or making appointments as regards any consultants or contractors whether internal appointments made informally or formally of the Owners or the SCDC's staff or employees in relation to the procurement construction and completion of the Community Buildings PROVIDED THAT the written notification from the Contracting Party to the Other Party shall also include supporting information justifying the need for the costs or appointments, how the costs or appointments present best value for the project and how the anticipated notified expenditure or appointments support the overall aim for high quality design for the Development.

**6** Where SCDC (or the Town Council as its nominee) are to construct a Community Building SCDC covenants with the Owners to use reasonable endeavours to efficiently design, layout and construct the Community Building (as applicable) including any use of the balance of the Community Building Land where such use does not involve the construction of building(s) with the overriding aim and commitment to the Owners that SCDC shall only use such part of the Community Building Land (as applicable) as is reasonably necessary to deliver the Community Buildings to applicable building standards and in accordance with the Community Building Detailed Specifications AND PROVIDED THAT in the event that the Reserved Matters Approvals indicate or it is otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners that any part of the Community Building Land is not required for the construction and laying out of the Community Buildings then SCDC covenants with the Owners to offer to transfer such land back to the HCA for nil consideration and if the HCA accepts such an offer SCDC shall complete such a transfer as soon as practicably possible with each party bearing their own legal costs and there being no restrictions contained in the transfer on the future use that the HCA to put such land to together with such other terms restrictions and rights as may be agreed between the parties.

### Part 3 — Option 1 Transfer and Construction Arrangements

**1** In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 1 in respect of any of the Community Buildings then subject to both paragraph 4 in Part 2 (Community Building Land Transfer Generic Arrangements) the following provisions shall apply.

**2** The Owners shall:

**2.2** execute and deliver the Community Building Land Transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) for the Community Hub no later than the date being 2 years' prior to the anticipated date for Occupation of 4,200 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site where SCDC is to construct the Community Hub;

**2.2** execute and deliver the Community Building Land Transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) for the Health/Library Facility no later than the date being 2 years' prior to the anticipated date for Occupation of 1,501 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site where SCDC is to construct the Health and Library Facility;

**2.3** pay to SCDC the Community Building Contribution in the following instalments:

**2.3.1** £152,250 (one hundred and fifty two thousand two hundred and fifty British pounds) to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the design contract for the Health and Library Facility Building has occurred PROVIDED THAT where such a design contract is awarded prior to the date of the Community Building Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Community Building Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above;

**2.3.2** the amount of the Community Building Actual Contribution attributable to the Health and Library Facility to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the construction contract for the construction of the Health and Library Facility in accordance with the Library Detailed Specification and the Health Facility Detailed Specification has occurred or to a written specification which has been approved by the Owners PROVIDED THAT where such a construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Community Building Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Community Building Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above; and

**2.3.3** the amount of the Community Building Actual Contribution attributable to the Community Hub to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the construction contract for the construction of the Community Hub in accordance with the Community Hub Detailed Specification has occurred or to a written specification which has been approved by the Owners PROVIDED THAT where such a construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Community Building Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Community Building Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above.

**3** SCDC shall (as applicable):

**3.1** accept the offer of the Community Building Land Transfer for the Health and Library Facilities (or the Town Council may accept the offer instead of SCDC if so nominated by SCDC) and:

**3.2** SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Health and Library Facilities no later than 18 calendar months after the date the Community Building Land Transfer for the Health and Library Facilities is completed subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 2.3 above; and

**3.3** SCDC shall construct the Health and Library Facilities in accordance with the approved Library Detailed Specification and the Health Facility Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to Clause 18 (Deemed Approval) PROVIDED ALWAYS that SCDC shall only be required to construct and complete the Health and Library Facilities to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction fit out and completion to the point where the Health and Library Facilities can be opened to the general public and that meets the Community Building Actual Contribution applicable to the Library and Health Facility and does not exceed the Community Building Contribution Cap applicable to the Health and Library Facilities unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other sources to construct fit out and complete to a higher standard PROVIDED THAT such funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the County Council from the Owners in relation to the Development.

**3.4** accept the offer of the Community Building Land Transfer for the Community Hub (or the Town Council may accept the offer instead of SCDC if so nominated by SCDC) and:

**3.5** SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Community Hub no later than 18 calendar months after the date the Community Building Land Transfer for the Health and Library Facility is completed subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 2.3 above; and

**3.6** SCDC shall construct the Community Hub in accordance with the approved Community Hub Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to Clause 18 (Deemed Approval) PROVIDED ALWAYS that SCDC shall only be required to construct and complete the Community Hub to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction fit out and completion to the point where the Community Hub can be opened to the general public and that meets the Community Building Actual Contribution applicable to the Community Hub and does not exceed the Community Building Contribution Cap applicable to the Community Hub unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other sources to construct fit out and complete to a higher standard PROVIDED THAT such funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the County Council from the Owners in relation to the Development.

### Part 4 — Option 2 Transfer and Construction Arrangements

**1** In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 2 in respect of any of the Community Buildings or any part then subject to paragraph 4 in Part 2 (Community Building Land Transfer Generic Arrangements) of this Schedule the following provisions shall apply:

**2** The Owners shall construct the Community Buildings (or relevant part) in accordance with the relevant Community Building Detailed Specification and in accordance with the provisions of Part 6 (Requirements to be Observed by the Owners When Constructing the Community Building) of this Schedule and shall complete such construction and provide a Certificate of Practical Completion in the case of the Library and Health Facility prior to the Occupation of 1,501 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site and for the Community Hub prior to the occupation of 4,200 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

**3** The written notice referred to in paragraph 1 of Part 2 (Community Building Land Transfer Generic Arrangements) setting out the Owners election to proceed with Option 2 shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the relevant Community Building Land and the relevant completed Community Building pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) accepts the offer of the relevant Community Building Land and the relevant Community Building the actual transfer will be completed 28 days after the date the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued unless otherwise agreed in writing by the Council.

**4** In the event that SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Community Building and the relevant Community Building Land within 40 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which contains one of the following options that the Owners intend to pursue:

**4.1** the Owners wish to offer the relevant Community Building Land and the relevant Community Buildings pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT such notification shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body as a suitable entity or organisation for the purposes of the management and maintenance of the Community Building Land and the Community Building PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Community Building Land and the relevant Community Building to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Community Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Community Building Land and the relevant Community Building the actual transfer will be completed 28 days after the date the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued; or

**4.2** the written notice may confirm that the Owners will either:

**4.2.1** retain and maintain the Community Building Land and the Community Buildings or relevant part and to appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the Community Building Land and Community Buildings (or relevant part) on the Owners' behalf; or

**4.2.2** transfer the Community Building Land and Community Buildings (or relevant part) to the Management Company subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options in paragraph 4.2 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company PROVIDED ALWAYS that the Owners shall unless otherwise agreed with SCDC notify which of those options is being chosen within 12 months of the initial written notice or the letting of the construction contract whichever is the earlier.

**5** The Owners shall construct and equip the Community Buildings (or relevant part) in accordance with the relevant Community Building Detailed Specification approved or deemed to have been approved pursuant to paragraph 4 of Part 7 (Community Buildings Location and Specifications) of this Schedule and in doing so to comply with the requirements of Part 6 (Requirements to be Observed by the Owners When Constructing the Community Building) of this Schedule PROVIDED THAT the relevant Community Building Detailed Specification and the construction of the Community Buildings (or relevant part) shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Community Buildings that would result in combined Costs attributable to the relevant Community Building that would exceed the Community Building Contribution Cap or the part.

**6** No more than 1,501 Dwellings of the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site shall be Occupied:

**6.1.1** until a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Library and Health Facility has been issued; and

**6.1.2** subject to the provisions of paragraph 8 of this Part 4 (Option 2 Transfer and Construction Arrangements) of this Schedule where the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to either SCDC or an Alternative Body subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule until the Owners have executed and delivered to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable) the Community Building Land Transfer; or

**6.1.3** where pursuant to paragraph 4.2.2 of this Part 4 (Option 2 — Transfer and Construction Arrangements) above the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to a Management Company subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule the Community Building Land Transfer has been completed; or

**6.1.4** where pursuant to paragraph 4.2.1 of this Part 4 (Option 2 — Transfer and Construction Arrangements) above the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Community Building and Community Building Land until Management Company has been appointed to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf.

**7** No more than 4,200 Dwellings of the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site shall be Occupied:

**7.1.1** until a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Community Hub has been issued; and

**7.1.2** subject to the provisions of paragraph 8 of this Part 4 (Option 2 Transfer and Construction Arrangements) of this Schedule where the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to either SCDC or an Alternative Body subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule until the Owners have executed and delivered to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable) the Community Building Land Transfer; or

**7.1.3** where pursuant to paragraph 4.2.2 of this Part 4 (Option 2 — Transfer and Construction Arrangements) above the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to a Management Company subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule the Community Building Land Transfer has been completed; or

**7.1.4** where pursuant to paragraph 4.2.1 of this Part 4 (Option 2 — Transfer and Construction Arrangements) above the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Community Building and Community Building Land until Management Company has been appointed to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf.

**8** In the event that SCDC or the Town Council declines or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to SCDC it the relevant Community Building Land and the relevant Community Building within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall either:

**8.1** Where the offer has been made to SCDC the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends either to

**8.1.1** transfer the Community Building Land to an Alternative Body subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule in which case any such notification the Owners shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Community Building Land and the Community Building; or

**8.1.2** transfer the Community Building Land to a Management Company subject to Part 5 (Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company) of this Schedule; or

**8.1.3** retain the Community Building Land and Community Building and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf

AND the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Community Building Land and Community Building in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 8.1 of this Part 4 (Option 2 Transfer and Construction Arrangements) of this Schedule PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

**8.2** Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body and the Alternative Body declines or does not respond to the offer the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends to either:

**8.2.1** transfer the Community Building Land to a Management Company; or

**8.2.2** retain the Community Building Land and Community Building and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf;

And the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Community Building Land and Community Building in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to this paragraph 8.2 of this Part 4 (Option 2 — Transfer and Construction Arrangements) of this Schedule.

**9** The Owners shall pay the Community Building Revenue Contribution to SCDC in the following instalments:

**9.1.1** 33% to be paid to SCDC 9 months prior to Occupation of 4,200 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site;

**9.1.2** 33% to be paid to SCDC on or before the date which is 12 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 9.1 of this Part 4;

**9.1.3** The balance to be paid to the County Council on or before the date which is 24 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 9.1 of this Part 4

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to SCDC.

**10** The Owners shall pay the Library and Lifelong Learning Capital Contribution to the County Council no later than 9 months prior to the occupation of 1,501 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

**11** The Owners shall pay the Library and Lifelong Learning Revenue Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**11.1** 33% to be paid to the County Council 9 months prior to Occupation of 1501 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site

**11.2** 33% to be paid to the County Council on or before the date which is 12 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 2.1 of Part 4 of the Second Schedule
