**11.3** The balance to be paid to the County Council on or before the date which is 24 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 2.1 of Part 4 of the Second Schedule

PROVIDED ALWAYS AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT THAT the Owners shall not Occupy nor permit the Occupation on the Site of any Dwelling that would result in more than the specified number of Dwellings being Occupied unless and until the instalment related to that specific number of Dwellings has been paid to the County Council.

### Part 5 — Transfer of Land to an Alternative Body or Management Company

**1** Where the Community Buildings (or relevant part) are to be transferred by the Owners to an Alternative Body or a Management Company pursuant to this Schedule 8 the Owners shall transfer it on the following terms:

**1.1** the transfer shall be of the leasehold interest and it shall be for no more than 10 years; or

**1.2** the Owners shall retain the freehold interest in the Community Buildings (or relevant part) and the Owners shall appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Community Buildings (or relevant part) on the Owners' behalf; and

**1.3** Owners shall be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete such leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible following the date of appointment of the Management Company and if within the first 6 months of the said appointment of the Management Company the leasehold transfer has not been completed the Owners shall, if requested, be required subject to SCDC acting reasonably in making any such request, provide evidence to demonstrate why there is any continuing delay in completion of the leasehold interest being transferred and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall thereafter continue to be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete the leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible.

**1.4** In the event that a Management Company is appointed by the Owners to manage the Community Buildings (or relevant part) or the Management Company or an Alternative Body takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Community Buildings (or relevant part) then after a period of 10 years of the earlier from (i) the date a Management Company is appointed by the Owners or (ii) the leasehold transfer to the Management Company is completed the following shall apply:

**1.4.1** the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Community Building Land (or relevant part) to SCDC or if SCDC so nominates the Town Council provided the Town Council has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land and in either case for nil consideration pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer and in the event SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Community Buildings to the Management Company;

**1.4.2** in the event of a transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company, if a Town Council has not been properly constituted and SCDC is not willing to accept the offer of transfer then the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it remains in the Community Buildings to the Management Company.

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC or the Town Council save that the Owners and SCDC or the Town Council shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

### Part 6 — Requirements to be Observed by the Owners When Constructing the Community Building

**1** The Owners shall notify the Surveyor of all formal site meetings relating to the construction or equipping of the Community Buildings (or relevant part).

**2** The Owners shall permit the Surveyor:

**2.1** to attend and to put forward his/her views at all site meetings during the course of construction and fitting out/equipping of the Community Buildings (or relevant part);

**2.2** on reasonable notice to inspect the Community Buildings (or relevant part) during the course of construction/fitting out;

**2.3** to suggest items for inclusion in any necessary schedule of defects issued to the contractor in respect of the Community Buildings (or relevant part) and the Owners acknowledge that the costs of remedying any Defects shall be undertaken at the Owners' expense and shall not be taken into account in determining if the Community Building Cost Cap has been reached or exceeded;

**2.4** to stipulate any items or areas where the Community Buildings (or relevant part) does not meet the final agreed Community Building Detailed Specification (which forms part of the contract for the construction of the Community Buildings (or relevant part) then SCDC may serve on the Owners a written notice which shall specify those parts of the Community Building Detailed Specification that have not been met and the Owners shall within 20 Working Days from the date of service of such notice (or such other period as SCDC may agree) carry out such works as are necessary to secure compliance with the Community Building Detailed Specification at the expense of the Owners and the Owners shall fund any and all reasonable subsequent inspections of the Community Buildings (or relevant part) until the approved Community Building Detailed Specification shall have been met PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the costs of such inspections do not result in the Community Building Cost Cap being exceeded.

**3** Not less than 20 Working Days prior to the anticipated issue of a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the Community Buildings (or relevant part) the Owners shall in writing invite the Surveyor to inspect the Community Buildings (or relevant part) and identify any Defects requiring attention.

**4** The Owners shall be entitled to issue the Certificate of Practical Completion if after the expiry of 40 Working Days after the invitation made pursuant to paragraph 3 of this Part 6 (Requirements to be Observed by the Owners When Constructing the Community Building) of this Schedule any of the following apply:

**4.1** the Surveyor has failed to inspect the Community Buildings (or relevant part); or

**4.2** the Surveyor has failed to notify the Owners in writing of any Defects such notification having been received by the Owners within the said 20 Working Days; or

**4.3** the Surveyor has indicated following an inspection there are no Defects to remedy; or

### Part 7 — Establishment of the Project Core Team

**1** SCDC, the County Council and the HCA shall as soon as reasonably practicable following the date of this Deed and no later than 6 months after the date of this Agreement establish the Community Project Core Team.

**2** The Community Project Core Team Terms of Reference shall be as set out in Part 8 of this Schedule.

**3** SCDC shall invite the County Council and the HCA or its nominee to all Community Project Core Team meetings that are convened whether such meetings are held in person or by telephone or videoconferencing.

**4** The frequency of the Community Project Core Team meetings shall be in accordance with the Community Project Core Team Terms of Reference.

**5** The Community Project Core Team meetings will continue to operate from the date of establishment until the Practical Completion of the Community Buildings.

**6** SCDC and the County Council shall consider, take into account, incorporate and action any proposals suggestions recommendations or comments made by the HCA whether the HCA make them in the Community Project Core Team meetings or writing served on SCDC and the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt the Community Project Core Team shall approve any actions variations amendments documentation and information before SCDC, the HCA and/or the contractor appointed to construct a Community Building take any such action, make such variation or amendment or finalise documentation or information or otherwise expend any monies related to tasks and stages referred to in the definition of Community Project Core Team in this Agreement.

**7** The Community Project Core Team shall consult the Public and Community Services Working Group and shall report to the New Communities Project Board for ratification of the Community Project Core Team decisions.

### Part 8 — Community Project Core Team Terms of Reference

SCDC the County Council and HCA have agreed to establish, as soon as reasonably practicable and in advance of any of the work, actions, decisions or otherwise as being within the Community Project Core Team Terms of Reference referred to below being undertaken or made, a Community Project Core Team of relevant qualified persons to deliver the actions necessary to deliver in the design and construction of the Community Buildings. The purpose of the Community Project Core Team is to oversee the design and construction of the Community Buildings and to ensure that SCDC the County Council and the HCA act collaboratively on the decisions that have to be made in delivery of the Community Buildings.

The Community Project Core Team shall consist of SCDC, the County Council and the HCA.

New Community Project Core Team members may be added as the needs of the Project requires by mutual agreement between the parties.

The Community Project Core Team will meet every 4 weeks while any of the work, actions, decisions or otherwise as being within the Community Project Core Team Terms of Reference are being undertaken unless decided otherwise by the parties on the Project Core Team.

Approvals by the Community Project Core Team shall unless stated otherwise be decisions approved/agreed by a majority of the parties on the Community Project Core Team (each party having one vote).

The terms of reference of the Project Core Team will be as follows:

   - **(a)** to approve the design brief for the Community Buildings Detailed Specifications and to approve the appointment of the designer;
   - **(b)** to approve any changes to the design brief;
   - **(c)** to approve the Community Buildings Detailed Specifications;
   - **(d)** to approve the Community Buildings scheme design;
   - **(e)** to approve the timetable for the design, procurement and construction of the Community Buildings;
   - **(f)** to approve the contractor for the Community Buildings;
   - **(g)** to receive / review monthly update reports on progress provided by the Community Buildings project manager/contractor;
   - **(h)** to approve the details of the Reserved Matters Application, and its accompanying drawings, plans, specifications and other documents in order to secure the Reserved Matters Approval or other form of planning approval for the Community Buildings; and
   - **(i)** input into the Town Centre Strategy.

## Schedule 9 — Allotments, Orchards and Burial Land

### Relevant Definitions

**"Agreement for the Allotment Land Lease"** means an agreement for the Allotment Land Lease to be exchanged between the Owners and the Managing Body in accordance with paragraph 9 below;

**"Allotments"** means the allotments which will be laid out as part of the Development and completed in accordance with the Allotments Specification;

**"Allotments Certificate"** means the certificate issued by or on behalf of SCDC confirming the works to construct and complete the Allotments have been completed to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC;

**"Allotment Cost Cap"** means the sum of £75,000 (seventy-five thousand pounds);

**"Allotment Land"** means the area or areas of land within the Site to be laid out as Allotments the location and boundaries of which are to be identified through the Design Codes to be submitted pursuant to Planning Condition 9 of the Planning Permission and which when combined with the Orchard Land shall be not less than 1.57 hectares in size (unless otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners);

**"Allotment Land Lease"** means a grant by the Owners of an unencumbered leasehold interest in the Allotment Land (or where the Allotments are to be delivered across more than one site and the parties agree to a phased granting of the leasehold interest the Relevant Phase Allotment Land) to the Managing Body; and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such lease but such lease shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict use as Allotments and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and

   - **(b)** not include any terms which would directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

   - **(c)** include any reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over Allotment Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site where such rights are reserved for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of Schedule 9, Part 1 (Allotment Land Location and Specification) paragraph 6.1.1 and paragraph 6.1.2; and

   - **(d)** include for the benefit of the Allotment Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services (in relation to water supply only) over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Allotment Land for its purpose as allotments or public open space;

   - **(e)** be at nil consideration;

   - **(f)** include a restriction on use such that the Allotment Land shall not be used for any purpose other than as allotments (as such term is commonly understood) or as public open space and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto to serve the residents of the Development only;

**"Allotments Programme"** means a programme for the laying out and completion of the Allotments on the Relevant Phase Allotment Land and the aim of such programme shall be to deliver completion of the Allotments on the Relevant Phase Allotment Land at the same time as the Dwellings in any Development Parcel(s) adjacent to the Relevant Phase Allotment Land are first being Occupied;

**"Allotments Specification"** means the specification for the Allotments which shall be agreed with SCDC in accordance with this schedule and shall specify how the Allotments are to be constructed, laid out including the servicing, the provision of any enclosure(s), and any provision for sheds or other structures to be utilised by users of the Allotments;

**"Burial Land"** means the area of land within the Site to be identified and delivered as a burial ground pursuant to Planning Condition 62 of the Planning Permission;

**"Burial Land Certificate"** means the certificate issued by or on behalf of SCDC confirming the works to construct and complete the delivery of a burial ground on the Burial Land have been completed to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC;

**"Defects"** means any defects in relation to the laying out and completion of the Allotments in accordance with the Allotments Specification and including the provision of any vehicular access and a single water supply to the Allotment Land each of which shall be fit for purpose;

**"Managing Body"** means SCDC, the Town Council or an Alternative Body nominated by SCDC to manage the Allotment Land or the Orchard Land as applicable;

**"Orchard Land"** means the area or areas of land within the Site to be laid out as orchards the location and boundaries of which are to be identified through the Design Codes to be submitted pursuant to Planning Condition 9 of the Planning Permission and which when combined with the Allotment Land shall be not less than 1.57 hectares in size (unless otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners);

**"Orchard Land Programme"** means a programme for the laying out and completion of the orchards on the Relevant Phase Orchard Land the aim of such programme shall be to deliver completion of the orchards on the Relevant Phase Orchard Land at the same time as the Dwellings in any Development Parcel(s) adjacent to the Relevant Phase Orchard Land are first being Occupied;

**"Relevant Phase Allotment Land"** means where the Allotment Land is to comprise more than one site and is to be phased the area of land to be laid out as Allotments for the relevant phase or where the Allotments are to come forward on a single site the area of land for that single site;

**"Relevant Phase Orchard Land"** means where the Orchard Land is to comprise more than one site and is to be phased the area of land to be laid out as orchard for the relevant phase or where the orchards are to come forward as single site the area of land for that single site.

### Part 1 — Allotment Land Location and Specification

**1** The broad location of the Allotment Land shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC as part of the submissions to discharge Planning Condition 9 of the Planning Permission.

**2** The exact location and boundaries of the Relevant Phase Allotment Land, the strategy for management of the same and the Allotments Programme shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC either:

**2.1** as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel(s) adjacent to or within which the Relevant Phase Allotment Land is located; or

**2.2** as a Reserved Matters Applications solely for the Relevant Phase Allotment Land.

**3** The Allotments Specification shall be submitted to SCDC either:

**3.1** at the same time as a Reserved Matters Applications for any Development Parcel adjacent to any Allotment Land; or

**3.2** at the same time as a Reserved Matters Application solely for any Allotment Land; or

**3.3** as a standalone submission to SCDC solely for the approval of the Allotments Specification which may be submitted to SCDC at any time prior to Reserved Matters Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation to any area of land that includes land which such Reserved Matters Applications seek to designate as the Allotment Land.

**4** The Allotments Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Allotments and in those circumstances the approval or deemed approval of the Allotments Specification will be considered as if the Allotments Specification has been made as a standalone submission as opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a consequence the approval or deemed approval of the Allotments Specification may occur at a different time to the approval of any Reserved Matters Application involving the Allotments.

**5** The Owners shall ensure that the Allotment Land is connected (as appropriate) to the drainage measures implemented for the rest of the Development and such that the drainage and the connection is fit for purpose unless the Owners shall have previously demonstrated to SCDC's reasonable satisfaction that the Allotments are sufficiently free draining such that additional drainage measures are not required.

**6** The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours:

**6.1.1** to locate any Utilities within a Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Allotment Land); and

**6.1.2** not to locate Utilities in under or over the Allotment Land which are outside the Service Corridor.

SAVE THAT if at any time prior to completion of the Allotment Land Transfer the Owners wish to locate Utilities in under or over the Allotment Land they shall first obtain the prior written approval or deemed approval of SCDC to the proposed location of such services and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the seeking of such approval.

**7** The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours not to locate any Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Allotment Land SAVE THAT if at any time prior to completion of the Allotment Land Transfer the Owners wish to locate the Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over they shall first obtain the prior written approval or deemed approval of SCDC to the proposed location and depth of such services and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the seeking of such approval.

**8** No later than the Occupation of any Dwelling on any Development Parcel adjacent to any Allotment Land the Owners shall offer to grant the Allotment Land Lease to the Managing Body and where the Managing Body is not SCDC to serve a copy of the offer on SCDC.

**9** On acceptance of the offer provided pursuant to paragraph 8 above to permit the Managing Body to negotiate and exchange an Agreement for the Allotment Land Lease PROVIDED THAT the Managing Body must exchange an Agreement for the Allotment Land with the Owners not later than 6 months of the date on which the offer in writing to enter into the Agreement for the Allotment Land Lease is given to the Managing Body by the Owners;

**10** Where the Managing Body has not exchanged contracts with the Owners in relation to the Allotments Land within 6 months of the date on which the offer in writing to enter into the Agreement for the Allotment Land Lease is given the obligation to provide the Allotments and grant the Allotment Land Lease pursuant to this Schedule shall cease to have effect and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall be entitled to use the Allotment Land for alternative use as Open Space and will not be required to lay out and construct the Allotments.

**11** The Owners will serve notify SCDC that it intends to commence works on the Allotments at least 20 working days before carrying out any works and shall thereafter permit the Surveyor on reasonable notice to inspect the Allotments during the course of laying out and completion.

**12** The Owners shall complete the Allotments on the Relevant Phase Allotment Land in accordance with the Allotments Specification and the Relevant Phase Allotment programme PROVIDED THAT the completion of the Allotments shall not require works that would result in a combined cost to lay out and complete the Allotments that would exceed the Allotments Cost Cap unless any excess in cost is procured from funding sources other than the Owners.

**13** Following completion of the Allotments on the Relevant Phase Allotment Land the Owners will serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Allotments and issue an Allotments Certificate within 20 working days confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction.

**13.1** If SCDC chooses to inspect the Allotments and identifies any Defects and necessary remedial work the Owners will:

**13.1.1** complete at their own cost such necessary remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and

**13.1.2** upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 13.1 of this Part 1 of this Schedule 9 and issue an Allotment Certificate confirming that the Allotments have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction.

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Allotments within 25 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue an Allotment Certificate within 25 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Allotment Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods. PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 13.1.1 and 13.1.2 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue an Allotment Certificate or an Allotment Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the Allotments.

### Part 2 — Orchards

**1** In relation to the Orchard Land subject to paragraph 2 of this Part 2 of Schedule 9 below the provisions set out in Part 1 of this Schedule 9 shall apply mutatis mutandis including paragraph 5 in Part 1 unless the Owners shall have previously demonstrated to SCDC's reasonable satisfaction that the Orchard Land is sufficiently free draining and suitable for such use and such that additional drainage measures are not required in relation to the Orchard Land.

**2** In relation to the Orchard Land the Owners may at any time prior to the transfer of such Orchard Land to the Managing Body elect that the Orchard Land be treated as part of the Open Space as regards to the provisions of Schedule 14 and for the purposes of the management of the Orchard Land and the Owners shall notify the Council in writing of such election.

### Part 3 — Burial Land

**1** The HCA shall ensure that the location, specification and a programme for the phased implementation for the Burial Land shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC as part of the submissions to discharge Planning Condition 62 of the Planning Permission and the HCA shall use all reasonable endeavours to agree the location and boundaries for the Burial Land with SCDC as soon as reasonably practical thereafter and then having agreed such location and boundaries to enter into a supplementary form of section 106 Agreement in the form attached as part 4 of Appendix B or in such other form as shall be agreed with SCDC whereby such land then becomes bound by the obligations in this Part 3 of Schedule 9 mutatis mutandis.

**2** The Owners shall ensure that the Burial Land is connected (as appropriate) to the drainage measures implemented for the rest of the Development and such that the drainage and the connection is fit for purpose unless the Owners shall have previously demonstrated to SCDC's reasonable satisfaction that the Burial Land is sufficiently free draining such that additional drainage measures are not required.

**3** The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours:

to locate any Utilities within a Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Burial Land); and

not to locate Utilities in under or over the Burial Land which are outside the Service Corridor.

**4** The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours not to locate any Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Burial Land.

**5** The Owners shall complete the Burial Land in accordance with the details and in accordance with the phased implementation programme submitted to and approved by SCDC as part of the submissions to discharge Planning Condition 62 of the Planning Permission.

**6** The Burial Land shall be retained as a burial ground in Perpetuity on the following terms and conditions:

**8.1** Subject to paragraph 8.2 of this Part 3 of Schedule 9 the Burial Land shall be made available for the burial or memorial of deceased individuals who have a local connection to Northstowe either because the deceased was at the time of their death or within 10 years of their death a resident of Northstowe or they are a parent sibling child or grandchild of a person who is a resident of Northstowe or they are a parent sibling child or grandchild of a deceased person buried or commemorated on the Burial Land;

**8.2** If it becomes clear to the Owners in agreement with SCDC at any time from the date of this Agreement that the Burial Land provides an oversupply of burial/memorial plots set against the demand of Northstowe as a whole the Owners shall be free to offer any oversupply of burial/memorial plots to a wider catchment area.

**8.3** The burial/memorial plots on the Burial Land shall be made available at a price commensurate with burial and memorial prices typical of the local area and which prices are no higher than those in relation to any burial ground(s) which are owned and/or managed by a body other than a private enterprise.

**8.4** The Burial Land shall be kept in a good state of repair and properly looked after in terms of paths and planted areas being suitably maintained and the Owners shall use all reasonable endeavours to ensure that the Burial land is reasonably kept clear of litter.

**8.5** The Owners shall be entitled to:

   - **(a)** retain the freehold interest and maintain the Burial Land and appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the Burial Land on the Owners' behalf; or

   - **(b)** transfer a freehold or leasehold interest of the Burial Land to a company, body or organisation with experience of maintaining and managing burial grounds PROVIDED THAT the provisions of paragraph 8.3 above shall apply equally following such transfer.

## Schedule 10 — Community Support and Development

### Relevant Definitions

**"Community Endowment Contribution"** means the sum of £100,000 (one hundred thousand pounds) to be used solely towards Community Endowment Purposes;

**"Community Endowment Purposes"** means any purposes associated with building and serving the community at Northstowe (i) by or on behalf of SCDC performing the role of a town council ahead of the Town Council for Northstowe being formed; and/or (ii) by a Town Council for Northstowe and the use of the funds and any specific projects shall be decided by the Councils before any part of the Community Endowment Contribution is spent having first liaised with the Owners;

**"Community Support Contribution"** means the sum of £1,000,000 (one million British pounds) to be paid by the Owners to the County Council on an instalment basis to be used solely towards the Community Support Purposes;

**"Community Support Purposes"** means any purposes associated with placemaking and building a community within Phase 2 of Northstowe by supporting new residents moving into Phase 2 of Northstowe through establishing and supporting community connections such as social networks and community-led support groups, and providing preventative and early support to individuals and/or families who are vulnerable or have difficulty adjusting to the new community and such purposes to be primarily (unless otherwise agreed) through (i) the appointment of a Community Support Team which shall include community development and other specialist workers to specifically support Northstowe residents adjust and thrive in their new community, (ii) furnishing and equipping of a Children's Centre or (iii) other equivalent facilities, supporting the establishment of community groups and (iv) funding activities and events. The specific projects and the use of the funds within those matters listed above will be determined and approved by the Councils having first liaised with the Owners;

**"Small Grants Scheme (Community Chest) Contribution"** means the sum of £30,000 (thirty thousand pounds) to be used solely towards the Small Grants Scheme (Community Chest) Purposes;

**"Small Grants (Community Chest) Purposes"** means any purposes associated with the payment of grants to community or local groups supporting Northstowe residents. The specific projects to be awarded grants will be determined and approved by the Councils having first liaised with the Owners;

**"Town Boards Contribution"** means the sum of £42,500 (forty-two thousand and five hundred pounds) to be used towards the Town Boards Purposes;

**"Town Boards Purposes"** means the costs of procuring and erecting notice boards for a town council or parish council related to Northstowe (or until such time as the town council has been established SCDC) to display notices and other information of relevance to residents of and visitors to Northstowe;

### COMMUNITY SUPPORT CONTRIBUTION

**1.1** The Owners shall pay to the County Council the Community Support Contribution in the following instalments:

   - **1.1.1** £100,000 (one hundred thousand British pounds) prior to Implementation;

   - **1.1.2** £900,000 (nine hundred thousand British pounds) prior to Occupation of the first Dwelling;

**1.2** Implementation shall not take place until the payment in paragraph 1.1.1 above has been made by the Owners to the County Council.

**1.3** No Dwellings shall be Occupied until the payment referred to in paragraph 1.1.2 above has been made by the Owners to the County Council.

### COMMUNITY ENDOWMENT CONTRIBUTION

**2.1** Prior to the formation of a shadow town council for Northstowe or other similar body the Owners shall pay to SCDC the Community Endowment Contribution in the following instalments:

   - **2.1.1** £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) on the later of the date of Commencement and the date of submission to SCDC of a Reserved Matters Application which includes Dwellings;

   - **2.1.2** £30,000 (thirty thousand British pounds) on the earlier of the Opening Date of the First Primary School or the Opening Date of the Library/Health Facility to the public;

   - **2.1.3** £30,000 (thirty thousand British pounds) on the date which is 12 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 2.1.2 of this Schedule 10;

   - **2.1.4** £30,000 (thirty thousand British pounds) on the date which is 24 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 2.1.2 of this Schedule 10;

**2.2** Implementation shall not take place until the payment in paragraph 2.1.1 above has been made by the Owners to the SCDC.

**2.3** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the earlier of the Opening Date of the First Primary School or the Opening Date of the Library/Health Facility to the public until the payment referred to in paragraph 2.1.2 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**2.4** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the date which is 12 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 2.1.2 of this Schedule 10 until the payment referred to in paragraph 2.1.3 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**2.5** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the date which is 24 calendar months after the date referred to in paragraph 2.1.2 of this Schedule 10 until the payment referred to in paragraph 2.1.4 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

### TOWN BOARDS CONTRIBUTIONS

**3.1** The Owners shall pay to SCDC the Town Boards Contribution in the following instalments:

   - **3.1.1** £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) on the later of the date of Commencement and the date of submission to SCDC of a Reserved Matters Application which includes Dwellings;

   - **3.1.2** £10,833 (ten thousand and eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) prior to Occupation of the first Dwelling;

   - **3.1.3** £10,833 (ten thousand and eight hundred and thirty-three British pounds) on the first anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling;

   - **3.1.4** £10,834 (ten thousand and eight hundred and thirty-four British pounds) on the second anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling;

**3.2** Commencement shall not take place until the payment in paragraph 3.1.1 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**3.3** No Dwellings shall be Occupied until the payment referred to in paragraph 3.1.2 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**3.4** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the first anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling until the payment referred to in paragraph 3.1.3 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**3.5** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the second anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling until the payment referred to in paragraph 3.1.4 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

### SMALL GRANTS SCHEME (COMMUNITY CHEST)

**4.1** The Owners shall pay to SCDC the Small Grants Scheme (Community Chest) Contribution in the following instalments:

   - **4.1.1** £7,500 (seven thousand and five hundred pounds) prior to Occupation of the first Dwelling;

   - **4.1.2** £7,500 (seven thousand and five hundred pounds) on the first anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling;

   - **4.1.3** £7,500 (seven thousand and five hundred pounds) on the second anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling; and

   - **4.1.4** £7,500 (seven thousand and five hundred pounds) on the third anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling.

**4.2** No Dwellings shall be Occupied until the payment referred to in paragraph 4.1.1 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**4.3** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the first anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling until the payment referred to in paragraph 4.1.2 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**4.4** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the second anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling until the payment referred to in paragraph 4.1.3 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

**4.5** No further Dwellings shall be Occupied after the third anniversary of the Occupation of the first Dwelling until the payment referred to in paragraph 4.1.4 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

## Schedule 11 — Land for Faith/Voluntary Groups

**Relevant Definitions:**

**"Faith/Voluntary Groups"** means any faith voluntary or community group which are capable of providing faith provision and/or community services and/or other services suitable for provision by any such group to the residents of the Development and which group(s) SCDC, the Alternative Body and/or the Town Council as relevant (in consultation with the Councils jointly) has approved to use the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and "Faith Groups" and "Voluntary Groups" shall be construed accordingly;

**"Faith/Voluntary Groups Land"** means:

   - **(a)** the area of Serviced land within the Site in a location to be identified through the Town Centre Strategy, the Economic Strategy and the Phasing Plan (in consultation with the councils jointly) and which shall be predominantly located in the Town Centre and capable of providing not less than 1,000 sq.m Gross Internal Area and no more than 1,050 sq.m gross internal Area of a stand-alone facility together with provision for or appropriate access to parking provision for disabled users of the facility; and

   - **(b)** an area or areas of Serviced land within the Open Space Land to provide facilities for Faith/Voluntary Groups the location of which shall be identified in the Phasing Plan having regard to the needs of the Faith/Voluntary Groups that have specified through the councils or otherwise; an interest in taking up space at the Development;

**"Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate"** means the certificate issued by SCDC confirming the works to Service the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land have been completed to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC or where the design or location of the facility makes an unencumbered leasehold interest more appropriate because it comprises floorspace within a building then the certificate shall confirm that the building has been constructed and completed such that the facility is fit for its intended purpose and such that it has been completed to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC;

**"Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer"** means the transfer by the Owners of:

   - **(a)** the unencumbered freehold interest in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land in the event that it is transferred to SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC for the construction of a facility that will be a stand-alone facility unless the design or location of the facility makes an unencumbered leasehold interest more appropriate (as agreed by the Owners and SCDC); or

   - **(b)** the unencumbered leasehold interest (for a term of not less than 125 years but with break clauses of not less than every 5 years in favour of the tenant only) in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land in the event that it comprises the provision of floorspace within a building or is land for a stand-alone facility transferred to an Alternative Body in accordance with paragraph 4.1 of this Schedule 11 below or the design or location of the facility otherwise makes an unencumbered leasehold interest more appropriate (as agreed by the Owners and SCDC);

   - **(c)** in the case of 1 above in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 20(a) or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer but which shall nevertheless not include any terms which would restrict the use as Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and for the avoidance of doubt such use may include the construction of buildings on the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land; and

   - **(d)** not include any terms which would adversely affect (and whether directly or indirectly) the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

   - **(e)** include reservation of rights of access and services over the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraph 5.4 of this Schedule 11 below; and

   - **(f)** include for the benefit of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land for its purpose for the benefit of local faith/voluntary groups;

   - **(g)** be at nil consideration; and

   - **(h)** include a covenant that the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land shall not be used for any purpose other than for the benefit of local faith and voluntary groups or faith/voluntary/community groups associated with the Development and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and

   - **(i)** include a covenant that any building subsequently constructed on the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land shall be used for the benefit of local faith and voluntary groups or faith/voluntary/community groups associated with the Development and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto.

**FAITH/VOLUNTARY GROUPS**

**1.** The location of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land shall be identified by the Owners and approved by SCDC through the Town Centre Strategy, the Economic Strategy and the Phasing Plan (in consultation with the Councils jointly).

**2.** The exact boundaries for the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land or a relevant part thereof shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC as part of the Reserved Matters Application for (i) the first Development Parcel for which a Reserved Matters Application is being submitted and which is adjacent to the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Land or (ii) the Development Parcel within which the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land is located PROVIDED ALWAYS that the Owners may submit for approval each area of Faith/Voluntary Groups Land separately and differently to any other and nothing shall compel the Owners to provide the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land all together.

**3.** The Owners shall execute and deliver the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer prior to the Occupation of the 2250th Dwelling across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site unless otherwise agreed by way of revised triggers being informed by the Phasing Plan.

**4.** In the event that prior to Occupation of the 2250th Dwelling across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site (unless otherwise agreed by way of revised triggers being informed by the Phasing Plan) the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC offering to transfer the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to:

**4.1** SCDC, or

**4.2** (or if applicable to such Alternative Body be nominated by SCDC) provided such Alternative Body has been established and is operational to the reasonable satisfaction of the Owners and provided such Alternative Body is capable of accepting the transfer or if applicable at the lease of the relevant land

and in any event the offer of transfer under 4.1 and/or 4.2 shall be pursuant to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC provides written acceptance of the offer within a period of 40 Working Days then the actual Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer will be completed by the parties prior to Occupation of the 2250th Dwelling across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site (unless otherwise agreed by way of revised triggers being informed by the Phasing Plan).

**5.** In the event that SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which contains the following option that the Owners intend to pursue in respect of the leasehold interest of the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Land:

**5.1** Body as a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land the Owners shall execute and deliver to the Alternative Body a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Lease in accordance with paragraph 3 above but limited to a term of no more than 10 years.

**5.2** That the Owners wish to offer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to an Alternative Body nominated by the Owners PROVIDED THAT in such notification the Owners shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve such Alternative Body as a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land the Owners shall execute and deliver to the Alternative Body a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Lease in accordance with paragraph 3 above but limited to a term of no more than 10 years.

**5.2.1** Where SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Alternative Body nominated by the Owners decline or do not accept the offer to have transferred to it the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land as provided for in paragraphs 4 or 5.1 above; or

**5.2.2** SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Alternative Body nominated by the Owners (as applicable depending on which has accepted the offer) have not exchanged contracts with the Owners in relation to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land within 60 Working Days of contracts being issued by the Owners and provided the Owners have used Reasonable Endeavours on their part to achieve an exchange of contracts within such period;

the obligations of this Schedule shall subject to (i) the proviso in 5.2.2 above and (ii) paragraph 5.3 below cease to have effect unless any dispute in relation to this Schedule has been referred to an Expert for determination in accordance with clause 18 and the obligations shall subject to paragraph 5.3 below cease to have effect once the determination has been made by the Expert such that it does not result in the situations referred to in this paragraph 5.2 no longer applying and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall be entitled to use the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land for alternative uses which are planning approved.

**5.3** Irrespective of whether an Alternative Body nominated by the Owners takes a transfer of leasehold interest in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land pursuant to paragraph 5.1 or not then after a period of 10 years from the date of the leasehold transfer to the Alternative Body nominated by the Owners or 10 years after the Alternative Body have declined or not accepted such leasehold interest then the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC if the Alternative Body has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land) for nil consideration pursuant to the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Transfer and in the event that SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to an Alternative Body nominated by the Owners PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council (save that the Owners and SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

**5.4** The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours:

**5.4.1** to locate any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land); and

**5.4.2** not to locate Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land which are outside of the Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land)

SAVE THAT if at any time prior to completion of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer the Owners wish to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land they shall first obtain the prior written approval or deemed approval of SCDC to the proposed location of such Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the seeking of such approval.

**6.** Following completion of works to Service the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land or to complete additional works where the design or location of the facility is such that it comprises floorspace within a building the Owners will serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate within 40 working days confirming that the relevant works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction and:

**6.1.1** If SCDC chooses to inspect the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and identifies any necessary remedial works the Owners will complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and

**6.1.2** upon completion of any remedial work, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 6.1.1 of this Schedule 11 and issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate confirming that the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land has been provided to their reasonable satisfaction,

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land within 40 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 6.1.1 and 6.1.2 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate or a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land.

## Schedule 12 — Environment, Ordnance and Heritage

**Relevant Definitions:**

**"Air Quality Monitoring Contribution"** means the sum of £124,000 (One hundred and twenty-four thousand British pounds) to be used for Air Quality Monitoring Purposes.

**"Air Quality Monitoring Purposes"** means the implementation of a monitoring programme to be undertaken by SCDC to assess/verify the impact of the Development (if any) both during construction and operational phases on air quality on the area in the vicinity of the Site (and including the local road network) and as appropriate within the Site and which shall include the purchase installation, operation and maintenance of such equipment as shall be required to undertake such monitoring provided that such equipment shall be solely used in connection with the monitoring of the impact of the Development and to enable SCDC to fulfil its obligations under Local Air Quality Management as a result of the Development.

**"Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution"** means the sum of £50,000 (fifty thousand pounds) to fund the cost of the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes.

**"Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution Contingency Cap"** means a sum in addition to the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution of up to £100,000 (one hundred thousand pounds) to fund the cost of the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes.

**"Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes"** means the funding of a suitably qualified and experienced independent environmental consultant to review site investigation reports and all subsequent reports relating to ground contamination and remediation on the Site.

**"Heritage Core Team"** means a steering group comprised of representatives of the County Council (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Heritage Core Team), SCDC (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Heritage Core Team) the Owners or their nominee (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Heritage Core Team) and which group shall:

   - **(a)** explore and consider and draw up a Heritage Strategy for the Development;

   - **(b)** in developing the Heritage Strategy consider and agree alongside the Owners' development of the Phasing Strategy and Design Code pursuant to Planning Conditions 8 and 9 respectively the accommodation of display and storage facilities for the Longstanton District Heritage Society within part of the community facilities or public realm within the Development

PROVIDED THAT each party acting reasonably may be permitted to invite other interested parties to the meetings as agreed by the other parties in advance.

**"Heritage Core Team Terms of Reference"** means those matters referred to in paragraph 3.5 below which are the responsibilities and powers and extent of the Heritage Core Team's decision making.

**"Heritage Display and Interpretation Contribution"** means the sum of £45,000 (forty-five thousands pounds) (Index Linked) to be used for the purpose of providing the necessary infrastructure for the display and interpretation of historic artefacts from the Phase 1 and Phase 2 Site.

**"Heritage Strategy"** means a strategy for the educational resource(s) which can be used to inform the local population and the wider academic environment on the archaeological significance of the area and the history of Northstowe.

**"Longstanton District Heritage Society Display Facilities"** means the accommodation of display and storage facilities for the Longstanton District Heritage Society within part of the community facilities or public realm within the Development the details and location of which are to be developed as part of the Heritage Strategy.

**"Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Contribution"** means the sum of £31,625 (thirty one thousand six hundred and twenty five pounds) to be used for the purchase of Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes.

**"Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes"** means such noise and vibration monitoring equipment as shall be required to accurately verify the impact of the Development (if any) both during construction and operational phases on noise and vibration levels in the area in the vicinity of the Site and as appropriate within the Site provided that such equipment shall be solely used in connection with the monitoring of the impact of the Development.

**"Traffic Noise and Vibration Mitigation Figure"** means a sum up to £70,000 (seventy thousand pounds) to be used for the purpose of providing Traffic Noise and Vibration Mitigation.

**"Traffic Noise and Vibration Mitigation"** means off-site noise mitigation measures against traffic noise and vibration such as noise barriers and sound insulation, including possible ventilation of residential properties and other noise sensitive receivers should such measures be reasonably required as a consequence of the detailed monitoring undertaken using the noise and vibration monitoring equipment purchased for the Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event(s)"** means any evacuation of people and or livestock required as a result of the discovery of unexploded ordnance on the Site.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs Demand"** means a written demand sent jointly on behalf of SCDC and/or the County Council that sets out the respective costs incurred by or on behalf of both SCDC and/or the County Council including written justification and a detailed breakdown together with receipts setting out the separate and individual costs incurred by or on behalf of SCDC and the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt such a written demand shall only seek to recover costs in arrears PROVIDED THAT where either SCDC or the County Council do not incur Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs then such written demand shall state this and must still be submitted on behalf of both SCDC and the County Council so that the Owners have certainty that there are no additional costs outstanding or that will be claimed PROVIDED FURTHER THAT only one such written demand may be permitted to be made by SCDC and the County Council in relation to each Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event and which demand shall deal with any and all incurred Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs associated with that Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event SUBJECT ALWAYS that the combined costs that are the subject of any and all such written demands shall not seek to demand payment of more than the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Live Costs Cap.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs"** means any costs to SCDC and/or the County Council of directly supporting and resourcing any and all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events PROVIDED THAT SCDC and the County Council shall only be entitled to reimbursement of their costs incurred in the required resourcing for all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs Cap"** means the combined sum for the direct costs to SCDC and/or the County Council of directly supporting and resourcing all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events which shall not total more than £60,000 (sixty thousand British pounds) per Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event which shall be paid to SCDC and/or the County Council in such proportions as shall be set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Live Costs Demand.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs Demand"** means a written demand sent jointly on behalf of SCDC and the County Council that sets out the respective costs incurred by both SCDC and the County Council including written justification and a detailed breakdown together with receipts setting out the separate and individual costs incurred by SCDC and the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt such a written demand shall only seek to recover costs in arrears PROVIDED THAT where either SCDC or the County Council do not incur Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs then such written demand shall state this and must still be submitted on behalf of both SCDC and the County Council so that the Owners have certainty that there are no additional costs outstanding or that will be claimed PROVIDED FURTHER THAT only one such written demand may be permitted to be made by SCDC and the County Council and which shall deal with any and all incurred Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs SUBJECT ALWAYS that such written demand shall not seek to demand payment of more than the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Planned Costs Cap.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Planned Costs Cap"** means the combined sum for forward planning of all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events which shall not total more than £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) which shall be paid to SCDC and/or the County Council in such proportions as shall be set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs Demand.

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs"** means any costs of the forward planning of all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events PROVIDED THAT SCDC and the County Council shall only be entitled to reimbursement of their costs incurred in pre-planning for all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT SCDC and the County Council shall only be entitled to recover costs that are properly and reasonably incurred by appointed SCDC and/or County Council officers.

**Environmental Contributions**

**1.1** Prior to Commencement of any Development Parcel the Owners shall pay SCDC the Air Quality Monitoring Contribution to be used for Air Quality Monitoring Purposes only.

**1.2** Prior to Implementation the Owners shall pay SCDC the Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Contribution to be used for Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes only.

**1.3** Prior to Implementation the Owners shall pay to SCDC one-fifth of the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution to be used for the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes only.

**1.4** On the first, second, third and fourth anniversary of the date of Implementation the Owners shall pay to SCDC a further one-fifth of the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution to be used for the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes only Provided That if the cost to SCDC of providing the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes shall at any time exceed the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution SCDC shall be free to require the Owners to pay an additional amount to cover the actual cost to SCDC of providing the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes up to the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution Contingency Cap and the Owners shall pay to SCDC no later than 90 Days after receipt of evidence that the actual cost to SCDC of providing the Contaminated Land Consultant Purposes has exceeded the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution but provided always SCDC shall not be required to pay back any unspent part of either the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution or the Contaminated Land Consultant Contribution Contingency Cap until first Occupation of the final Dwelling permitted under the final Reserved Matters Application.

**1.5** If at any time after Implementation and up to one year from Occupation of the final Dwelling permitted under the final Reserved Matters Application the results of surveying from the noise and vibration monitoring equipment purchased for the Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes leads SCDC to conclude that any Traffic Noise and Vibration Mitigation is reasonably required the Owners shall (subject to any affected third party permitting access and subject to securing all necessary consents) implement such mitigation as shall first be agreed in writing by SCDC Provided That the total amount spent by or on behalf of the Owners in such mitigation shall not exceed the Traffic Noise and Vibration Mitigation Figure.

**Ordnance**

The Owners covenant with SCDC and the County Council:

**2.1** that if by Implementation or following Implementation a contract has been awarded by the Owners to undertake intrusive ground investigations on the Site including boreholes, trial boreholes, test pits and window samples and in consequence any Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event is required then the Owners will pay SCDC and the County Council their respective proportions as set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Planned Demand Notice of the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs within 40 Working Days of the receipt of the validly served Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Planned Costs Demand.

**2.2** to pay SCDC and the County Council their respective proportions as set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Live Costs Demand of the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs within 40 Working Days of the receipt of the validly served Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs Demand.

**Heritage**

**3.1** Prior to occupation of the 300th Dwelling on the Site the Owners shall pay to the County Council the Heritage Display and Interpretation to be used for Heritage Display and Interpretation Purposes only.

**3.2** The County Council, SCDC and the Owners shall as soon as reasonably practicable following the date of this Deed establish the Heritage Core Team PROVIDED THAT the County Council and SCDC shall not be permitted to undertake any work actions decisions or otherwise as are described as being within the Heritage Core Team Terms of Reference.

**3.3** The County Council and SCDC shall invite the Owners or their nominee to all Heritage Core Team meetings that are convened whether such meetings are held in person or by telephone or videoconferencing.

**3.4** The County Council covenants to convene Heritage Core Team meetings at least once every 3 months (or such lesser period as may be agreed between the parties).

**3.5** The Heritage Core Team shall operate in accordance with the following terms of reference:

**3.5.1** to explore and consider, draw up and agree a Heritage Strategy for the Development;

**3.5.2** In developing the Heritage Strategy to consider and agree (alongside the Owners' development of the Phasing Strategy and Design Code pursuant to Planning Conditions 8 and 9 respectively) the accommodation of display and storage facilities for the Longstanton District Heritage Society within part of the community facilities or public realm within the Development;

**3.5.3** to approve the details of the planning application, and its accompanying drawings, plans, specifications and other documents to be prepared by the Owners in order to secure the Reserved Matters Approval or other form of planning approval (if required) for the Longstanton District Heritage Society Display Facilities.

**3.6** The Heritage Core Team meetings will continue to operate from the date of establishment until the parties agree (acting reasonably) that there is no further role for the Heritage Core Team.

**3.7** The County Council shall consider, take into account, incorporate and action any proposals suggestions recommendations or comments made by the Owners whether the Owners make them in the Heritage Core Team meetings or writing served on the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt the Heritage Core Team shall approve any actions variations amendments documentation and information before the County Council take any such action, make such variation or amendment or finalise documentation or information or otherwise expend any monies related to task and stages referred to in the definition of Heritage Core Team in this Agreement.

**3.8** In bringing forward the Development the Owners shall accommodate the Longstanton District Heritage Society Display Facilities and shall provide or procure the necessary rights of access to the Longstanton District Heritage Society Display Facilities to enable the management and maintenance thereof.

## Schedule 13 — Fire and Rescue Services

**Relevant Definitions:**

**"Cambridgeshire Fire and Rescue Service (FRS)"** means the Fire and Rescue Service serving Cambridgeshire or any successor body;

**"Fire Strategy"** means the strategy to be agreed between the HCA and the Council in consultation with Cambridgeshire Fire and Rescue Service for measures to be provided as part of the Development to provide appropriate Fire Safety and prevention at the Development having regard to fire incident response times for the Development and fire standards at the time of approval of the Strategy and the need to protect residents such strategy may include the provision of a new emergency outstation (if requested by Cambridgeshire Fire and Rescue Service) or the provision of fire safety infrastructure to support vulnerable residents at the Development Provided That the total cost of providing a new emergency outstation and/or fire safety infrastructure shall not exceed the Fire Strategy Contribution;

**"Fire Strategy Contribution"** the difference between the sum of £1,820,000 (One million eight hundred and twenty thousand pounds) and the sums spent or committed to be spent pursuant to paragraph 1.1 of this Schedule 13 to be spent by SCDC solely on the Fire Strategy Purposes.

**FIRE STRATEGY**

The Owners covenant with the Councils that:

**1.1** No part of the Development shall Commence, apart from Enabling Works, Earthworks, Strategic Engineering Elements and Strategic Landscaping Elements, until a Fire Strategy has been submitted to and approved in writing by SCDC.

**1.2** When bringing forward the Development (save for the Enabling Works, Earthworks, Strategic Engineering Elements and Strategic Landscaping Elements) to implement the Fire Strategy approved pursuant to paragraph 1.1 of this Schedule 13 and to comply with any and all triggers for performance stipulated therein.

## Schedule 14 — Open Space

### Relevant Definitions

**"All Weather Pitch"** means a senior size short pile 3G (40mm) rubber crumb artificial grass pitch, also known as Synthetic Turf Pitch, designed by a specialist contractor in accordance with "Selecting the Right Artificial Pitch" which standards are jointly approved by English Hockey Board, The Football Association, The Football Foundation, The RFL, The RFU and Sport England or such variation or amendment to the standards or such other publication as may from time to time replace the standards and which may be jointly approved by the bodies for use in relation to artificial pitches.

**"Alternative Public Realm Funding"** means any means of funding the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Purposes other than by means of utilising HCA's own direct funds and which may include but not be limited to the following:

   - **(a)** Annual Maintenance Charge;
   - **(b)** hire charges levied on users of the Sports Pavilion and/or the Sports Pitches and other parts of the Community Facilities; and
   - **(c)** any grants or donations whether from public or private sources.

**"Annual Maintenance Charge"** means any annual maintenance charge or rent charge or other such financial charge imposed on and secured against the Dwellings whose specific purpose is to secure financial contributions by owners of the Dwellings towards the management and maintenance of all or any part of the Public Realm where such financial charges may be levied by the Owners or a Management Company responsible for the Public Realm to which the financial charge relates and which will be imposed from the date each Dwelling is Occupied for the first time.

**"Formal Park"** means the area of land being 1.2 hectares (unless otherwise agreed between the Owners and SCDC) within the Development whose location is shown indicatively on Plan 14 attached to this Agreement at Appendix 23 to be dedicated for use by the public as town park/town square within the Development and to be laid out in accordance with the Formal Park Specification.

**"Formal Park Specification"** means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the Formal Park on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a chartered member of the Registered Practice of the Landscape Institute (such Institute being a Charity registered in England and Wales number 1073396) which specifications shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement.

**"Incidental Open Space"** means any area of land immediately surrounding any part of the Open Space Land or On Site SUDS and land which would not otherwise form part of a garden of a Dwelling.

**"Initial Sports Pavilion Specification"** means the initial specification for the Sports Pavilion appended to this Agreement at Appendix 17.

**"LAP"** means a local area of play being an area of play within the Development the exact location and specification of which will be the subject of approval as part of Reserved Matters Approvals for use by children and young people and which for the avoidance of doubt shall not include the provision of any play equipment but which unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC shall be in accordance with paras 4.48 and 4.49 (inclusive) of SCDC's SPD on Open Space in New Developments (the "Open Space SPD").

**"LEAP"** means three (3) Local Equipped Areas for Play for children of early school age, the exact locations of which will be the subject of approval through the Youth and Play Strategy and as part of Reserved Matters Approvals which are to be designated and equipped in accordance with the approved LEAP Specification and which unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC shall be provided in accordance with:

   - **(a)** paras 4.50–4.55 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD; and
   - **(b)** the locational and design criteria set out in the Fields in Trust document "Guidance for Outdoor Play and Sports: Beyond the Six Acre Standard October 2015" which directly relate to the provision of a LEAP and provided that no regard shall be had to the content of any documents or other sources which may be referred to in the Fields in Trust document in order to set criteria and provided always that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between:
      - **(i)** the Open Space SPD; and
      - **(ii)** the Fields in Trust document "Guidance for Outdoor Play and Sports: Beyond the Six Acre Standard October 2015" then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence.

**"LEAP Specification"** means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the LEAPs on the Site which specification shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement.

**"NEAP"** means a Neighbourhood Equipped Area for Play and which is mainly for older children to be located within the area shown indicatively on Plan 9 attached to this Agreement at Appendix 23 and the exact location of which will be the subject of approval through the Youth and Play Strategy and as part of Reserved Matters Approvals and which NEAP is to be designated and equipped in accordance with the approved NEAP Specification and which unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC shall be provided in accordance with:

   - **(a)** paras 4.56–4.63 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD; and
   - **(b)** the locational and design criteria set out in the Fields in Trust document "Guidance for Outdoor Play and Sports: Beyond the Six Acre Standard October 2015" which directly relate to the provision of NEAPs and provided that no regard shall be had to the content of any documents or other sources which may be referred to in the Fields in Trust document in order to set criteria and provided always that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between:
      - **(i)** the Open Space SPD; and
      - **(ii)** the Fields in Trust document "Guidance for Outdoor Play and Sports: Beyond the Six Acre Standard October 2015" then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence.

**"NEAP Specification"** means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the NEAP on the Site which specification shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement.

**"Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Purposes"** means any management and/or maintenance work reasonably required to be carried out in respect of the Public Realm.

**"Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy"** means a strategy to be approved or deemed approved by SCDC which shall:

   - **(a)** be such as to ensure the effective management and maintenance of each and all of the areas forming parts of the Public Realm, promoting matters including accessibility to the Public Realm;
   - **(b)** incorporate details setting out a Landscape Management and Maintenance Plan as required by Planning Condition 24 of the Planning Permission;
   - **(c)** set out a comprehensive approach to management of the Public Realm; and
   - **(d)** promote community engagement and consultation in respect of the Public Realm.

**"Northstowe Sports Purpose"** means towards the provision of the Sports Pitches.

**"Open Space Land"** means the Play Areas, the Sports Pitches, the Formal Park and any Incidental Open Space which is not to go to a Dwelling or plot purchaser.

**"Open Space Land Transfer"** means the transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered freehold or leasehold interest in any Play Area, or part of the Sports Pitches or Formal Park in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 21 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer but which shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict public access save for the purposes of maintenance works, ecological or similar environmental requirements or in the case of emergency; and
   - **(b)** not include any terms which would directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
   - **(c)** include any reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Play Area Sports Pitches or Formal Park (as applicable) for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity; and
