   - **(d)** include for the benefit of the Play Area Sports Pitches or Formal Park (as applicable) the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services which are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Play Area Sports Pitches or Formal Park (as applicable) over any adjoining land for its intended purpose as set out in this Schedule; and be at nil consideration; and
   - **(e)** in relation to LEAPs and SIPs, NEAP and Formal Park that the LEAPs and SIPs, NEAP and Formal Park shall not be used for any purpose other than for a publicly accessible free at the point of use games area or publicly accessible free at the point of use recreational facilities; and
   - **(f)** in relation to Sports Pitches: that the Sports Pitches shall not be used for any purpose other than for publicly accessible sport and recreation facilities; and
   - **(g)** in relation to any LAP: that the LAP shall not be used for any purpose other than for public open space.

**"Play Areas"** means collectively the LAPs LEAPs, SIPs, and the NEAP to be located on the Site.

**"Public Realm"** means those areas, land and buildings within the Site as set out below:

   - **(a)** the Sports Pavilion as defined in this Schedule;
   - **(b)** the Orchard Land as defined in Schedule 9; and
   - **(c)** the Open Space Land (which shall include the Allotments in the event of paragraph 10 of Schedule 9 becoming applicable).

**"SIPs"** means a Space for Imaginative Play to complement the LEAPs the exact locations of which will be the subject of approval through the Youth and Play Strategy and as part of Reserved Matters Approvals which unless otherwise agreed with SCDC are to be designated in accordance with:

   - **(a)** paras 4.64–4.66 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD;
   - **(b)** chapter D10 of the Northstowe Area Action Plan (adopted July 2007); and
   - **(c)** the approved SIP Specification and based on the locational and design criteria set out in sections 6.1 and 6.2 of the Fields Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008" which directly relate to provision of a SIP and provided that no regard shall be had to the content of any documents or other sources which may be referred to in these documents in order to set criteria and provided always that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between:
      - **(i)** the Open Space SPD; and
      - **(ii)** the Fields in Trust document "Guidance for Outdoor Play and Sports: Beyond the Six Acre Standard October 2015" then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence.

**"Sports Hub East"** means that part of the Site edged green on the east side of Plan 5 at Appendix 23.

**"Sports Hub West"** means that part of the Site edged green on the west side on Plan 5 at Appendix 23.

**"Sports Pavilion"** means a building together with associated facilities and to have an internal floor space of 636.25 square metres (unless otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners) to be provided within part of the Eastern Sports Hub and which land building and associated facilities are to be used for sports and recreational purposes together with ancillary bar and/or function room together with associated car parking facilities provided that for the avoidance of doubt SCDC and the Owners agree that nothing in this definition or Schedule shall prevent SCDC from extending part or parts of the Sports Pavilion by way of future ground floor extensions outside the scope of this Agreement which extension(s) for the avoidance of doubt shall not be at the Owners' cost and shall not be covered by any Contribution payable pursuant to this Agreement.

**"Sports Pavilion Contribution"** means the capital sum of £1,500,000 (one million five hundred thousand British pounds) and the revenue sum of £135,000 (one hundred and thirty-five thousand British pounds) which is to be paid by the Owners to SCDC and which can be used by SCDC for the Sports Pavilion Contribution Purpose.

**"Sports Pavilion Contribution Purpose"** means as to the capital sum towards the construction completion and fitting out of the Sports Pavilion and as to the revenue sum for the maintenance and management of the Sports Pavilion.

**"Sports Pavilion Costs"** means those costs applicable to the Sports Pavilion which shall be permitted to be included within any calculation to establish whether the Sports Pavilion Costs Cap has been exceeded shall include:

   - **(a)** any costs arising in connection with any construction contract let in connection with the construction and completion of the Sports Pavilion which shall also include the provision of landscaping and access arrangements internal to the Sports Pavilion Land, boundary treatments and car parking and for the avoidance of doubt unless such costs are already covered in the main construction contract the costs shall include costs of any inspections by the Surveyor as provided for in this Schedule 8 and the costs of inspections by such other person or company undertaking inspections of the Sports Pavilion for the purpose of issuing the Certificate of Practical Completion and/or the Certificate of Final Completion; and
   - **(b)** abnormal costs agreed by SCDC acting reasonably which shall include responding within a reasonable timescale having regard to any programme including but not limited to acoustic mitigation, external works and retaining structures, disabled ramps and lifts, foundations to cater for slopes on the Sports Pavilion Land to accommodate for gradients on and adjacent to this land, measures to address potential for flooding;
   - **(c)** any reasonable commitment fees, procurement fees, finance charges and any interest payable in respect of the Sports Pavilion;
   - **(d)** design costs including all professional fees associated with the Sports Pavilion:
      - **(i)** where the contract is a design and build contract 12% of the total contract value; or
      - **(ii)** 12% of the total cost cap;
   - **(e)** statutory fees;
   - **(f)** costs associated with requirements from the statutory undertakings;
   - **(g)** cost of fixtures and fittings and equipment;
   - **(h)** such reasonable legal agents and consultancy fees (whether such fees relate to the Owners' (or SCDC's if the Sports Pavilion is constructed by SCDC) internal staff or employees or external advisors) as may be associated with the entering into of the construction contract or any other contracts for the design, construction, completion, fitting out and equipping of the Sports Pavilion,

PROVIDED THAT the parties acknowledge and agree that in identifying the Sports Pavilion Costs as defined by this definition no element of double counting of elements of costs shall be permitted.

**"Sports Pavilion Cost Cap"** means the total cost of providing the Sports Pavilion pursuant to the approved Sports Pavilion Specification which shall not exceed £1,500,000 (one million five hundred thousand British pounds) and the Sports Pavilion Costs shall be permitted to be included within any calculation to establish whether the said cap has been exceeded.

**"Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification"** means a detailed specification which shall include the building floorspace, the height of the building, the number of storeys, the number of car parking spaces, the total site area, other relevant size details, materials, equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the Sports Pavilion on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in preparing and reviewing specifications for such Sports Pavilions which specification shall be submitted and approved by SCDC which specification shall include room shapes sizes and heights as well as details of external and internal work specifications including services as may be required and are appropriate pursuant the provisions of this Agreement and subject to there being sufficient funds available within the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap shall provide for a reasonable level of internal fitting out work and equipment consistent with the intended use as a Sports Pavilion provided that the specification shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Sports Pavilion that would exceed the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap and provided further that the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification shall be substantially in accordance with the Initial Sports Pavilion Specification.

**"Sports Pavilion Land"** means the area of land Serviced within the Site not less than 0.22 hectares in size and more than 0.4 hectares in size to be provided by the Owners in a location within the Eastern Sports Hub and the final area to be approved in accordance with the provisions of this Schedule and provided that in any event the Sports Pavilion Land shall be accessed by a road which is an adopted highway or which is or is intended to be the subject of a section 38 highway Agreement as to future adoption.

**"Sports Pavilion Land Transfer"** means a transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered freehold in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 18 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the Sports Pavilion Land on terms to be agreed and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer but which shall nevertheless:

   - **(a)** not include any terms which would restrict public access; and
   - **(b)** not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
   - **(c)** subject to the provisions of paragraph 5 of Part 12 include the reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Sports Pavilion Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity; and
   - **(d)** include for the benefit of the Sports Pavilion Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services which are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Sports Pavilion Land over any adjoining land for its intended purpose as set out in this Schedule; and
   - **(e)** be at nil consideration; and
   - **(f)** include a covenant that the Sports Pavilion that has or will be constructed on the Sports Pavilion Land shall after construction subject to suitable funding be maintained for the benefit of the general public arising from the Development; and
   - **(g)** the Sports Pavilion Land shall not be used for any other purpose other than for sports, community and recreation activities and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto or which are directly associated and ancillary facilities and which may include any ancillary bar and/or function room that may be constructed; and
   - **(h)** provisions dealing with the arrangements set out in paragraph 5 of Part 2 of this Schedule.

**"Sports Pitches"** means sites for formal sports pitch space which unless otherwise agreed with SCDC are to be provided in accordance with paras 4.10–4.32 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD (located on the Site with a proper and adequate (i.e. fit for purpose) means of access provided, and which shall be further detailed within the Sports Pitches Specification and which shall include the following and within the areas specified:

   - **(a)** Eastern Sports Hub
      - 4 mini football pitches;
      - 2 junior pitches;
      - 1 senior football pitch;
      - 1 All Weather Pitch (Synthetic Artificially Turfed Pitch);
      - 1 Skate Park;
      - 1 Cricket pitch (as part of Eastern Sports Hub) and with the cricket square of such pitch being made of artificial turf to a specification approved by SCDC and which square shall provide 8 wickets across the square;
   - **(b)** Sports Pavilion Land
      - 4 full size Courts Tennis/netball;
   - **(c)** Western Sports Hub
      - 2 junior pitches;
      - 2 senior football pitches;
   - **(d)** Area not specified
      - 1 BMX track,

and provided always that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between:

   - **(i)** the Open Space SPD; and
   - **(ii)** the standards set out by Sport England in relation to such Sports Pitches

then the standards set out by Sport England shall take precedence.

**"Sports Pitches Specification"** means the specification for the design laying out equipping and maintaining each of the Sports Pitches to the standards set out by Sport England in relation to such Sports Pitches or as agreed by SCDC which specification shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement and which shall where relevant also cover the following:

   - **(a)** details of sports surface (where applicable);
   - **(b)** minimum run-off areas to relevant governing body specifications;
   - **(c)** including the courts for tennis/netball use being for tennis as the primary sport and netball as the secondary sport;
   - **(d)** details of line markings; and
   - **(e)** details of post sockets for relevant equipment.

**"Sports Revenue Contributions"** means the following sums:

   - **(a)** £135,000 which forms part of the Sports Pavilion Contribution;
   - **(b)** £170,000 as the Indoor Sports Building Revenue Contribution; and
   - **(c)** £270,000 as a Sports Hub East Revenue Contribution and/or a Sports Hub West Revenue Contribution PROVIDED THAT the total of such contribution(s) is no greater than £270,000

payable in accordance with Part 11 of this Schedule 14.

**"Sports Strategy"** means a strategy setting out the provision of the Sports Pavilion the Sports Pitches and other associated facilities including details of implementation of such provision and facilities.

**"Surveyor"** means a Surveyor appointed and instructed by the SCDC/CCC at their expense to inspect the installation layout out and completion of the Town Park, Sports Pitches, LEAPs and NEAPs.

**"Youth and Play Strategy"** means a strategy setting out the provision of the Formal Park, Play Areas and other associated facilities including details of implementation of such provisions and facilities.

### Part 1 — Sports Strategy

**1** Prior to or simultaneously with submitting the first Reserved Matters Application for any Development Parcel within which Dwellings are to be constructed the Owners shall submit a full programme for implementation of all of the various parts the Sports Strategy to SCDC for approval.

**2** The Owners shall in accordance with the approved programme for implementation implement or procure implementation of each part of the Sports Strategy following approval of the Sports Strategy pursuant to paragraph 1 of this Part 1 of Schedule 14 and shall thereafter continue to comply with the Sports Strategy.

### Part 2 — Youth and Play Strategy

**1** Prior to or simultaneously with submitting the first Reserved Matters Application for any Development Parcel within which Dwellings are to be constructed the Owners shall submit the Youth and Play Strategy to SCDC for approval, and which Youth and Play Strategy shall include a full programme for implementation of all of its various parts.

**2** The Owners shall in accordance with the approved programme for implementation implement or procure implementation of each part of the Youth and Play Strategy following approval of the Youth and Play Strategy pursuant to paragraph 1 of this Part 1 of Schedule 14 and subject thereto shall thereafter comply with the Youth and Play Strategy.

### Part 3 — LAP, LEAP and SIP, NEAP and Formal Park Specifications

**1** The Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification shall be submitted by the Owners to SCDC and approved by SCDC (or deemed in relation to sub-paragraph (c) and paragraph 2 only to have been approved by SCDC) either:

   - **(a)** as part of Reserved Matters Applications for any Development Parcel within which Dwellings are to be constructed and which is adjacent to or within which the Sports Pitches Formal Park NEAP LEAP or SIPs (as applicable adjacent) are located; or
   - **(b)** as part of a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Sports Pitches Formal Park NEAP LEAP SIPs (as applicable); or
   - **(c)** as standalone submissions to SCDC solely for the respective approval of the Sports Pitches Specification Formal Park Specification NEAP Specification or various LEAP Specification (as applicable) which may be submitted to SCDC at any time prior to Reserved Matters Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation to any area that includes land which such Reserved Matters Applications identify as Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs.

**2** The Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pitch, Formal Park, NEAPs, LEAPs and in those circumstances the approval or deemed approval of the Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification will be considered as if the Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification has been made as a standalone submission as opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a consequence the approval or deemed approval of the Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification may occur at a different time from the approval of any Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pitch, Formal Park, NEAPs, LEAPs and for the avoidance of doubt any approval or deemed approval in such regard shall not be taken to imply approval of any Reserved Matters Application.

**3** The LAP Specification shall (if not organised previously and unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC as not being required) be submitted for approval as part of a Reserved Matters Application for a relevant Development Parcel within which Dwellings are to be constructed and a LAP is to be provided.

### Part 4 — Open Space Land Transfer

**1** Prior to Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC as to the Open Space Land:

**1.1** The written notice will confirm that the Owners will either:

   - **(a)** retain the freehold interest and maintain the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land and appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part(s) of the Open Space Land on the Owners' behalf; or
   - **(b)** transfer a leasehold interest of not more than ten years in the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part(s) of the Open Space Land to the Management Company,

provided that the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options in paragraph 1.1 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company provided always that the Owners shall notify SCDC which of these options is being chosen within 12 months of the appointment of the Management Company or the transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company whichever is the earlier.

**1.2** In the event of a transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to transfer the leasehold interest to the Management Company as soon as reasonably practicable following the date of appointment of the Management Company and the Owners shall use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete such leasehold transfer within six months of the appointment of the Management Company and if within the first six months of the said appointment of the Management Company the leasehold transfer has not been completed the Owners shall, if requested, be required by SCDC acting reasonably in making such request, provide evidence to demonstrate why there has any continuing delay in completion of the leasehold interest being transferred and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall thereafter continue to be under an ongoing obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete the leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible.

**1.3** Unless otherwise agreed by SCDC the Owners shall make any offer to transfer the Open Space Land pursuant to this paragraph 1 conditional upon the relevant Open Space Land thereafter being managed in accordance with the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy.

**1.4** In the event that a Management Company is appointed by the Owners to manage the Open Space Land or the Management Company takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Open Space Land then after a period of ten years from the date a Management Company is appointed by the Owners or the leasehold transfer to the Management Company is completed the following shall apply:

   - **(a)** then the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Open Space Land to SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC if such Alternative Body has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land) for nil consideration pursuant to the relevant Open Space Land Transfer; and
   - **(b)** in the event SCDC (or if applicable such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days,

then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Open Space Land to the Management Company provided always that nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and for the avoidance of doubt nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC) save that the Owners and SCDC (or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC) shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

**2** The above provisions in Part 4 of this Schedule shall likewise apply mutatis mutandis to the Transfer/management of all LAPs but the timing under paragraph 1 of this Part 4 of this Schedule 14 as to service of the notice as to which option is being pursued rather than being by reference to "prior to Occupation of any Dwelling" shall instead be by reference to "prior to Occupation of any Dwelling in the particular Land Parcel in which the relevant LAP is situated".

### Part 5 — Management and maintenance of the public realm

**1** The Owners shall prior to Occupation of any Dwelling comprised in the Development submit the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy to SCDC for approval or deemed approval.

**2** The Owners shall maintain or procure that each area of Open Space Land shall be maintained in accordance with the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy.

**3** No Occupation of any Dwelling shall be permitted until SCDC has approved or be deemed to have approved the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy in writing.

**4** No Dwellings shall be permitted to be Occupied until the Management Company has been employed to ensure that the mechanism for recovery from future owners of the Dwellings of the maintenance costs for the Open Space Land as detailed in the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy can be achieved.

**5** The Owners shall implement or procure implementation of each part of the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy and following implementation shall thereafter comply with each part of the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy in all respects and in the event of any default in the proper implementation and maintenance of the Public Realm in accordance with all aspects of the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy then the responsibility and liability shall rest with the HCA unless satisfactory security arrangements acceptable to SCDC are agreed such that SCDC agrees to release the HCA from such residual responsibility and liability.

**6** The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another and agree that priority will be given by all parties to pursuing and utilising Alternative Public Realm Funding to undertake the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Purposes provided that nothing in this Agreement shall be deemed to require or permit the use of any Annual Maintenance Charge for the management and maintenance of any part of the Public Realm in relation to which the payment of the Annual Maintenance Charge was not described as being collected towards.

### Part 6 — Maintenance scheme provisions

**1** The Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy shall be prepared by the Owners in writing and which shall include details of the following:

   - **(a)** responsibility for maintaining each of the areas of Open Space Land which may involve transfer of the liability to a Management Company (consistent with the other provisions of this Agreement) of all or any parts of the aforementioned areas;
   - **(b)** frequency of maintenance of the different areas of Open Space Land; and
   - **(c)** standards of maintenance and repair to be achieved in respect of the different areas of Open Space Land.

**2** The Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy shall also include provisions regarding how the costs of the maintenance shall be funded.

### Part 7 — Covenants relating to laying out and certification

In relation to the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park the Owners and SCDC covenant with each other as follows:

**1.1** The Owners shall lay out install and complete the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) to the satisfaction of SCDC and in accordance with:

   - **(a)** for the Sports Pitches the approved Sports Pitch Specification;

   - **(b)** for the Formal Park the approved Formal Park Specification;

   - **(c)** for any of the NEAPs the approved NEAP Specification;

   - **(d)** for any of the LEAPs the approved LEAP Specification; and

   - **(e)** for any LAP the Reserved Matters Approval relevant to the Development Parcel within which the LAP is located,

provided that in laying out and installing:

   - **(i)** the Sports Pitches the Owners use a contractor that has been approved by the Sports and Play Construction Association (or such other body or association as may from time to time carry out the same or similar functions to the Sports and Play Construction Association as regards its members professional competency and performance in installation of sports and play pitches and/or field (or such other contractor as may be approved by SCDC as being suitably experienced in laying out and installing sports pitches and fields),

and provided always that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between:

      - **(A)** the Open Space SPD; and

      - **(B)** the Fields in Trust document "Guidance for Open Space and Play: Beyond The Six Acre Standard October 2015"

then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence; and

   - **(ii)** in relation to the Formal Park the Owners shall use a contractor approved by SCDC as suitably experienced for work of that particular kind.

**1.2** The Owners shall permit the Surveyor:

   - **(a)** to attend and to put forward his/her views at all formal site meetings during the course of laying out the Sports Pitches, LEAPs, NEAPs and LAPs and which overall shall not unless otherwise agreed by SCDC and/or the Surveyor be at intervals of not less than one such meeting every 13 weeks but otherwise the frequency of such meetings to be agreed between the Owners and SCDC and/or the Surveyor (acting reasonably) having regard to the construction steps in the laying out of the Sports Pitches, LEAPs, NEAPs and LAPs and the timing of key decisions or milestones in that laying out;

   - **(b)** to inspect the Sports Pitches, LEAPs, NEAPs and LAPs during the course of construction/fitting out provided that the Surveyor has given reasonable notice of his/her wish to inspect;

   - **(c)** to suggest items for inclusion in any necessary schedule of defects issued to the contractor in respect of the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, LEAPs, NEAPs and LAPs and the Owners acknowledge that the costs of remedying any defects shall be undertaken at the Owners' expense and shall not be taken into account in determining if the relevant Cost Cap has been reached or exceeded;

   - **(d)** to stipulate any items or areas where the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, LEAPs, NEAPs and LAPs do not meet the relevant Specification (which forms part of the contract for the laying out of the relevant areas) then SCDC may serve on the Owners a written notice which shall specify those parts of the relevant Specification that have not been met and the Owners shall within 60 Working Days from the date of service of such notice (or such other period as SCDC may agree) to carry out such work as are necessary to secure compliance with the relevant Specification at the expense of the Owners and the Owners shall fund any and all reasonable subsequent inspections of the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, LEAPs, NEAPs and LAPs until the relevant approved Specification shall have been met.

**3** Following completion of the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) or any part of these areas the Owners will:

   - **(a)** serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction;

   - **(b)** if SCDC chooses to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial work to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable;

   - **(c)** upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 3(b) of this Part of this Schedule and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) have been completed to SCDC's reasonable satisfaction,

provided that if SCDC fails to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) within 40 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Completion Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Completion Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods provided further that the inspection procedure identified in this paragraph 3 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Council Completion Certificate or a Council Completion Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable).

**4** Following issue or deemed issue of a Council Completion Certificate in respect of the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable), the Owners shall:

   - **(a)** maintain the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) for the Maintenance Period to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC; and

   - **(b)** allow access to members of the public to the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) (free of charge but on a permissive basis only) each day for the purpose of recreation and play (unless SCDC agrees otherwise in writing).

**5** On expiration of the Maintenance Period the Owners shall:

   - **(a)** serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that such works have been maintained to their reasonable satisfaction;

   - **(b)** if SCDC chooses to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) and identifies necessary remedial works, to complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and

   - **(c)** upon completion of any remedial works, to serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial work identified by them pursuant to paragraph 5(b) and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) have been maintained to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC,

provided that if SCDC fails to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) within 40 Working Days of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Final Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Final Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods provided further that the inspection procedure identified in this paragraph 5 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Council Final Certificate or a Council Final Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable).

**6** The Owners shall maintain the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) in accordance with the relevant specification unless and until such time that the relevant Open Space Land Transfer is completed to SCDC or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Management Company who shall subject to which is then applicable thereafter become responsible for the maintenance of the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) provided that transfer of the relevant parts of the Open Space Land may be completed on a phased basis following the issue of Council Final Certificates in relation to such part of the Open Space Land.

**7** On expiration of the Maintenance Period in respect of the Sports Pitches before any Certificate of Final Completion is issued the Owners will procure an independent surveyor's report addressed to SCDC or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Management Company from a reputable consultant (and which Consultant will enter into a collateral warranty in favour of SCDC (or if applicable in favour of such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Management Company).

**8** Where specialist design is involved and/or where specialist contractors are engaged the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that with effect from the Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pitches or if later from the date of transfer the benefit of all guarantees and warranties relating to the design and construction/laying out of the Sports Pitches are assigned or otherwise passed on to as applicable:

   - **(a)** SCDC; or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC; or

   - **(b)** the Management Company,

provided that in the event of an Insolvency Event on the part of the designers or contractors of the Sports Pitches such that the Owners having used Reasonable Endeavours have not obtained assignment or otherwise of all guarantees and warranties relating to the design and/or construction of the Sports Pitches the Owners shall obtain the necessary insurance for the benefit of SCDC or if applicable such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Management Company (as applicable and if requested in writing by such entities) in relation to defective construction and/or latent defective design as may be available from commercial insurers at reasonably commercial rates and terms for an insured term of not more than the period that the warranty or guarantee would otherwise endure for and on such insurance being put in place the obligation to obtain guarantees and warranties will cease to apply.

**9** The Owners will use Reasonable Endeavours to obtain a collateral warranty for each of the NEAPs/LEAPs/LAPs where these are available at normal and standard commercial terms and such that on expiration of the Maintenance Period in respect of the NEAPs/LEAPs/LAPs and before any relevant Certificate of Final Completion is issued then collateral warranties are given in favour of SCDC or if applicable in favour of such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC or the Management Company where these are available at normal and standard commercial terms.

### Part 8 — Covenants relating to delivery requirements

The Owners covenant with SCDC:

**1.1** to:

   - **(a)** complete (and complete here meaning in relation to those parts which comprise Sports Pitches such that they are ready for full use as sports pitches and having been laid out for a period of at least 18 months previously so that the sports pitches have had time to properly settle) the Western Sports Hub in accordance with the Phasing Plan and approved Sports Strategy and in any event prior to occupation of 2,000 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site and which completion shall be evidenced by the issuing or the deemed issue of the Council Completion Certificate; and

   - **(b)** complete (and complete here meaning in relation to those parts which comprise Sports Pitches such that they are ready for full use as sports pitches and (with the exception of the All Weather Pitch) having been laid out for a period of at least 18 months previously so that the sports pitches have had time to properly settle) the Eastern Sports Hub in accordance with the Phasing Plan and approved Sports Strategy and in any event prior to Occupation of 2,500 Dwellings across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site and which completion shall be evidenced by the issuing or the deemed issue of the Council Completion Certificate; and

**1.2** To complete the Formal Park and NEAP in accordance with the Phasing Plan and Town Centre Strategy and/or Youth and Play Strategy and in any event no later than Occupation of the 2000th Dwelling across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site or the 500th Dwelling on the Site or in accordance with such other trigger as may be agreed as part of the Phasing Plan and which completion shall be evidenced by the issuing or the deemed issue of the Council Completion Certificate.

**1.3** No later than whichever of the following shall be the first to occur to complete each respective LEAP and/or SIP which shall be evidenced by the issuing or the deemed issue of the Council Completion Certificate in respective of each respective LEAP and/or SIP:

   - **(a)** occupation of 50% of the Dwellings within the Development Parcel that the LEAP and/or SIP forms part of; or

   - **(b)** occupation of 75% of the Dwellings which are adjacent to or have a frontage facing the respective LEAP and/or SIP.

**1.4** Notwithstanding paragraph 1.3 of this part 8 of Schedule 14 the first LEAP shall be provided no later than the Occupation of the 500th Dwelling on Phase 2 or the 2000th Dwelling across the Northstowe Phase 1 Site and the Site.

**1.5** No later than whichever of the following shall be the first to occur to complete the each respective LAP which shall be evidenced by the issuing or the deemed issue of the Council Completion Certificate in respective of each respective LAP:

   - **(a)** occupation of 50% of the Dwellings within the Development Parcel that the LAP forms part of; or

   - **(b)** occupation of 75% of the Dwellings which are adjacent to or have a frontage facing the respective LAP.

### Part 9 — Incidental Open Space

The Owners covenant with SCDC as follows:

**1.1** The areas of Incidental Open Space shall either be maintained as follows:

   - **(a)** as Open Space Land in accordance with Part 5 of this Schedule 14; or

   - **(b)** transferred as part of the land comprising a Dwelling where a Dwelling is located adjacent to any area of Incidental Open Space and the owners of the Dwelling shall be responsible for the maintenance of such area of Incidental Open Space but in such case the obligation as to maintenance shall despite clause 11.2.7 shall be enforceable against the owner of the relevant Dwelling,

provided that nothing shall prevent the Owners from choosing a variety of transferees referred to in this paragraph 1.1 of Part 9 of this Schedule 14 for different parts of the Incidental Open Space and provided further that the Owners shall maintain the area(s) of Incidental Open Space referred to in paragraph 1.1(b) until such time as the relevant area(s) are transferred pursuant to the provisions of paragraph 1.1(b) of this part 9 of this Schedule 14.

**1.2** The relevant transfer referred to in paragraph 1.1(b) of this part 9 of this Schedule 14 above shall form part of the property which is the subject of the transfer to the plot.

### Part 10 — Terms of reference for the steering group for the management and maintenance of the Public Realm

**1 PURPOSE**

**1.1** SCDC and HCA have agreed to establish, as soon as reasonably practicable after the approval of the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy, a Steering Group of relevant qualified persons to deliver the actions necessary to deliver the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy.

**1.2** The purpose of the Steering Group is to oversee the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Purposes and to ensure that the SCDC, County Council, Northstowe Parish/Town Council (where such body is established), HCA and any other relevant organisation act collaboratively on the decisions that have to be made in managing and maintaining the Public Realm there for the benefit of the residents of the Development.

**2 REPRESENTATION**

**2.1** Initially, the Steering Group shall consist of one representative from each of the following:

   - **(a)** the County Council;

   - **(b)** SCDC;

   - **(c)** HCA; and

   - **(d)** the Town Council where such a body has been established at the time the Steering Group is established.

Any other Alternative Body with a legal interest in the Public Realm.

**2.2** New Steering Group members may be added as the need arises by mutual agreement between the parties on the Steering Group. The Steering Group will meet every month unless decided otherwise by the parties on the Steering Group.

**2.3** Approvals by the Steering Group shall unless stated otherwise be decisions approved/agreed by a majority of the parties on the Steering Group save that in the event that HCA acting reasonably is not one of the parties in that majority then HCA may if it wishes the majority decision not to apply refer the matter to an Expert for determination and in such cases the approval shall be suspended and shall not be capable of being relied and acted upon unless and until the decision of the majority has been confirmed as reasonable by the Expert.

**3 TERMS OF REFERENCE**

**3.1** The initial terms of reference of Steering Group will be as follows but this will not prevent the Steering Group adding to or amending the terms of reference at a later date provided that any such additions or amendments are reasonable and necessary:

**3.2** To ensure the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy is implemented according to the agreed regimes and standards contained within it.

**3.3** To explore all funding mechanisms in order to support delivery of the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy is and in making recommendations, acting advising or otherwise acting within the terms of reference that the Steering Group shall operate within shall always give priority to pursuing and utilising (or securing the pursuit and utilisation) of Alternative Public Realm Funding to undertake the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Purposes.

**3.4** To receive and review monthly update reports on management and maintenance issues provided by the contractors and organisations managing and maintaining the Public Realm.

**3.5** To review the operation of the Northstowe Phase 2 Management and Maintenance Strategy from time to time taking into account relevant performance indicators and targets.

**3.6** To liaise with any Parish Councils whose administrative boundaries abut the Site.

### Part 11 — Revenue and Capital Contribution Payments

The Owners covenant to pay to SCDC the Sports Revenue Contributions as follows and each to be paid either:

   - **(a)** as a lump sum payment prior to the completion of the relevant facility; or

   - **(b)** in three annual tranches with the first tranche payable prior to the completion of the relevant facility and the second and third payments to be made on or before the first and second anniversary of the first payment:

      - **(i)** Sports Pavilion £135,000;

      - **(ii)** Sports Hub East Contribution and/or Sports Hub West Contribution together totalling no more than £270,000;

      - **(iii)** Indoor Sports Building £170,000.

**2** The Owners covenant to pay to SCDC or if applicable to such Alternative Body nominated by SCDC revenue contributions for the Play Areas and such revenue contributions to be calculated in accordance with the Open Space SPD on the approval of reserved matters for each application involving a relevant Play Area and such contribution being paid on legal completion of the Transfer of a relevant Play Area to SCDC or if so nominated by SCDC to the Town Council.

### Part 12 — Sports Pavilion

**1** In the event that the Owners submit the Reserved Matters Application for the Sports Pavilion then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application the Owners shall undertake pre-application discussion with SCDC in relation to the exact location and boundaries for the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land and the exact location and boundaries for the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land shall be submitted to and approved by or deemed to have been approved by SCDC either:

**1.1** as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel adjacent to which the Sports Pavilion is located; or

**1.2** as a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Sports Pavilion.

**2** In the event that SCDC submits the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application for the Sports Pavilion then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application it shall have obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the exact size and location of the Sports Pavilion and in the event that such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is submitted before the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer is completed then SCDC shall have also obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the extent of the site in relation to which such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is made.

**3** Subject to paragraph 4 below the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC (or in relation to paragraph 3.3 only deemed to have been approved by SCDC) either:

**3.1** as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel adjacent to which the Sports Pavilion is located; or

**3.2** as part of a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Sports Pavilion; or

**3.3** as a standalone submission to SCDC solely for the approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification which may be submitted to SCDC at any time prior to Reserved Matters Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation to any area of land that includes land which such Reserved Matters Applications seek to designate as the Sports Pavilion Land.

**4** The Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pavilion and in those circumstances the approval or deemed approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification will be considered as if the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification has been made as a standalone submission as opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a consequence the approval or deemed approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification may occur at a different time from the approval of any Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pavilion and for the avoidance of doubt any approval or deemed approval shall not be taken to imply approval of any Reserved Matters Application.

**5** The Owners shall:

**5.1** use Reasonable Endeavours not to locate any Service Corridor on the Sports Pavilion Land; and

**5.2** subject to paragraph 5.1 above the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to locate any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within a Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Sports Pavilion) which Service Corridor shall not be located underneath the actual or anticipated building footprint of the Sports Pavilion (as applicable) provided that if SCDC has not advised the Owners in writing of the actual or anticipated building footprint before the said Service Corridor has been approved or deemed to have been approved then the Owners shall be permitted to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the approved or deemed approved Service Corridor notwithstanding that the footprint of the Sports Pavilion (as applicable) may ultimately be constructed within the Service Corridor; and

**5.3** save as otherwise specifically agreed by an Expert, not locate Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Sports Pavilion Land which are outside of the Service Corridor and/or which are under an existing or known building footprint.

### Part 13 — Sports Pavilion Land Transfer generic arrangements

Prior to Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which sets out which of the options set out in paragraphs 1.1, 1.2, 2.1 and 2.2 of this Part 13 of this Schedule 14 the Owners intend to elect to progress:

**1.1** Option 1:

The Owners to construct the Sports Pavilion to the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification subject to the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap not being exceeded.

**1.2** Option 2:

SCDC to construct the Sports Pavilion and for the Owners to pay the Sports Pavilion Actual Contribution to SCDC to be used for the Sports Pavilion Purpose provided that if the Owners elect to pursue this option the written notice shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Sports Pavilion Land pursuant to the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer provided that if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) accepts the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land the actual transfer will be completed prior to Occupation of 2,500th Dwellings across Phase 1 and Phase 2.

**2** In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they:

**2.1** elect to pursue Option 1 then the provisions of Part 14 and Part 17 shall apply; and

**2.2** elect to pursue Option 2 then the provisions of Part 15 shall apply.

**3** In the event that SCDC declines or does not accept any of the offers made to it pursuant to Options 2 to have the Sports Pavilion Land or both areas of land transferred to it within 40 Working Days for SCDC to construct the Sports Pavilion the Owners shall not be obliged to transfer the Sports Pavilion Land nor will the Owners be obliged to pay the Sports Pavilion Contribution (as applicable) to SCDC and the provisions of and restrictions in the relevant Options referred to above as in respect of Option 2 shall cease to apply.

**4** Whether the Owners or SCDC construct the Sports Pavilion the following provisions shall apply and the party with whom the responsibility to construct the Sports Pavilion rests (the "Constructing Party") shall seek and consult with SCDC or the Owners whichever is not the Constructing Party (the "Other Party") to obtain the prior input and representations in writing from the Other Party in relation to the following and where received the Contracting Party shall act in good faith in taking into account any such input and representations before making any decisions or preparing details or taking steps referred to below provided that any such representations are received within 40 Working Days of any such request:

   - **(a)** Tendering or making appointments as regards any consultants or contractors whether internal appointments made informally or formally of the Owners or the SCDC's staff or employees in relation to the procurement construction and completion of the Sports Pavilion provided that the written notification from the Contracting Party to the Other Party shall also include supporting information justifying the need for the costs or appointments, how the costs or appointments present best value for the project and how the anticipated notified expenditure or appointments support the overall aim for high quality design for the Development.

   - **(b)** Where SCDC (or the Town Council as its nominee) are to construct the Sports Pavilion SCDC covenants with the Owners to use reasonable endeavours to efficiently design, layout and construct the Sports Pavilion including any use of the balance of the Sports Pavilion Land where such use does not involve the construction of building(s) with the overriding aim and commitment to the Owners that SCDC shall only use such part of the Sports Pavilion Land as is reasonably necessary to deliver the Sports Pavilion to applicable building standards and in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification. In the event that the Reserved Matters Approvals indicate or it is otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners that any part of the Sports Pavilion Land is not required for the construction and laying out of the Sports Pavilion then unless otherwise agreed it shall be used as Public Open Space.

### Part 14 — Option 1 Transfer and Construction arrangements

**1** In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 1 then the following provisions shall apply.

**2** The Owners shall construct the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and in accordance with the provisions of paragraphs 1, 2, 3 and 4 of Part 12 of this Schedule 14 and shall complete such construction and provide a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pavilion prior to the Occupation of 2,500 Dwellings across Phase 1 and Phase 2.

**3** The written notice referred to in paragraph 1 of Part 13 setting out the Owners' election to proceed with Option 1 shall also include and provide for one of the following as regards future management and transfer arrangements:

   - **(a)** an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) the Sports Pavilion Land and completed Sports Pavilion provided that if SCDC accepts the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land and completed Sports Pavilion the Owners shall execute and deliver to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer after the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued; or

   - **(b)** the written notice shall notify SCDC that the Owners wish to offer Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion pursuant to the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer to an Alternative Body provided that in such notification to set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion provided further that the written offer of a transfer of the Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process and provided FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land the Owners shall execute and deliver to the Alternative Body the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer after the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued but in any event subject to Part 16 of this Schedule; or

   - **(c)** the written notice will confirm that the Owners will either:

      - **(i)** retain and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion and to appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf; or

      - **(ii)** transfer the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion to the Management Company subject to Part 16 of this Schedule,

      provided that the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company provided always that the Owners shall unless otherwise agreed with SCDC notify which of those options is being chosen within 12 months of the initial written notice or the letting of the construction contract whichever is the earlier.

**4** The Owners shall construct and equip the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification approved or deemed to have been approved and the provisions of paragraphs 3 and 4 of Part 12 of this Schedule 14 shall be applied equally in relation to the approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification provided that the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and the construction of the Sports Pavilion shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Sports Pavilion that would result in a combined cost to construct and fit out and equip that would exceed the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap.

**5** No more than 2500 Dwellings across Phase 1 and Phase 2 shall be Occupied until:

**5.1** a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pavilion has been issued; and

**5.2** subject to Part 16 of this Schedule where the Owners have elected to transfer the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land to either SCDC or an Alternative Body and the Owners have executed and delivered the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable); or

**5.3** subject to Part 16 of this Schedule where the Owners have elected to transfer the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land to a Management Company the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer has been completed; or

**5.4** where the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land that a Management Company has been appointed to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf.

**6** In the event that SCDC or the Alternative Body declines or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall where the offer has been made to SCDC the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends either to:

**6.1** transfer the Sports Pavilion Land to an Alternative Body subject to Part 16 of this Schedule in which case any such notification the Owners shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion; or

**6.2** transfer the Sports Pavilion Land to a Management Company subject to Part 16 of this Schedule; or

**6.3** retain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf,

and the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 1 of Part 13 of this Schedule 14 provided that a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

**6.4** Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends to either:

   - **(a)** transfer the Sports Pavilion Land to a Management Company subject to Part 16 of this Schedule; or

   - **(b)** retain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf,

   and the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to this paragraph 6.2 of Part 14 of this Schedule 14.

### Part 15 — Option 2 Transfer and Construction arrangements

**1** In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 2 then subject to paragraph 3 of Part 13 of this Schedule 14 the following provisions shall apply:

**1.1** The Owners shall:

**1.1.1** execute and deliver the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) no later than Occupation of 500 Dwellings; and

**1.1.2** pay the Sports Pavilion Contribution to SCDC in the following instalments:

   - **(a)** £210,000 (two hundred and ten thousand British pounds) is to be paid prior to Occupation of 230 Dwellings or within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the design contract for the Sports Pavilion has occurred whichever is the later;

   - **(b)** £915,000 (nine hundred and fifteen thousand British pounds) to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that an award of the construction contract for the construction of the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Building Detailed Specification or to a written specification which stipulates levels of design, standards, materials and equipment that are not less than that set out in the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification provided that where such a construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above; and

   - **(c)** £375,000 (three hundred and seventy five thousand British pounds) to be paid within 140 Working Days of the date of the payment made pursuant to paragraph 1.1.2 (b) of this Part 15 above.

**1.2** SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Sports Pavilion no later than 18 calendar months after the date the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer is completed subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 1.1.2 above; and

**1.3** SCDC shall construct the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the approved Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to clause 18 provided always that SCDC shall only be required to construct and complete the Sports Pavilion to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction fit out and completion to the point the Sports Pavilion can be opened to the general public does not exceed the Sports Pavilion Contribution unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other sources to construct fit out and complete to a higher standard provided that such funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the County Council from the Owners in relation to the Development.

### Part 16 — Transfer of Land to an alternative body or management company

**1** Where the Sports Pavilion is to be transferred by the Owners to an Alternative Body or a Management Company pursuant to this Schedule 14 the Owners shall transfer it on the following terms:

   - **(a)** the transfer shall be of the leasehold interest and it shall be for no more than ten years; or

   - **(b)** the Owners shall retain the freehold interest in the Sports Pavilion and the Owners shall appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf; and

   - **(c)** in the event of a transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company the Owners shall be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete such leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible following the date of appointment of the Management Company and if within the first six months of the said appointment of the Management Company the leasehold transfer has not been completed the Owners shall, if requested, be required subject to SCDC acting reasonably in making any such request, provide evidence to demonstrate why there is any continuing delay in completion of the leasehold interest being transferred and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall thereafter continue to be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete the leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible;

**1.2** In the event that a Management Company is appointed by the Owners to manage the Sports Pavilion or the Management Company or an Alternative Body takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Sports Pavilion then after a period of ten years of the earlier from:

   - **(a)** the date a Management Company is appointed by the Owners; or

   - **(b)** the leasehold transfer to the Management Company is completed

   the following shall apply:

   - **(i)** the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Sports Pavilion Land to SCDC or if SCDC so nominates the Town Council provided the Town Council has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land and in either case for nil consideration pursuant to the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer and in the event SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Sports Pavilion Land to the Management Company; and

   - **(ii)** If a Town Council has not been properly constituted and SCDC is not willing to accept the offer of transfer then the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it remains in the Sports Pavilion to the Management Company,

   provided always that nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and for the avoidance of doubt nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC or the Town Council save that the Owners and SCDC or the Town Council shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

### Part 17 — Requirements to be observed by the Owners when constructing the Sports Pavilion

**1** The Owners shall notify the Surveyor of all site meetings relating to the construction or equipping of the Sports Pavilion.

**2** The Owners shall permit the Surveyor:

   - **(a)** to attend and to put forward his/her views at all site meetings during the course of construction and fitting out/equipping of the Sports Pavilion;

   - **(b)** on reasonable notice to inspect the Sports Pavilion during the course of construction/fitting out;

   - **(c)** to suggest items for inclusion in any necessary schedule of defects issued to the contractor in respect of the Sports Pavilion and the Owners acknowledge that the costs of remedying any Defects shall be undertaken at the Owners' expense and shall not be taken into account in determining if the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap has been reached or exceeded; and

   - **(d)** to stipulate any items or areas where the Sports Pavilion does not meet the final agreed Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification (which forms part of the contract for the construction of the Sports Pavilion) then SCDC may serve on the Owners a written notice which shall specify those parts of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification that have not been met and the Owners shall within 20 Working Days from the date of service of such notice (or such other period as SCDC may agree) carry out such works as are necessary to secure compliance with the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification at the expense of the Owners and the Owners shall fund any and all reasonable subsequent inspections of the Sports Pavilion until the approved Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification shall have been met provided further that the costs of such inspections do not result in the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap being exceeded.

**3** Not less than 20 Working Days prior to the anticipated issue of a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the Sports Pavilion the Owners shall in writing invite the Surveyor to inspect the Sports Pavilion and identify any Defects requiring attention.

**4** The Owners shall be entitled to issue the Certificate of Practical Completion if after the expiry of 40 Working Days after the invitation made pursuant to paragraph 3 of this Part 17 of this Schedule any of the following apply:

   - **(a)** the Surveyor has failed to inspect the Sports Pavilion; or

   - **(b)** the Surveyor has failed to notify the Owners in writing of any Defects such notification having been received by the Owners within the said 20 Working Days; or

   - **(c)** the Surveyor has indicated following an inspection there are no Defects to remedy; or

   - **(d)** where the Surveyor (acting reasonably) has identified any Defects and where all such Defects have been properly remedied to the Surveyor's reasonable satisfaction provided that the provisions of Clause 17 shall apply in the event of dispute as to:

      - **(i)** whether any Defects identified by the Surveyor are reasonable and should be remedied or not; and/or

      - **(ii)** whether the Defects have been properly remedied.

**5** The Owners shall send the Surveyor copies of all instructions issued to a contractor in respect of the Sports Pavilion at the same time as issuing them and a copy of any Certificate of Practical Completion, list of Defects or Certificate of Final Completion issued in respect of the Sports Pavilion within five Working Days of its receipt by the Owners and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall obtain a Certificate of Practical Completion and Certificate of Final Completion themselves and these certificates will not be issued by the Surveyor save that the Owners shall have complied with the provisions of paragraph 3 of this Part 17 above in relation to the issue of the Certificate of Practical Completion only.

**6** Notwithstanding any Transfer of the Sports Pavilion to SCDC or an Alternative Body or Management Company when a Certificate of Practical Completion is issued:

**6.1**

   - **(a)** the Owners shall be responsible for rectification of any and all material Defects which manifest themselves during a period of 12 months after the issue of a Certificate of Practical Completion is issued and thereafter (subject to the law relating to latent defects) the Owners shall procure that a Certificate of Final Completion is obtained; and

   - **(b)** after the end of the 12 months period as aforesaid and before any Certificate of Final Completion is issued the Owners will procure an independent surveyor's report addressed to SCDC or an Alternative Body or Management Company from a reputable consultant (and which Consultant will enter into a collateral warranty in favour of SCDC or Alternative Body or Management Company) and the Owners will take account of and rectify any and all Defects set out by such Surveyor's report; and

   - **(c)** the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that with effect from the Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pavilion or if later from the date of transfer the benefit of all guarantees and warranties relating to the design and construction of the Sports Pavilion are assigned or otherwise passed on to as applicable:

      - **(i)** SCDC; or

      - **(ii)** the Alternative Body; or

      - **(iii)** the Management Company,

      provided that in the event of an Insolvency Event on the part of the designers or contractors of the Sports Pavilion such that the Owners having used Reasonable Endeavours have not obtained assignment or otherwise of all guarantees and warranties relating to the design and/or construction of the Sports Pavilion the Owners shall obtain the necessary insurance for the benefit of SCDC the Alternative Body or the Management Company (as applicable and if requested in writing by such entities) in relation to defective construction and/or defective design as may be available from commercial insurers at reasonably commercial rates and terms for an insured term of not more than the period that the warranty or guarantee would otherwise endure for and on such insurance being put in place the obligation to obtain guarantees and warranties will cease to apply.

**6.2** The Owners shall be responsible for the management and maintenance of any surface water drainage systems that serve the Sports Pavilion Land but which are in whole or part located outside of the boundaries of the Sports Pavilion Land until such time as they become adopted under an adoption Agreement with Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority.

**6.3** The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that any foul and surface water drainage systems serving the Sports Pavilion Land shall be the subject of an adoption Agreement with Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority by the time the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer is completed.

**6.4** Where the Owners have been unable to secure the adoption of the foul and surface water drainage systems referred to under paragraph 6.5 of this Part 17 of Schedule 14 above prior to the date of the completion of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer then such Sports Pavilion Land Transfer shall contain covenants in favour of the transferee from the Owners for themselves and their successors in title to the relevant parts of the remainder of the Site:

   - **(a)** to keep those foul and surface water drainage systems referred to under paragraph 6.5 of this Part 17 of Schedule 14 above in good repair and condition; and

   - **(b)** to indemnify the relevant transferee of the Sports Pavilion Land against all and any costs and claims made against the transferee in respect of the existence and condition of the same pending adoption by Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority.

**6.5** The responsibility for the maintenance and management of any foul and surface water drainage systems that are situated in on or under the Sports Pavilion Land which are for the use of the Sports Pavilion Land and which are not the subject of an adoption Agreement with Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority shall pass to the transferee of the Sports Pavilion Land.

**6.6** The Owners shall be responsible for the management and maintenance of a road intended for adoption that serves or will serve the Sports Pavilion Land and shall procure that a road serving the Sports Pavilion Land shall be built to an adoptable standard and the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that the road serving the Sports Pavilion Land which is intended for adoption shall be the subject of an adoption Agreement with the highway authority to be entered into within a reasonable timeframe having regard to the anticipated timeframe of the anticipated date of completion of the Sports Pavilion.

**7** The Owners shall bear the reasonable costs of the Surveyor incurred in association with the provisions of this Part 17 of Schedule 14 and for the avoidance of doubt such costs shall be taken into account as part of the Sports Pavilion Costs and associated costs caps referred to within the definition of Sports Pavilion Costs in this Schedule.

## Schedule 15 — Waste Facilities and Waste and Recycling Bins

**Relevant Definitions:**

**"Waste and Recycling Bins Contribution"** means the sum of up to £376,250 (Three hundred and seventy-six thousand two hundred and fifty British pounds) to be used towards Waste and Recycling Bins Purposes only.

**"Waste and Recycling Bins Purposes"** means for the provision of waste and recycling bins for each Dwelling forming part of the Development.

**"Waste Collection Vehicle Contribution"** means the sum of £119,000 (One hundred and nineteen thousand British pounds) to be used towards Waste Collection Vehicles Purposes only.

**"Waste Collection Vehicles Purposes"** means for the investment in a new vehicle for the purpose of collecting waste and recycling from the Dwellings forming part of the Development if such vehicle is required because of the provision of underground bins or other similar arrangement.

**Waste and Recycling Bins Contribution**

Prior to Occupation of any Dwelling within a Development Parcel the Owners shall pay to SCDC or at SCDC's nomination the County Council £72.50 per Dwelling (where the Dwelling is a house as such term is traditionally known/interpreted and £150.00 per Dwelling (where the Dwelling is a flat/apartment as such terms are traditionally known/interpreted) and in each case within the relevant Development Parcel as evidenced by Reserved Matters Approvals for that Development Parcel to be used towards the Waste and Recycling Bins Purposes only PROVIDED THAT the total amount that is paid in relation to the whole Development does not exceed the Waste and Recycling Bins Contribution.

**Waste Collection Vehicle Contribution**

Prior to 7 months of the anticipated first Occupation of any Dwelling where there is a provision for underground bins or other similar arrangement the Owners shall pay the Waste Collection Vehicle Contribution to SCDC or at SCDC's nomination the County Council to be used towards the Waste Collection Vehicle Purposes only.

## Schedule 16 — Webbs Hole Sluice

**Relevant Definitions:**
