**"Project Management Fee"** Gallagher's management fee of up to 7.5% of the total design and construction costs for completing the Webbs Hole Sluice Works.

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Works Costs"** means the actual cost of the carrying out and completing the Webbs Hole Sluice Work including the Project Management Fee.

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Contribution"** means the sum of (i) £647,500 (Six hundred and forty seven thousand five hundred British Pounds) or (ii) the sum of the Webbs Hole Sluice Works Contribution and the Webbs Hole Sluice Maintenance Contribution (whichever is the lower of (i) and (ii)) payable by the Owners to SCDC.

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Maintenance Contribution"** means the sum of £180,000 (one hundred and eighty thousand British Pounds) (Indexed) which is to form part of the Webbs Hole Sluice Contribution towards the future running and maintenance costs of the Webbs Hole Sluice Works.

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Works Contribution"** means 35% (thirty-five percent) of the Webbs Hole Sluice Work Costs and which is to form part of the Webbs Hole Sluice Contribution.

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Plan"** means the plan attached to this Agreement and marked "Plan 11".

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Works"** means the provision of a new pumping station at the Webbs Hole Sluice and in the location as shown illustratively on the Webbs Hole Sluice Plan and such installation being carried out and completed in accordance with the Webbs Hole Sluice Specification.

**"Webbs Hole Sluice Specification"** means the written specification approved by SCDC and a copy of which has been supplied to the Owners prior to the signing of this Agreement.

**Webbs Hole Sluice Works**

**1.1** the Owners shall pay 60% of the Webbs Hole Sluice Contribution to SCDC prior to Implementation 20% prior to first Occupation of any Dwelling and the balance on the third anniversary of the date Implementation occurred provided that it has received at least 20 Working Days beforehand written evidence from SCDC that the relevant works have been completed and PROVIDED THAT the said written evidence shall also include written evidence of the final anticipated Webbs Hole Sluice Works Costs; and

**1.2** SCDC shall use the Webbs Hole Sluice Contribution towards meeting 35% (thirty-five percent) of the cost of the Webbs Hole Sluice Works and the balance up to £180,000 (one hundred and eighty thousand pounds) towards the future running and maintenance costs of the Webbs Hole Sluice Works following practical completion of such works and for no other purpose.

**1.3** The total payment by or on behalf of the Owners to the Council under this Schedule shall not exceed £647,500 (excluding Indexation on the sum payable and which shall be paid in addition).

## Schedule 17 — Covenants by SCDC

**APPLICATION OF FUNDS**

SCDC covenants with the Owners that it will:

**1.1** Keep account of interest attributable to all of the Contributions received by it under this Agreement;

**1.2** In the event that upon the expiration of ten years (unless otherwise expressly stated below) after the date of receipt by SCDC of any Contribution or any part of any Contribution (together with any monies paid alongside the Contribution attributable to Indexation) if there should be any unexpended balance (and/or which is not committed to be spent pursuant to a contract entered into prior to such date) that unexpended balance shall be regarded as a Clawback Contribution and SCDC shall thereafter comply with the provisions of clauses 29.4 and 29.5.

**1.3** Work with the Owners in order to use all Reasonable Endeavours to secure available and appropriate funding grants credits and benefits in respect of any SCDC Matters pursuant to this Agreement in respect of the Development and subject to the provisions of clause 29 and the Overall Costs Cap to allocate any such funding grants credits and benefits obtained by SCDC as a priority to the benefit of the Development PROVIDED ALWAYS that the spending of any such funding grants credit and benefits obtained by SCDC pursuant to this paragraph 1.3 shall not reduce the Owners' liability as to the aggregate Overall Costs Cap until the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind payable pursuant to this Agreement total at least £6 million before Indexation.

**1.4** Apply use and expend any Contributions (together with any monies paid alongside the Contribution attributable to Indexation and any interest accrued on the Contributions) it receives in an efficient and prudent way only towards the purposes for which they have been paid pursuant to the provisions of this Agreement (and which for the avoidance of doubt may include use towards any purposes pursuant to clause 29.4) with the overriding aim and commitment by SCDC to the Owners that subject to clause 29 they shall apply use and expend any Contributions so far as are reasonably necessary to fulfil the specified purposes of this Agreement so as to create the most reasonably beneficial circumstances for a Clawback Contribution to arise.

**1.5** To make available to the Owners reasonable information in response to a request from the Owners as to how any Contributions have been or are being spent.

**1.6** If SCDC shall pay any monies paid by the Owners under this Agreement to any third party or body (other than any employee of SCDC or supplier or contractor to SCDC) it shall use all reasonable endeavours to obtain obligations (for the benefit of and enforceable by the Owners under the Contracts (Rights of Third Parties) Act 1999) from such person or body that:

   - **1.6.1** the person or body will only use or expend such money for the purposes specified in this Agreement or for which such money was paid;

   - **1.6.2** the person or body will refund any unspent money to SCDC together with any unspent interest accrued on it if it is not spent within the periods specified in paragraph 1.2 of this Schedule 17.

**1.7** To only draw against any Contribution Bonds that have been provided by the Owners to the County Council's benefit pursuant to the terms of this Agreement in the event of default by the Owners of the bonded obligations and to comply with the terms of the Bond in relation to drawing against the Bond and discharging it in full or in part.

**COMMUNITY**

SCDC covenants with the Owners that in relation to those SCDC covenants that involve community and/or Faith/Voluntary Groups SCDC will liaise with such parts of the community it considers relevant in each case with a view to inviting comments back for due consideration as might be appropriate in each case.

**GOVERNANCE**

SCDC covenants with the Owners that it will use Reasonable Endeavours to establish/approve a Town Council for Northstowe as soon as reasonably practicable following Commencement if it has not already taken steps to establish/approve a Town Council in order that the Town Council is functioning and operational to the extent it can accept transfers of any land and/or buildings as may be provided for under this Agreement and thereafter has the necessary powers to appoint engage instruct contractors employees agents consultants or other bodies organisations or individuals to manage and maintain any land or buildings that the Town Council accepts a transfer of under the terms of this Agreement.

**STATUTORY AND OTHER CONSENTS AND ORDERS REQUIRED**

SCDC will use Reasonable Endeavours to co-operate with the Highways England and any other statutory parties in connection with any aspect of the Development which involves the assistance consent or co-operation of the Highways England or other statutory parties PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT SCDC will not be required to spend any monies whether capital or revenue in so doing but will ensure that reasonable officer time is allocated from time to time.

**LAND**

SCDC will use Reasonable Endeavours to co-operate with the Owners in the grant of any rights, approvals and consents in respect of land (if any) owned by SCDC as reasonably required by the Owners to comply with the obligations in the Agreement PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT SCDC will not be required to spend any monies whether capital or revenue in so doing but will ensure that reasonable officer time is allocated from time to time.

**CONSULTATION WITH THE OWNERS**

Where in this Agreement there is reference to consultation or liaison with the HCA (as the HCA or as the Owners) SCDC shall undertake meaningful consultation at a time when the HCA still has the ability to influence any decision and shall consider and take into account all reasonable proposals suggestions recommendations or comments made by the HCA whether the HCA make them through one of the project core teams referred to in this Agreement or in writing served on SCDC.

## Schedule 18 — Covenants by the County Council

**APPLICATION OF FUNDS**

The County Council covenants with the Owners that it will:

**1.1** keep account of interest received by it under this Agreement;

**1.2** In the event that upon the expiration of ten years after the date of receipt by the County Council of any Contribution or any part of any Contribution other than those Contribution stated in paragraph 7 of this Schedule (together with any monies paid alongside the Contribution attributable to Indexation) there should be any unexpended balance (and/or which is not committed to be spent pursuant to a contract entered into prior to such date) that unexpended balance shall be regarded as a Clawback Contribution and the County Council shall thereafter comply with the provisions of clauses 29.4 and 29.5.

**1.3** Work with the Owners to use all Reasonable Endeavours to in order to secure all available and appropriate funding grants credits and benefits in respect of any County Matters pursuant to this Agreement in respect of the Development which funding grants credit and benefits obtained by the County Council shall subject as hereinafter provided be allocated and used by the County Council to the benefit of the Development PROVIDED ALWAYS that the spending of any such funding grants credit and benefits obtained by the County Council pursuant to this paragraph 1.3 shall not reduce the Owners' liability as to the aggregate Overall Costs Cap until the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Work in Kind payable pursuant to this Agreement total at least £6 million before Indexation.

**1.4** To only draw against any Contribution Bonds that have been provided by the Owners to the County Council's benefit pursuant to the terms of this Agreement in the event of default by the Owners of the bonded obligations and to comply with the terms of the Bond in relation to drawing against the Bond and discharging it in full or in part.

**1.5** Apply use and expend any Contributions (together with any monies paid alongside the Contribution attributable to Indexation and any interest accrued on the Contributions) it receives in an efficient and prudent way to only towards the purposes for which they have been paid pursuant to the provisions of this Agreement (and which for the avoidance of doubt may include use towards any purposes pursuant to clause 29.4) with the overriding aims and commitments by the County Council to the Owners that subject to clause 29 they shall only apply use and expend any Contributions so far as are reasonably necessary to fulfil the specified purpose so as to create the most reasonably beneficial circumstances for a Clawback Contribution to arise.

**1.6** To make available to the Owners such information as it can reasonably be expected to provide in response to a request from the Owners as to how any Contributions have been or are being spent.

**1.7** If the County Council shall pay any monies paid by the Owners under this Agreement to any third party or body (other than any employee of County Council or supplier or contractor to the County Council) it shall use all reasonable endeavours to obtain obligations (for the benefit of and enforceable by the Owners under the Contracts (Rights of Third Parties) Act 1999) from such person or body that:

   - **1.7.1** the person or body will only use or expend such money for the purposes specified in this Agreement or for which such money was paid;

   - **1.7.2** the person or body will refund any unspent money to the County Council together with any unspent interest accrued on it if it is not spent within the periods specified in paragraph 1.2 of this Schedule 18.

**EDUCATION**

**2.1** In respect of the planning obligations contained in Schedule 2 the County Council additionally covenants as follows:

   - **2.1.1** the County Council covenants with the Owners not to use the School Building Contribution received from the Owners pursuant to Schedule 2 towards any costs or fees associated with delivering a School Building that is larger or is delivered to higher specifications than that approved as part of the Project Core Team meetings as necessary to mitigate the impact of the Development;

**STATUTORY AND OTHER CONSENTS AND ORDERS REQUIRED**

The County Council will co-operate with the Highways Agency and any other statutory parties in connection with any aspect of the Development which involves the assistance consent or co-operation of the Highways Agency or other statutory parties.

**LAND**

**4.1** The County Council will use all Reasonable Endeavours to co-operate with the Owners in the grant of any rights, approvals and consents in respect of land owned by the County Council as reasonably required by the Owners to comply with the obligations in the Agreement PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT the County Council will not be required to spend any monies whether capital or revenue in so doing but will ensure that reasonable officer time is allocated from time to time.

The County Council shall use Reasonable Endeavours to respond in writing to any offer to transfer land pursuant to this Agreement within 60 Working Days.

**4.3** In the event that the County Council accepts the offer made to have transferred to it the Campus Site and the Second Primary School Land then:

   - **4.3.1** the County Council shall use Reasonable Endeavours to agree the final transfer within three months of the date of the offer having been made; and

   - **4.3.2** the County Council shall accept the transfer on the terms as set out within the relevant Schedule of this Agreement; and

   - **4.3.3** the County Council shall execute and deliver the relevant transfer to the Owners with time being of the essence.

**4.4** In respect of any land transferred to the County Council pursuant to the terms of this Agreement the County Council covenants to use the land solely for the purpose for which the land has been transferred.

**INDOOR SPORTS PROVISION**

**5** Subject to the Owners paying the Indoor Sports Building Contribution to use reasonable endeavours to construct, complete fit out and equip the Indoor Sports Building no later than 18 months after receipt of the Indoor Sports Building Contribution in accordance with the Indoor Sports Building Specification.

**TRANSPORT**

**6.1** In the event that upon the expiration of five years after the date of receipt by the County Council of all or any part of the following Contributions (together with any monies paid alongside the Contribution attributable to Indexation) there should be any unexpended balance (and/or which is not committed to be spent pursuant to a contract entered into prior to such date) that unexpended balance shall be regarded as a Clawback Contribution and the County Council shall thereafter comply with the provisions of clauses 29.4 and 29.5:

   - **6.1.1** Capital Transport Monitoring Measures Contribution; and

   - **6.1.2** Cycleway Network Design Contribution and/or the Cycleway Network Construction Contribution.

**CONSULTATION WITH THE OWNERS**

**7** Where in this Agreement there is reference to consultation or liaison with the HCA (as the HCA or as the Owners) the County Council shall undertake meaningful consultation at a time when the HCA still has the ability to influence any decision and shall consider and take into account all reasonable proposals suggestions recommendations or comments made by the HCA whether the HCA make them through one of the project core teams referred to in this Agreement or in writing served on the County Council.

**MUGA PROVISION**

**8** Subject to the SCDC having paid the sum referred to in paragraph 2 of Part 7 of Schedule 2 to use reasonable endeavours to construct or procure the construction of the MUGA no later than the opening of the Secondary School to pupils and thereafter to provide for its immediate use pursuant to the Community Access Agreement appended to this Agreement as Appendix 22.

**AS TO COMMUNITY FACILITIES**

**9** Without prejudice to paragraphs 5 and 8 above the County Council covenants with SCDC as follows with all defined terms in this paragraph 9 carrying the same definitions as in Schedule 2:

**9.1** Subject to the County Council having received the relevant part of monies due under this Schedule or under Schedule 2 the County Council will use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus Site to ensure that at the date of the opening to pupils of each relevant part of the Campus Site those parts of the Community Facilities which are appropriate to the relevant part of the Campus Site are completed such they are fit for purpose and ready and available and where the County Council having used reasonable endeavours as aforesaid has been unable to complete or procure completion by such time then the County Council shall remain under an on-going obligation to use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus Site as soon as reasonably practical thereafter Provided That if the Campus Site is operational and open prior to the trigger for the relevant part of the monies due under this Schedule or under Schedule 18 the County Council will use reasonable endeavours to complete or procure the completion of the Community Facilities on the Campus as soon as reasonably practical.

**9.2** The County Council will as soon as reasonably practical following completion of the Campus Site Transfer enter into a Community Facilities Principles Agreement.

**9.3** The County Council will procure that each relevant School Promoter which is relevant to the Campus Site will prior to taking possession of that part of the Campus Site which is appropriate to them first of all enter into a Community Facilities Principles Agreement.
